በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ፈነዎ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለሚካኤል ፡ ኅበ ፡ በለዓም ፡ መሰግል ፡ አመ ፡ ጸውዖ ፡ በላቅ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይርግሞሙ፤ ፡ ለእስራኤል ፡ ቆመ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ በፍኖት፤ ፡ ወአደንገጾ ፡ እስከ ፡ ነበበት ፤ ዕድግቱ ፡ በልሳነ ፡ ሰብእ ፣ ወሜጣ ፡ ለመርገም ፡ መን ገለ ፡ በረከት ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐሮ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ለፍቁሩ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ በዓለ ፡ አስተርእዮተ ፥ ድንግልናሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ድሜጥርዮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ። ውእቱኬ ፡ እስመ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዮልያኖስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ዘእምቅድሜሁ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ ዘየዐርፍ ፡ ባቲ! ፡ አስተርአዮ ፤ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወይ ቤሎ ፡ ናሁ ፡ አንተ ፡ ተሐውር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወዘይበውእ ፡ ኀቤከ፤፤ ፡ በሳኒታ ፡ ወምስሌሁ ፡ አስካለ ፡ ወይን ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘይቴይስ"" ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳተ ፡ እምድኅሬከ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሳኒታ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ድሜጥርዮስ"" ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዮልያኖስ ፡ ወምስሌሁ ፡ አስካለ ፡ ወይን ፡ ወአኀዞ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዮልያኖስ ፣፤ ወይቤሎ ፡ ለሕዝብ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳቲክሙ ፡ ዘእምድኅሬየ! ። ወነገሮሙ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፤ ወአኃዝዎ! ፡ ወሜምዎ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳተ ። ወኮነ ፡ አውሰበ፤ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘተሠይመ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳተ ፡ በሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ዘአውሰበ ፡ ብእሲተ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወቦአ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልበ ፡ ሕዝብ! ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ ይትናገሩ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወየሐምይዎ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ብእሲቱ ። ወአስተር አዮ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ድሜጥርዮስ፤ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ እስመ ፡ ቦእምነ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ የሐምዩከ ፡ ወይደልወከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ታእትት ፡ ኑፋቄ ፡ እምውስተ ፡ አልባቢሆሙ ፡ ወትክሥት ፡ሎሙ ፡ገድለከ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ብእሲትከ ፡ ወጸልአ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ግብረ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ኢይደልወከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ታድኅን ፡ ነፍሰከ ፡ ወይጥፍኡ ፡ ካልኣን ፡ በእንቲአከ ። አላ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኮንከ ፡ ኖላዌ ፡ ጌረ ፡ ተጋደል!" ፡ ከመ ፡ ታድኅን ፡ ሕዝበከ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ በሳኒታ ፡ ዝውእቱ ፡ አመ ፡ ፲ወ፪ለወርኃ ፡ መጋቢት ፡ ቀደሰ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ድሜጥርዮስ፤ ፡ ቅዳሴ ፡ ርባን ፡፣ ወአዘዞሙ ፡ ለሕዝብ ፤ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ኢትፃኡ ፡ እምቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ አላ ፡ ጽንሑኒ ፡ ንስቲተ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያንድዱ ፡ እሳተ ፤ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ፈነወ። ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ አንስት ፡ ወአብጽሓ ፡ ለብእሲቱ ። ወአንከሩ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ እምግባሩ ፡ እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ኢያእመሩ ፡ ምንተኑ ፡ ዘይፈቅድ ፡ ይግበር ። ወእምዝ ፡፤ ተንሥአ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወጸለየ ፡ ወአንሶሰወ ፡ ወቆመ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እሳት ፡ እንዘ ፤ ትነድድ ። ወነሥአ ፡ እሳተ ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ ወአንበራ ፡፣ ውስተ ፡ ኩፋሩ ፡ ወጸለየ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ነዊኃ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይቀውም ፡ ውስተ ፡ እሳት ፣ ወእሳት ፡ይነብር ፡-ውስተ ፡ ኩፋሩ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ጸውዓ ፡ ለብእሲቱ ፡ ወወደየ ፡ ውእተ ፡ እሳተ ፤ ውስተ ፡ ዓጽፋ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ጸለየ ፡ ነዊኃ ፣ ጊዜ ፤ ወኢያውዓየ ፡
[On this day, God sent Michael to Balaam the magician when Balaq called the latter to curse Israel. (Michael) stood before him in the way and frightened him so much that his donkey spoke in the language of men and (that Balaam) changed his curse into a blessing. May God have mercy through his prayer on his dear Athanasius in the ages of ages.] On this day also, there is the feast of the manifestation of the virginity of the holy (and) glorious Demetrius, patriarch of the city of Alexandria. This (took place) thus: When it was the night when the saint Julian, the patriarch of the city of Alexandria who (had been) before him, was to die, the angel of God appeared to the latter and said to him: "Behold, you are going to go to Christ; the one who will enter to you tomorrow, (having) with him a bunch of grapes, is the one who is most suitable to be patriarch after you." When it was the next day, this saint Demetrius entered to the saint Julian, (having) with him a bunch of grapes. The saint Julian seized him and said to the people: "Here is your patriarch who (will be) after me," and he told them how the angel of God had spoken to him about him. They seized him (Demetrius) and consecrated him patriarch. He had been married and, before this saint, no one who had married a woman had been consecrated patriarch in the city of Alexandria. Satan entered into the heart of the people and (the latter) began to speak against him (= Demetrius) and to blame him because of his wife. The angel of God appeared to the saint Demetrius and said to him: "Some among the people blame you; it is fitting that you remove the doubt from their hearts and reveal to them your way of living with your wife." (Demetrius) hated this thing. The angel said to him (again): "It is not fitting that you be saved and others perish because of you. But if you are a good shepherd, fight to save your people." When it was the next day, that is to say the twelfth of the month of Maggabit, the saint Demetrius celebrated the holy sacrifice, gave an order to the faithful, and said to them: "Do not go out of the church, but wait a little for me." Then he ordered a fire to be lit in the church, then sent to the women's quarters and had his wife brought. The faithful were astonished at his action, for they did not know what he wanted to do. Then the saint rose, prayed, walked, and stood in the fire while it was burning. He took fire in his hand, put it in his episcopal garment, and prayed again for a long time, standing in the fire and the fire being in his garment. Then he called his wife and put fire in her robe. He prayed again for a long time and (the fire) did not burn any of their clothes, the two of them. The faithful were astonished and glorified God, then they asked the holy patriarch to teach them why he had done this thing. He


[p. 344] {#po.46.p344}
ወኢምንተኒ ፡ እምአልባሰ ፡ ክልኤሆሙ ። ወአንከሩ ፡ ሕዝቡ ፡ ወሰብሕዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብ ሔር ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሰአልዎ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ከመ ፡ ያጠይቆሙ ፡ ለምንት ፣ ዘገብረ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ወነገሮሙ ፡ ገድሎ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ብእሲቱ ፡ ወዘከመ ፡ አስተዋሰቦ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፈቃዱ ፡ ወካዕበ ፡ ብእሲቱ ፡ ኢኮነት ፡ ትፈቅድ ፡ ትትዋሰብ ። ወናሁ ፡ ነበርነ ፡ ኅቡረ ፡ በበይናቲነ ፡ ሣ ወ፳ ዓመተ ፡ እምጊዜ ፡ ተዋሰብነ" ፡ ወንሕነ ፡ ንሰክብ ፡'ዲበ ፡ 8ምስካብ፤” ፡ ወንትከደን ፡ 8ክዳነ ። ወኢያአምራ ፡ በከመ ፡ የአምሩ፣ ፅደው ፡ ለአንስት ፡ አሐተ ፡ ፅለተ ፤ ግሙራ ። ወመጠነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ኮነ ፣ መልአከ ፡እግዚአብሔር ፡ይወርድ ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ለለ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ለያልይ ፡ ወይሴውረነ ፡ በክነፊሁ ። ወኢከሠትኩ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ለመኑሂ ፡ እምሰብእ ፡ በመጠነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ወኢፈቀድኩ ፡ እክሥቶ ፡ ግሙራ ። አላ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አዘዘኒ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትድኃኑ ፡ አንትሙ ፡ እምጌጋይ ። ወአንከሩ ፡ ሕዝብ፥፤ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ እምዘ ፡ ርእዩ ፤ ወሰምዑ ፡ ወሰብሕዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወሰአልዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስረይ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ስሕተቶሙ?። ፡ ወሐሜቶሙ ፡ ወሰረየ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ አበሳሆሙቶ፤ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ኢትሕ ዝኑ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ እምኔክሙ ፡ አላ ፡ እምሰይጣን ፡ ጸላኢ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ባረከ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ወፈነዎሙ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አብያቲሆሙ ፡ በሰላም ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሴብሕዎ ፡
told them his way of living with his wife and how his father had married him against his will—his wife also did not want to marry. "Behold, (he said), that we have remained together forty-eight years since the moment we were married. We sleep in one bed and are covered by one blanket, and I have never known her a single day as men know women. During these days, the angel of God descended from heaven every night and covered us with his wings. I have revealed this to no one during all these days and have never wanted to reveal it, but God himself ordered me to so that you might be saved from error." The faithful were very astonished at what they had seen and heard; they glorified God and asked him (= Demetrius) to forgive them their error and their slander. He forgave them their fault and said to them: "Do not be afflicted, for this did not come from you, but from Satan the Enemy." Then he blessed them and sent them back to their homes in peace, (they) glorifying the Holy Trinity and making known what they had seen of the miracles of this saint Demetrius. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.
"ለሥሉስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወያየድዑ ፡ በዘርእዩ ፡ እምተኣምራቲሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ድሜጥርዮስ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ መላዚ?" ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ዘእምሀገረ ፡ ፍልስጥ ኤም ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሀረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ አሜን ።
On this day also the holy Maläzi (= Malachi), who was from the land of Felset'em (= Palestine), became a martyr. May God have mercy on us through his prayer, amen.

### READING FOR THE 13TH OF MAGGABIT
አመ ፡ ፲፻ለመጋቢት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ጣሐራ ፡ ሰማዕታት ፡ በሀገረ ፡ ስብስጥያ ። እሉ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ኮኑ፤ ፣ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለእልፍያኖስ" ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እመኳንንቲሁ ፡፤ ለቄስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ንጉሥ ። ወሜሞ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ 8ብሔር ፡ እምበሐውርተ ፡ ሮሜቶ ፡ ወአዘዞ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይዕቀቦሙ ፡ ለክርስቲያን ፡ ወያክብሮሙ ፡፤ ወያፍቅሮሙ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡፤ ኀበ ፡፤ ብሔር ፡ አምለከ ፡ ጣዖታተ ፡ ወአዘዞሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያምልኩ ፡ ጣዖታተ ። ወተንሥኡ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወረገምዎ ፡ ወረገሙ ፤ ጣዖታቲሁ ። ወበይእቲ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ ተሰነአዉ ፡ ግዕደው ፡ ሐራ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይብጽሑ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወይንሥኡ ፡ አክሊለ ስ ምፅ ፡ በእዴሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኖሙ ፡ አስተርአዮሙ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በራእይ ፡ ወወሀቦሙ ፡ ኃይለ ፡'ወአጽንዓ ፤ ልቦሙ ። ወበጽባሕ ፡ ተንሥኡ ፡ ወቆሙ ፡ በቅድመ ፣ ሊቀ ፡ ሐራሁ ፡ እግሪጎላዎስ ፤ ወተአመኑ ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወተምዓ ፡ ላዕሌ ሆሙ ፡ ወአፍርሆሙ ፡ ወኢፈርሁ ፡እምኔሁ ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይውግርዎሙ ፡ በአአባን ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day, forty holy soldiers became martyrs in the city of Sebestyä (= Sebaste). These saints lived in the time of 'Elfeyanos (= Licinius) the emperor: he was one of the officers of Quastantinos (= Constantine) the emperor. The emperor appointed him head of a province among the provinces of Romë (= Rome) and ordered him to protect the Christians, to honor them, and to love them. When he arrived in the province, he worshipped idols and ordered all the people to worship idols. (The people) rose up against him, cursed him and cursed his idols, and, that night, forty soldiers agreed among themselves to go to the governor and receive the crown of martyrdom by his hand. When they fell asleep, the angel of God appeared to them in a vision, gave them strength, and fortified their hearts. In the morning, they rose, and stood before his (= 'Elfeyänos') general

ወኮኑ ፡ አእባን ፡ ይትመየጡ ፡ ላዕለ" ፡ ዘይወግሩ ፡ ቦሙ ። ወሀለወት ፡ ዐዘቅተ ፡ ማይ ፡ ቅሩብ ፤ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ወረግዐት ፡ እምአስሐቲያ ፡ ወቅሩብ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ብለኔ ። ወአዘዘ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ከመ ፡ ይውግርዎሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ዐዘቅተ ፡ አስሐትያ" ፡ ወተበትከ ፡ መልያልያቲሆሙ ፡ እምብገኃ ፡ ምር ። ወፈርሀ ፡ 8እምኔሆሙ ፡ ወወፅአ ፡ እምዐዘቅት ፡ ወቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ብለኔ ፡ ወሞተ!" ፡ ወጠፍአ ፡ ዕሜቱ!! ፡ ወኢረ ከበ ፡ ዘፈቀደ ። ወርእየ ፡ 8እምነ ፡ ሰገራት ፡ ዓ አክሊላተ ፡ ወረዱ ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ ወነበሩ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ወ፪ አክሊላት ፡ ዲበ ፡ አርእስቲሆሙ ፡ ለ ፲ወ፪ሰማዕታት ፡፣ ቅዱሳን ። ወተረፈ ፡ 8አክሊል ፡ ስቁል ፡ ምስለ፤፤ ፡ መልአክ ። ወተንሥአ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ 8እምነ ፡ ሐራ፣ እለ ፡ ነበሩ፤ ፡ በህየ ፡ ወአውፅአ ፡ አልባሲሁ ፡ ወወረደ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ዐዘቅት ፡ ምስለ ፡ ቅዱሳን ። ወአዕረፈ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ ውእተ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ስቁለ ፡ ምስለ" ፡ መልአክ፤፤ ። ወኮኑ! ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ወራዙት ፡ ወኮና ፡ እማቲሆሙ!፤ ፡ 'ይጹንዓሆሙ ፡ ወያስተዔግሣሆሙ፤፤ ። ወሶበ ፡ ነበሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ዓዘቅት ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ጊዜ ፤ ወኢሞቱ ፡ ወፈቀዱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስብሩ ፡ አዮያጺሆሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሙቱ ፡ ፍጡነ ። ወነ ሥአ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ነፍሳቲሆሙ ፡ ወኢተክህሎሙ ፡ ለእሙንቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስብሩ ፡ አዕጽምቲሆሙ!፤ ።
'Egrigoläwos (= Agricola) and confessed our Lord Christ. (The general) became angry with them and (wanted) to frighten them, but they were not afraid of him. He ordered them to be stoned; but the stones returned upon those who threw them. There was a pool of water near them, (it was) frozen with ice, and near it (there was) a bath. The governor ordered them to be thrown into the frozen pool, and their limbs were broken by the severity of the cold. One of them was afraid, came out of the pool, entered the bath, and died; his reward was lost and he did not find what he wanted. One of the guards saw forty crowns: they were descending from heaven and thirty-nine of them rested on the heads of the thirty-nine holy martyrs. One crown remained suspended with an angel. One of the soldiers who were there then rose, took off his clothes, and went down into the pool with the saints. He died and received the crown that was suspended with the angel. There were adolescents among them, and their mothers fortified them and exhorted them to constancy. As they had remained a long time in the pool and had not died, they wanted to break their legs so that they would die quickly. God took their souls and they (= the guards) could not break their bones.

Then, the governor ordered their bodies to be burned with fire, to be transported on a chariot, and to be thrown into the sea. As they were being transported on the chariot, they found among them an adolescent who had not died and he was not carried on the chariot. His mother carried him and (wanted) to throw him on the chariot with the martyrs. The guards prevented her (from doing so) because he was alive, but he died on her (= his mother's) neck and she threw him on the chariot with the holy martyrs. They were taken out near the city of Sebestyä, thrown into the fire, burned, and then thrown into the sea. On the third day, the holy martyrs appeared to the bishop of the city of Sebestyä and said to him: "Go near the river, you will find our bodies, take them." The bishop rose, with priests with him; they went out by night, went near the river, and found the bodies of the holy martyrs gathered in one place like shining stars. Nothing of them had been destroyed. They were taken and placed in a beautiful place. Their story spread to all the ends of the universe and their commemoration was celebrated. Churches were built for them in all the ends of the universe. Many miracles and wonders occurred through them. May their prayer and their blessing be with us, amen.
እምኔሆሙ ፡ ተኣምራት ፡ ወመንክራት ፡ ብዙኃት ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ያብዕሉ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ አርቶዶክሳዊያን ፡ ተዝካረ ፡ ስደቶሙ ፤ ለአብ ፡ መቃርዮስ ፡ ዘየዐቢ ፡ ወለመቃርዮስ ፡ ዘእስክንድርያ ። ወሶበ ፡ ነግሠ ፡
[On this day also, the people of the right faith celebrate the commemoration of the exile of Father Magäryos (= Macarius) the Great and of Magäryos (= Macarius)

ወዓሊጢኖስ ፡ መንግሥተ ፡ ሮም ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ዋልስ ፡ እሁ ፡ አምጽአ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡ ወስደተ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ምእመናን ፡ አርቶዶክሳዊያን ፡ እስመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ይትአመን ፡ በሃይማኖተ ፡ ማኒ ፡ ርኩስ ፡ ወበሃይማኖተ ፡ አርዮስ ፡ ዓላዊ ። ወሜመ?' ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት 7፣ በእስክንድርያ ፡ ሉክዮስሃ ፡ አርዮሳዌ ፡ ወሰደዶ ፡ ለአትናቴዎስ ፡ ሐዋርያዊ ። ወዝን ቱሰ ፡ ሉክዮስ፡ ፡ ኃሠሦሙ ፡ ለአብ ፡ መቃርዮስ" ፡ ዘየዐቢ ፡ ወለአብ ፡ መቃርዮስ፤ ፡ እስክንድራዊ ፡ በትእዛዘ ፡ ዋልጥስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወአብጽሖሙ ፡ እምገዳም ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ አስፈጦሙ ፡ ወቴጦሙ ፡ በነገር ፡ ልምሉም ፡ ወአገበሮሙ ፡ ይባኡ ፡ ምስሌሁ፣ ውስተ ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ አርዮስ ፤ ወኢገብኡ ፡ ለቃሉ ፡ ወኢፈርህዎ ፡ አላ ፣ ገሠጽዎ ፡ ወተዋሥፅዎ ፡ ምስለ ፡ የውሃት ፡ ወትዕግሥት" ፡ ወዐበየኑ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ እመጻሕፍት ፡ ቅዱሳት ፡ ሙስናሁ ፡ ለሃይማኖተ ፡ አርዮስ ፡ ወእከየ ፡ ባህሉ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ሰደዶሙ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ደሴት ፡ርኅቅት ፡ ዘእማእከለ ፡ ሮምያ ፡ ወቀኦስጥንጥንያ ፡ ወአስተዳለዉ ፡ ሐመረ ፡እለ፣ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወሐራሂ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ዓቃብያነ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ሙቁሓኒሆሙ ፡ በሐናን ፡ ወበኩሉ ፡ ግብር ፡ ጥውቅ ፡ ዘየአኪ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ደሴት ፡ "አቀምዎሙ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ደሴትቶ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ እኩየ ፡ ወመምለኬ ፡ ለሰይጣን ። ወአስሐተ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ደሴትኒ ፡ በሥራዩ ፡ እስከ ፡ አምለክዎ ፡ ወረሰይዎ ፡ አምላከ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ ይሰግዱ ፡ ሎቱ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእዮሙ ፡ ለአበው ፡ አገበሮሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስግዱ ፡ ሎቱ ። ወውእቶሙሰ ፡ ኢተአዘዝዎ ፡ ሎቱ?" ፡ ሰአልዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትመየጥ ፡ እምክሕደቱቶ፥ ፡ ወኢተመይጠ ፡ ለመብህሎሙ ፡ ወባሕቱ ፡ ዘግሖሙቶ፥ ፡ ወሞቅሖሙ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሥቃያት ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ወፅፁብ፥የ ፡ ወአመንደቦሙ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ያስተርኢ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ወያጸንዖሙ ፡ ወያስተዔግሦሙ ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ አስተርእዮተ ፡ ትሩፋቲሆሙ ፡ አኀዛ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ለወለተ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወሣቀያ ፡ ሥቃየ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ እስከ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ወእማ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኀዘን ፡ ብዙኅ ። ወውስተ ፡ ሀገርኒ፤ ፡ ሰይጣናት ፡ ያስተርእዩ ፡ ገሃደ ፣፡ ይጸርሑ ፡ ወይብሉ ፡ ምንት ፡ ለነ ፡ ወለክሙ ፣ ኦመቃርስ ፡ ወቢጹ ፡ ዘሰደድዎሙ ፡ እምብሔሮሙ ። ወሰይጣንኒ ፡ ዘሀሎ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ወለተ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ጸርሐ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኢበጽሑ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ ወቢጹ ፡ ኢይወፅእ ፡ እምወለትከ ፡፤ ኦንጉሥ ። ወሰሚዖሙ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወመኳንንቲሁ ፡ ወጉቡኣን ፡ እለ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ አንከሩ ፡ ወአብጽሕዎሙ ፡ ለመቃርዮስ ፡ ወቢጹ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ንጉሥ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ሰሰለ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ርኩስ ፡ ዘኅዱር ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ወለተ ፡ ንጉሥ፤ ፡ ወሐተቶሙ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ግብሮሙ ፡ ወነገርዎ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘኮነ ። ወአስተብቀዖሙ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ወለቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፈውስዋ ። ወገሠጽዎ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ አንተ ፡ ለምንት ፣ ትሬሲ ፡ርእሰከ ፡ አምላከ ፡ እመሰ ፡ አንተ፥ ፡ ፈጠርከ ፡ ሰማየ ፡ ወምድረ ፡ ይደልወከኬ ፡ ከመ ፡ ታድኅን ፡ ወለተከ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ጸለዩ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወአውዕፅኡ ፡ ጋኔነ ፡ እምወለቱ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ወወዩኦ ፡ ጋኔን ፡ 'እምወለቱ ፡ ለንጉሥ፥ ፡
of Alexandria. When Wa'älitinos (= Valentinian) reigned over the empire of Rom (= Rome) after Wäles (= Valens) his brother, he brought misfortune and persecution upon the believers of the right faith, for he believed in the faith of Mäni (= Mani) the impure and in the faith of 'Aryos (= Arius) the heretic. He appointed Lukyos (= Lucius) the Arian as patriarch of Alexandria and drove out 'Atnätëwos (= Athanasius) the Apostolic. This Lukyos sought Father Magäryos the Great and Father Magäryos the Alexandrian on the order of Wältes (= Valentinian) the emperor and brought them from the desert. When they arrived before him, he made promises to them, flattered them with honeyed words, and (wanted) to force them to enter with him into the faith of 'Aryos. They did not yield to his word nor were they afraid of him, but they rebuked him, contradicted him with gentleness and patience, and demonstrated to him from the Holy Scriptures the depravity of the faith of 'Aryos and the wickedness of his discourse. He then drove them to a remote island which (was) between Romyä (= Rome) and Questentenyä (= Constantinople). A ship was prepared from Alexandria, (there were) also soldiers with them guarding them while they were shackled with iron (chains) [and tormented by all the bad treatment (that was) inflicted upon them]. When they arrived at the island, they were brought before the king of the island. He was a wicked man, a worshipper of Satan. He had deceived the people of the island by his magic so that they worshipped him, had made him a god, and prostrated themselves before him. When he saw the fathers, he (wanted) to force them to prostrate themselves before him. But they did not obey him (and) asked him to turn away from his impiety. He did not yield to their words; on the contrary, he arrested them, imprisoned them with numerous and painful torments, and subjected them to great tortures. The Lord appeared to them, fortified them, and made them constant. When the Lord wanted to manifest their virtues, Satan took possession of the king's daughter and subjected her to numerous torments so that her father and mother were in great sorrow. In the city also, demons appeared openly; they cried out and said: "What is there in common between us and you, Magäres and your companion, (you) who have been driven from your country?" As for the demon that was in the king's daughter, it cried out saying: "If Magäres and his companion do not come (here), I will not leave your daughter, O king." Hearing (this), the king, his officers, and the group that was with him were astonished, and Magäryos and his companion were brought before the king. When they arrived before him, the impure spirit that dwelt in the king's daughter disappeared. (The king) questioned them about their way of life and they told him everything that had happened. He begged them to heal his daughter. They rebuked him and said to him: "While you are a man, why have you made yourself a god?" If you had created the


[p. 350] {#po.46.p350}
ከልሐ ፡ ወተዛለፎ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ወለሰብአ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ደሴት ፡ በእንተ ፡ ምግባራቲሆሙ ፡ እኪት ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወጉቡኣን ፡ ጸር ፡ ወይቤሉ ፡ ነአምን ፣ በአምላከ ፡ እሉ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ እስመ ፡ ጠየቅነ ፡ ከመ ፡ አልቦ ፡ አምላክ ፡ በሰማይ ፡ ወበምድር ፡ ዘእንበለ ፤ አምላኮሙ ።
heaven and the earth, it would certainly have been up to you to save your daughter." They then prayed to our Lord Jesus Christ and made the demon leave the king's daughter. Having left the king's daughter, the demon cried out and rebuked the king and the people of the island because of their evil deeds. When the king and the gathered (people) saw this, they cried out and said: "We believe in the God of these saints, for we are certain that there is no god in heaven and on earth apart from their God."
ወአበውኒ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፣፤ መሐርዎሙ ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ወአስተጋብዕዎሙ ፡ ለሕሙማን ፡ ወድውያን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፳መካን ፡ ወጸለዩ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ዘይት ፤ ወአተቡ ፡፤ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ በትእምርተ ፡ መስቀል ፤ ወአሕየውዎሙ ። ወለብዙኃን ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እለ ፡ አኃዝዎሙ ፡ አጋንንት ፡ ፈወሱ ፡ ወአሕየዉ ፡ ወነበሩ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይገ ብሩ ፤ ከመዝ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ነሰቱ ፡ ምኩራበ ፡ ሀገር ፤ ወአስተናጽሑ ፡ ወሐነጹ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ መካኑ ፡ ወርቲብ ፡ ጥቀ ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ያምጽኡ ፡ ወርቀ ፡ ወብሩረ ፡ ዘአልቦ ፡ ጐልቀነ ፡ ወከማሁ ፡ አልባሳተ ፡ ሐሪር ፡ ወኩሎ ፡ ንዋያተ ፡ ዘይትፈቀድ ፡ ለመቅደሰ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወአዘዝዎሙ ፡ አበዊነ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ 'ለጠበብት ፡ ወለነሀብት" ፡ ከመ ፡ ይግበሩ ፡ ፲ ወ፪አጽሕልተ ፡ዘመድሎቱ፣፤ ለለ ፤ ፳8-ልጥረ ፡፤ ወርቅ ፣፡ ወይሥዐሉ ፡ 'ውስቴቶሙ ፡ ሥዕለ፤ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወሥዕለ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡፤ ማርያም ፡ ድንግል ፤ ወ፪መላእክት ፡ ዐበይት ፣ ሚካኤል ፡፤ ወገብርኤል ፡ ወይግበሩ ፡ ፲ወ፪መናብርተ ፡ ዘይትፈቀድ ፡ ለጽዋዓት ፡ ወዓሠርተ ፡ ወክልኤተ ፡ ጽዋዓተ ፡ ፲ ወ፪መንካተ ፡ ፳ወ9መስቀለ ፡ ለለኩሉ ፡ 8በበ። ፡ ፻ ልጥራት ፤ ወአዘዙ ፡ከመ ፡ ይሥዓሉ ፡፻ ቤተ ፡ መቅደስ ። ወተፈጸመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ሕንጻ ፡ መርጡል ፤ ወንዋያቲሃ ፤ ወዘተርፈ ፡ ንዋይ ፡ ዘረዉ ፡ ለኅጡኣን ፣ ወለመፍቅዳን ።
The holy fathers taught them the faith of Christ; they gathered the suffering and the sick in one place, prayed over oil, made the sign of the cross over them, and healed them. They treated and healed many people whom demons had possessed. They remained for many days acting in this way. After that, they overthrew the temple of the country, proceeded to a purification, and built a church there (= on its site), for the place (was) beautiful and very vast. The king ordered gold and silver (in incalculable quantity) to be brought, and likewise silk garments and all the objects that are necessary for the sanctuary of God. Our holy fathers ordered skilled (workers) and blacksmiths to make twelve basins, each weighing nine pounds of gold, and to engrave on them the image of our Lord Christ and the image of our Lady Mary the Virgin and of the two great angels Mikä'ël (= Michael) and Gabr'ël (= Gabriel). (They also ordered) to make twelve supports that (were) necessary for the cups, twelve cups, twelve pedestals (and) twenty-four crosses, each of three pounds. They (also) ordered three altars (?) to be engraved. All the elaboration of the holy place and its utensils was completed. As for the silver that remained, they distributed (it) to the indigent and the needy.
ወሶበ ፡ኮነ ፡ አመ ፡፲ ወ ፳ለጥር ፡ በፅለት ፡ ዘተጠምቀ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ አዘዝዎሙ ፡ ለሰብአ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይረዱ ፡፤ ውስተ ፡ ሐይቀ ፡ ባሕር ፤ ወይትጋብኡ ፤ ሀየ ፤ ወአምጽኡቶ፥ ፡ ሜሮነ ፡ ቅዱሰ ፡ ዘተማልኡ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ስደቶሙ ፡ እምእስክንድርያ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ዕቁበ ፡ እመዋዕለ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ሐዋርያዊ ፣፤ ወሉክዮስ ፡ አርዮሳዊ ፡ ኢረከቦ ፡ ወበዛቲኒ ፡ ኮነ ፡ የዓቅቦ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኩሎ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ዘነበሩ ፡ ሀየ ፡ ወኢረከቦ ፡ ሙስና ። እስመ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ኮነ ፡ በትእዛዘ ፣ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ውእተ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ዓተበ ፡ አብ ፡ መቃርዮስ ፡ በሜሮን ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ቀላይ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ከመ ፡ ማይ ፡ ዘይፈልሕ ፡ እምዋዕየ ፡ እሳት ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ መቅጹት ። ወጸለለ ፡ ዘይት ፡ ወሜሮን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገጸ ፡ ማይ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘከዋክብት ፡ ብሩሃን ፡ ወአስተርአየ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ከመ ፡ ብርሃነ ፡ ፀሐይ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፣ ንጉሥ ፡ ወጉባኤ ፡ ፈርሁ ፡ ወአስተርአየ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ ወዮሐንስ ፣፤ መጥ ምቅ ፡ ወመላእክቲሁ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቅዳሴ ፡ ማይ ። ወተጠምቁ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ነወ ፡ ፄና ፡ ሠናይ ፡ መዓዛ" ፡ ዘይምዕዝ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ዕፍረት ። ወርእዮሙ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ወድቁ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወጐንደዩቶ ፡ ስጉዳኒሆሙ ። ወሰአሎ ፡ አብ ፡ መቃርዮስ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ስጉድ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወይቤ ፡ እግዚእየ ፡ እስእለከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትርከበኒ ፡ ምሕረትከ ፡
When it was the eleventh of Terr, the day on which our Lord Christ was baptized, they ordered the people of the country to go down to the seashore and gather there. They brought the holy oil that they had taken with them at the time of their expulsion from Alexandria (and) which had been preserved since the time of 'Atnätëwos the Apostolic - Lukyos the Arian had not found it. In this (island) also, God had kept it during all the time that (the saints) had remained there and corruption had not touched it: this had happened on the order of the Holy Spirit. At the moment when Father Magäryos made the sign of the cross with the holy oil over the open sea, the sea became like water that is in a cauldron, boiling under the heat of the fire. The holy oil covered the surface of the water and became like shining stars. A light appeared in it like the light of the sun. When the king and the crowd saw (this), they were afraid. Our Lord Christ appeared (coming) from heaven, as well as Yohannes (= John) the Baptist and the holy angels, for the consecration of the water. The people were baptized in the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit. Immediately a sweet fragrance was exhaled, a suave odor, more suave

than all perfumes. Seeing this, the saints fell to the ground and remained prostrate. Father Magäryos, being prostrate on the ground, addressed a request to our Lord and said: "My Lord, I ask you (to make) your mercy touch me and that this altar as well as the vessels be consecrated by your divine hand." The Lord said to him: "Yes." He took the holy oil (from the hands) of the holy father, placed it in his right hand, and consecrated the altar as well as its vessels. He said to Father Magäryos: "I order you to consecrate three porticos, I (will be) with you; your companion Magärä will perform this holy office with you. Begin with the middle portico (which will be) in the name of Mary the Virgin my mother, the one on the right (will be) in the name of the holy Cross, and the one on the left, in your name, for you have shed your blood for my name, by the hand of Lukyos the heretic, in this island." When the blessed ones began to act as the Lord had ordered them, Däwit (= David) began to chant and the angels to say the Sanctus

in the language of the people of this island. Hearing the beautiful words, the people were astonished and glorified God. Our Lord Christ further ordered the saint Magäryos to bring the offering to the altar and to perform the consecration. Magäryos said to him: "O my Lord, order Pêtros (= Peter), the chief of the apostles, to perform the consecration today, for this day (is) the first on which the offering is brought for your holy name in this island." Our Lord said to him: "It is to you that (this) belongs; it is you who have sown in this island, I, I have made (the grain) grow. Have you not heard what I said by the mouth of Päwlos (= Paul) my disciple: Let him who sows and labors eat the fruit first." When Father Magäryos began to perform the consecration, our holy fathers stood with him, Pêtros at his right, Märqos (= Mark) at his left, and all the apostles around them to assist him; Magäryos the Alexandrian (served as) deacon. Päwlos read an epistle of Päwlos, Yohannes (= John) read the Catholic Epistles, Lugäs (= Luke) the Acts of the Apostles, Däwit read the Psalms, and our God and our Savior - adoration returns to his mention - read the Gospel. The Holy Spirit, in the form of a white dove, remained on the bread and wine and made them the flesh and blood of our Lord Christ. When the saint put his fingers on the chalice, his fingers took the color of blood. All the hosts of the cherubim spread their wings above the altar. Mikä'ël, Gabr'ël, and all the apostles came to assist him. They took the appearance of Magäryos the father (and) of Magäryos the Alexandrian and administered the holy Mystery to the people. After the end of the Eucharist, our Lord Christ took the appearance of Magäryos the father and sat on the throne from which the Gospel was read. His face shone like the sun, he gave the blessing to the people, and returned to the altar, the disciples with him.

Furthermore, after seven days, our father Magäryos prayed to God by recalling the institution of the hierarchy of the Church. Our Lord appeared to him and said to him: "For me, it is you who (are) worthy of this great glory." Hearing (this), the old man prostrated himself before the Lord, shed bitter tears, and said: "Remember, Lord, what you said to me: I will bring you back to your children, the monks of the mountain of Sihat (= Scetis), and you will see them before leaving this body. Otherwise, they will say that Magärä has abandoned the monastic state and has become patriarch." The Lord said to him: "My chosen one Magäryos, I will accomplish for you all the desire of your heart. Bring one of your (spiritual) sons, whom you will have chosen, to consecrate him patriarch." The saint said to him: "Lord, you know the hearts, look then at (this) elderly man, a blacksmith: he is firm in the holy faith and he has not known union with a woman." Our Lord said to him: "Let it be as you have said; in truth, he is virtuous." The saint called this blacksmith and brought him before the altar. The saint Märqos the Evangelist came, put his hand on the blacksmith, and ordained him deacon; then he ordained him priest and, after that, (made him climb all) the degrees until he had accomplished the patriarchal (consecration). He called him by the name of Yohannes (= John). All the hosts of the heavenly beings cried out three times saying: "Worthy (is) Yohannes the patriarch, son of Magäryos, new plant of Märqos the Evangelist!" The king and all the people heard these words. Our Lord opened the eyes of the heart of Yohannes the patriarch and (the latter) saw our Lord Christ as much as it was possible for him to see him. He spoke many words and saw the (heavenly) sanctuary full of the heavenly hosts, the prophets, and the holy apostles.


[p. 354] {#po.46.p354}
ቅዱሳን ። ወጸውዖሙ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ለአባ ፡ መቃራ ፡ ዘየዐቢ ፡ ወለአባ ፡ መቃራ፣ እስክንድርያዊ ፤ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ብፁዓን ፡ አንትሙ ፡ ወብፁዓን ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እለ ፡ ይዜክሩ ክሙ ፡ በዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወይወርሱ ፡ ሕይወተ ፡ ዘለዓለም ። እምሕል ፡ በርእስየ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘያስተሐምም ፡ በእንተ ፡ ጸማክሙ ፡ ዘጾርክምዋ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስምየ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ደሴት ፡ ይጽሕፎ ፡ ወያነብቦ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያናት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሰብሐኒ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘሰምዓ ፡ እሁቦሙ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ሠናያተ ፤ ወኢያኃጥኦሙ" ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ ወኣድኃኖሙ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ምንዳቤሆሙ ፡ ወእሰሪ ፡ ሎሙ?" ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ኃጢአቶሙ ፡ ወበጊዜ ፡፤ ፍልሰ ቶሙ ፡ እፌኑ ፡ መላእክተ ፡ ምሕረት ፡፣ ይንሥኡ?" ፡ ነፍሳቲሆሙ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ትፍሥሕት ፡ ዘለዓለም ፡ ወኀበ ፡ መንግሥትየ ፡ ዘትሔልዉ ፡ አንትሙ ። ወኩሉ ፡ ምኔት ፡ አው ፡ ሀገር ፡ አው ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ አው ፡ ቤተ ፤ አርቶዶክሳዊ ፡ ዘይትነበብ ፤ ውስቴቱ ፤ ዝንቱ ፡ ዜና ፡ አነ ፡ አኃድር”” ፡ ህየ ፡ ወእሬሲ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ በረከትየ ፡ ወሰላምየ ፡ እስከ ፡ ኅልቀተ ፡ ዓለም ። ወኩሉ ፡ ዘይከውን ፣፤ መስተቃርነ ፡ ለዜና ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ስደት ፡ እም ጌሶ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ኢተወልደ ፡ በዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወለእመሰ ፡ ነስሑ ፡ በአሚን ፡ እሰሪ ፡ ሎሙ። አማን ፡ እብለክሙ ፡ በከመ ፡ አመንክሙኒ ፡ ኪያየ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ አነሂ ፡
Our Lord Christ called abbä Magärä the Great and abbä Magärä the Alexandrian. He said to them: "Blessed are you and blessed are the people who will mention you on earth: they will inherit eternal life! I swear by myself that whoever will have shared the pains that you have endured for my name in this island, will have written them, and will have read them in the churches - to glorify me - (and) whoever will have heard (them), to (all) those I will give every good thing and will not let them lack anything. I will save them from all their misfortunes, I will forgive them all their sins, and, at the moment of their death, I will send the angels of mercy to carry their souls into eternal joy and into my kingdom (in) which you, you will be. In every monastery, city, church, or house (of a man) of the right faith in which this story will be read, I, I will dwell, and I will place my blessing and my peace there until the end of the world. It would be better for anyone who would contradict the story of this exile not to have been born on earth. If he repents with faith, I will forgive him. In truth, I say to you: just as you have declared yourselves for me before men, I too, I will declare myself for you before


[p. 356] {#po.46.p356}
አአምነክሙ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ አቡየ ፡ ዘበሰማያት ፣ ወመላእክቲሁ ፡ ቅዱሳን ። ወካዕበ ፣ ይቤሎሙ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ጊዜሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትትመየጡ ፡፤ ውስተ ፡ ሀገርክሙ ፡ ወወሀቦሙ ፡ ሰላመ ፡ ወዓርገ ፤ ውስተ ፡ ሰማያት! ።
my Father who (is) in heaven and (before) his holy angels." He said to them again: "The moment has come for you to return to your country," and he gave them peace and ascended into heaven.
ወሶቤሃ ፡ አስተጋብእዎሙ ፡ አበው ፡ ክቡራን ፡ ለኩሉ ፡፤ ሕዝብ ፡፤ ወለንጉሥኒ ፡ ወአይድዕዎሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትመየጡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ብሔሮሙ ፡ ወአዘዝዎሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይዕ ቀቡ ፤ ኩሎ ፡፤ ሕገ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወከመ ፡ ያክብርዎ ፡ ለሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ወአማኅፀንዎሙ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ከመ ፣ ኢይትገኃሠ ፡ ኢለየማን ፡ ወኢለፀጋም ፤ እምሃይማ ኖት ፤ ርትዕት ። ወሰሚዖሙ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ደንገፁ ፡ ጥቀ ፡ ወበከዩ ፡ ብካየ ፡ መሪረ ። ወቅዱሳንኒ ፡ በከዩ ፤ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ኅቡረ ፤ ወተሰነዓልዎሙ ፡ በሰላም ፤ ወባረክዎሙ ፡ ለለ8፳ ። ወሶቤሃ ፣፡ መጽአ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ኪሩባዊ ፡ ወአፅረጎሙ ፡ ወጾሮሙ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ክነ ፊሁ ፤ ወአብጽሖሙ ፡። በአሐቲ ፡ ፅለት ፡ ወአውረዶሙ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እለ ፡ እስክንድ ርያ ፡፤ ቅድመ ፡ አንቀጸ ፡ ምኔተ" ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ አብ ፡ ጢሞቴዎስ ፡ ጽባሐ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ እሑድ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ አብ ፡ ጢሞቴዎስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዝክተ ፤፡ ተፈሥሐ ፤ ዐቢየ ፡ ፍሥሐ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእዮሙ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ በከየ፤ ፡ ብካየ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ ወሰገደ ፡ ለአበው ፡ ውእቶሙኒ ፡ ሰገዱ ፡ ወተአም ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ ወተናገሩኒ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ። ወዜነውዎሙ ፡ በእንተ ፤ ተመይጦተ ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ ነኪራን ፡ ኀበ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡፤ ወመንክራተ ፡ እንተ ፡ ኮነት ። ወአንከረ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ወኩሎሙ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ እለ ፡
Then the illustrious fathers gathered all the people as well as the king; they informed them that they were returning to their country and ordered them to observe all the law of Christ and to honor the patriarch Yohannes. They entrusted them with many (directives) so that they would not deviate from the right faith, neither to the right nor to the left. Hearing (this), the people were very upset and shed bitter tears. The saints wept with them together, bade them farewell in peace, and blessed them one by one. A cherubic angel then came, lifted them up, carried them on his wings, transported them in a single day, and made them descend near the city of Alexandria before the gate of the monastery of the patriarch, Father Timotëwos (= Timothy), on the morning of Sunday. When Father Timotëwos the patriarch learned this, he experienced great joy. When he saw them again, he shed great tears and prostrated himself before the fathers, they too prostrated themselves; they embraced and (the fathers) recounted what had happened to them and gave them (= the assistants) an account of the conversion to God of (these) foreign peoples and (of) the miracle that had occurred. The patriarch was astonished and all the people who had listened were astonished and glorified God.
ሰምዑ ፡ አንከሩ ፡ ወሰብሕዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወነበሩ ፡ ህየ ፡ ሰሙነ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ወአስተርአዮሙ ፡ ዳግመ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ኪሩባዊ ። ወጾሮሙ፤ ፡ በክነፊሁ ፡ ወአብጽሖሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ሲሐት" ። ወጸርሐ ፡ 8ኪ ሩብ ፡ በውስተ ፡ አየር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ኦውሉድዶ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርዮስ ፡ ናሁ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ አቡክሙ ፡ እምስደት ፡ ጸዊሮ ፡ ፍሬያተ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፤ ወሰሚዖሙ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ወፅኡ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ እምበዓታቲሆሙ‹ ። ወኮነ ፡ ጐልቆሙ ፡ ፻፳፤ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ወእምኔሆሙ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ሐቂር ፡ ወአባ ፡ ብሶይ ። ወአምጉ“ ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ወእገሪሁ ፡ ወበከዩ። ፡ ብካየ ፡ መሪረ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ዜነዎሙ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘበጽሐ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወካዕበ ፡ ተፈሥሐ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘርእዩ ፤ ገጾ ። ወውእቱኒ ፡ ተፈሥሐ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ወባረኮሙ ፣ በስሙ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ።
They remained there eight days and the cherubic angel appeared to them again, he carried them on his wings and brought them to the mountain of Sihat. A cherub cried out in the air saying: "Sons of abbä Magaryos, behold your father has arrived from exile, bearing the fruits of the Holy Spirit." Hearing this, all went out of their cells. Their number was one million monks - among them (there were) abbä Yohannes (= John) the Dwarf and abbä Bsoy -, they kissed his hands and his feet and wept bitter tears when he told them everything that had happened to him. They rejoiced again because they saw his face. He too rejoiced with them and blessed them in the name of our Lord Jesus Christ.
ወበእንተዝ ፡ ይደልወነ ፡ ኦምእመናነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ሮምያ ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ አህጉር ፡ ወበሐውርት ፡ከመ ፡ ትትፈሥሑ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ዕለት ። ወንኤዝዘክሙ ፡ ትረስይዎ ፡ በዐለ ፡ ለለ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዓም ፡ ተዝካረ ፡ ለጻማ ፡ እሉ ፣ አበው ፡ መስተጋድላን ፡ ከመ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ወጳውሎስ ፡ ወከዐዉ ፡ ደሞሙ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስመ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ አምላክነ ፡ ወውእቶሙ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ ዘየዐቢ ፡ ወእሁ ፡ አብ ፡፤ መንፈሳዊ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ እስክንድርያዊ ። ወውእቱ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አመ ፡ ፲ ወይለመጋቢት ፡ ዘሰደዶሙ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ሉክዮስ ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ እለ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደሴት ፡ እንተ ፡ ቀደመ ፡ ዘክሮተ ። ወበከመ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡፤ ገብኡ ፤ ወበጽሑ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡፣ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፤ ፍቁሮሙ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፤ ወለፍቅርቶሙ ፡፣ ኮቶሊካዊት ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
Because of this, it is fitting for us, that (you), the faithful of Christ - people of Romyä (= Rome) and of all the cities and countries -, should rejoice on this day. We prescribe that you make it a feast every year in commemoration of the pains of these fathers, (spiritual) combatants like Pêtros and Päwlos, (who) shed their blood for the name of Christ our God: abbä Magäres the Great and his brother, the spiritual father Magäres the Alexandrian. It is the day - the thirteenth of maggäbit - when Lukyos exiled them from the city of Alexandria, to the island which was previously mentioned; and the same (day) as this day again, they returned and arrived at the city of Alexandria. May their prayer and their blessing be with their dear 'Atnätëwos and their dear Kotolikäwit in the centuries of centuries, amen.]

ወበዛቲ ፡ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ዲዮናስዮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወውእቱ ፤ እምጐልቆሙ ፡ ለአበው ፡ ሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ፲ወ8 ። ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ አበዊሁ ፡፤ አረማዊያነ ፡ 'ወያመልኩ ፡ ፀሐየ ። ወኮነ ፡ ተምህረ ፡ ትምህርታተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ፡ እስመ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምዘመድ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ወሐለየ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ እምንእሱ ፡ ወመሀሮ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ጥበበ ፡፣ ወትምህርተ ፡ እስከ ፡ ኮነ ፡ መም ሀረ ፤ ለጠቢባን ፣ አረማዊያን ። ወእንዘ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይነብር ፡ በአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ወናሁ ፤ አሐቲ ፡ አረጋዊት ፡ ክርስቲያናዊት ፡ ኀለፈት ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ ወምስሌሃ ፡ 8ጥራዝ” ፡ እመጽሐፈ ፡፤ መልእክቱ ፡ ለጳውሎስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ። ወትቤሎ ፡ 'ኀበኒ ፡ ምንተኒ ፡ ወነ ሥኣ!፤‹0 ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ጥራዝ ። ወሶበ ፡ አንበበ ፡ ረከበ ፡ በውስቴታ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ነኪረ ፡ ወአእ ምሮ ፡ መድምመ ። 'ወኅሠሠት ፡ እምኔሁ ፡፤ ሜጫጣ ፡ ፳!"፤! ድርቲመ ፡ ወርቅ ፡፤ ወወሀባ ፡ ፻!"። ድርቲመ ፡ ወርቅ ፡፤ ወይቤላ ፤ ሖሪ ፤ ወሕትቲ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትርከቢ ፡ ዘተረፈ ፡ እምዝ ንቱ ፡ መጽሐፍ ፤ ወአምጽኢ ፡ ሊተ ። ወሖረት ፡ ወአምጽአት ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ፻ጥራዛተ ፡ ካል ኣነ ፡ ወወሀባ ፡ 8ድርቲመ ፡ ወርቅ ። ወሶበ ፡ አንበቦሙ ፡ ረከበ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ሕጹጸ ። ወይቤላ ፡ ሖሪ ፤ ወኅሥሚ ፡ ዘተረፈ ፤ እምዝንቱ ፣፤ መጽሐፍ ። ወትቤሎ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ረከ ብኩ ፡ እሎንተ ፡ በውስተ ፡ መጻሕፍተ!" ፡ አበዊየ ፡ ወለእመ ፡ ፈቀድከ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ፍጹመ ፡ ኅሥሣ ፡ እምሰብአ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወይቤላ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኀሠሥክዎኑ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ይሁቡኒሁ! ፡ ኪያሁ ፤ ወትቤሎ ፡ እወ ። ወሖረ ፣ ወኀሠሠ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ እም ፳እምካህናት ፡ ወወሀቦ፤ ፡ ኪያሁ ። ወአንበቦ ፡ ወአጽንዖ ፡ በልቡ ፡ ወኮነ፤ ፡ ያነብቦ ፡ ገሃደ ፡ ዘእንበለ፤ ፡ መጽሐፍ ፡ ወአምነ ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ድሜጥርዮስ፤" ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡፤ ወሰአሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያጥምቆ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ፡ ወመሀሮ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘፈቀደ ፤ ወአጥመቆ ፡ "ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና፤ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ፍጹመ ፡ በአእምሮ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወረሰዮ ፡ አባ ፡ ድሜጥርዮስ፤፤ ፡ መምህረ ፡ ለውሉደ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወሶበ ፡ ተሠይመ ፡ አባ ፡ ያሮክላ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳተ ፡ ረሰዮ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ህየንቴሁ ፣ በውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይግበር ፡ ፍትሐ ፡ ማእከለ ፡ መሃይምናን፤፤፤ ። ወአብሖ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጵጵስና ፡ ከመ ፡ ይመግባ ። ወሶበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ያሮክላ ፡ ተሰናአዉ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ወሜምዎ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ አባ ፡ ዲዮናስዮስ ፡
On this day also died the holy father abbä Diyonäsyos (= Dionysius), patriarch of the city of Alexandria; among the patriarch fathers, he is the fourteenth. The parents of this father were pagans and worshipped the sun. He had learned many sciences, for his father was from an illustrious family, had taken care of him since his childhood, and had taught him all wisdom and science until he became a master for the pagan sages. While he was sitting one day, behold a Christian old woman passed before him; (she had) with her a fascicle of the letters of Päwlos (= Paul) the apostle. She said to him: "Give me something." He took this fascicle. When he read (it), he found in it an admirable word and an astonishing science. (The woman) asked him as a price for it a gold drachma. He gave her three gold drachmas and said to her: "Go, make inquiries to find what remains of this book and bring (it) to me." She went and brought him three other fascicles; he gave her nine gold drachmas. When he read them, he found the book incomplete. He said to her: "Go and look for what remains of this book." She said to him: "I found these (fascicles) among the books of my parents; if you want the complete book of Päwlos the apostle, ask for it from the people of this church." He said to her: "If I ask them for it, will they give it to me?" She said to him: "Yes." He went to ask for the book of the apostle Päwlos from one of the priests, and (the latter) gave it to him. He read it, learned it by heart - he recited it out loud without reading it - and believed in our Lord Christ. Then he went to the saint Demêtreyos (= Demetrius) the patriarch and asked him to baptize him with Christian baptism; (Demêtreyos) taught him everything that (Diyonäsyos) wanted, baptized him with Christian baptism, and he became accomplished in the science of the Church. Abbä Demêtreyos established him as a master for the children of the Church. When abbä Yäroklä (= Heraclas) was appointed patriarch, he established this father in his place in the city of Alexandria to render justice among the believers, and he entrusted him with all the affairs of the patriarchate to administer them. When the saint abbä Yäroklä died, all the people agreed and appointed this father abbä Diyonäsyos patriarch of the city of Alexandria. He guarded his flock with a beautiful guard.
ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፣፤ ወዓቀበ ፣ መርዔቶ ፡ በሠናይ ፡ ተዓቅቦ ። ወበጽሖ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ወሐዘን ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ በመዋዕለ ፡ ሚመቱ ። እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ተሠይመ ፡ በመዋዕለ ፤ መንግሥቱ ፡ ለፊልኦስ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ያፈቅሮሙ ፡ ለክርስቲያን ። ወሶበ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ዳኪዮስ፤፤ ፡ ወቀተሎ፤፤ ፡ ወነግሠ፤፤ ፡ ህየንቴሁ ፡ ወአመንደቦሙ ፡ ለመሃይምናን፤!፤ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡ ዓቢየ ። ወቀተለ ፡ ሰማዕታተ ፡ ብዙኃነ ፡ እምነ ፡ ሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ወኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅሳት ፡ ወካልኣን ። ወጐዩ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ በእንተ ፡ ድንጋፄ ፤ ወፍርሀት ፡ ወቦኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳማት ፡ ወሞቱ ፡ ብዙኃን ፣ እምኔሆሙ፤! ፣ በህየ ። ወአኀዝዎ ፡ ሐራሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ወአኅሰርዎ ፡ ብዙኃ ።
Great misfortunes and numerous sorrows happened to him during the days of his charge: he was appointed in the time of the reign of Filpos (= Philip the Arab) and (the latter) loved the Christians. When Däkiyos (= Decius) rose against him, (Däkiyos) killed him, reigned in his place, and subjected the believers to a great persecution. He killed numerous martyrs among the patriarchs, the bishops, and the other (Christians). Many (people) fled far from him out of dread and fear, and entered the deserts; many of them

ወእምዝ ፡ ጠፍአ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ከሐዲ ፡ ወነግሠ ፡ እምድኅሬሁ፤!' ፡ ግርላዎስ ፡ ወሃ ድአ፤፤ ፣ ምንዳቤ ፡ እምላዕለ ፡ ውሉደ ፡ ጥምቀት ፡ በኩሉ ፡ መዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ንጉሥ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሞተ ፡ ነግሠ ፡ እምድኅሬሁ ፡ ዋላርዮስ ፡ አንሥአ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡ ላዕለ!! ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወአኀዝዎ ፡ መኳንንቲሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፥ አብ ፡ ወሞቅሕዎ ፡ ወኩነንዎ ፡ ኩኔኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ፅፁበ!። ፡ ፈድፋደ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ኃሠሥ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስግድ ፡ ለጣዖታት ፡ ወአውሥኦሙ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ንሕነሰ ፡ ንሰግድ፤፡፤ ፡ ለእግዚአብ ሔር ፡ አብ ፡ ወለወልዱ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወለመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወተምዓ ፡ ላዕ ሌሁ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወቀተለ ፤ በቅድሜሁ ፡፣ ብዙኃነ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያፍርሆ ። ወቅዱስሰ ፡ ኢፈርሀ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወሰደዶ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ስደት ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ መዋዕለ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ሜጦ ፡ እምስደት ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ሰማዕኩ ፡፣ በእንቲአከ ፡ ከመ ፡ አንተ ፡ ትቄድስ፤፡። ፡ በኅቡእ፤፤ ፡ ባሕቲትከ ። ወአውሥኦ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ንሕነሰ ፡ ኢነኃድግ ፡ ጸሎታቲነ፤፤ ፡ ኢበሌሊት ፡ ወኢበመዓልት ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተመይጠ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ እለ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ሖሩ ፡ ወቀድሱ ፡ ወለእመ ፡ ኮንኩ ፡ ርሑቀ ፡ እምኔክሙ ፡ በሥጋ ፡ አንሰ ፡ እሄሉ ፡ ምስሌክሙ ፡ በመንፈስ ። ወተምዓ ፡ መኩንን ፡፤ ወሜጦ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ስደት ። ወአንሥአ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ከሐዲ ፡ ትዕይንተ ፡ እምሰብአ ፡ በርባር ፣ ወፈርሀ ፤ እምኔሆሙ፤ ። ወመጠወ ፣ መንግሥቶ ፡ ለወልዱ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ጠቢበ ፡ ወማእምረ፤" ፡ ወሰደዶሙ፤፡ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡መሃይምናን ፡እለ ፡ ሞቅሖሙ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወሜጦሙ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ስደት፣ ወጸሐፈ ፤ መጽሐፈ ፡ መልእክት ፡ ለሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ወለኩሎሙ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅሳት ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ኢትፍርሁ፤ ፡ ወአርኅዉ ፡ አብያተ ፤ ክርስቲያናቲክሙ!ቦ ። ወኢይበ
died there. The soldiers arrested this father and subjected him to many insults. Then the impious one perished, Gerläwos (= Gallus) reigned after him, and the persecution against the children of baptism subsided during all the days of this emperor. When he died, Wäläryos (= Valerian) reigned after him and reawakened the persecution against the Christians. His officers seized this father, imprisoned him, subjected him to a great (and) very painful torture, then asked him to prostrate himself before the idols. He answered them and said to them: "We prostrate ourselves before God the Father, his Son Jesus Christ, and the Holy Spirit." The emperor became very angry with him and (had) many people killed before him to frighten him, but the saint was not afraid of him. (The emperor) exiled him and he remained in exile for many days. After that, (the emperor) made him return from exile and said to him: "I have learned about you that you were celebrating the office alone in secret." The saint answered him and said to him: "We, we do not cease our prayers neither by night nor by day." Then the saint turned to the people who (were) with him and said to them: "Go and celebrate the office; if I am far from you by the body, I am with you by the spirit." The prince became angry and sent him back into exile. God raised up armies of barbarians against this infidel; (the latter) was afraid of them and handed over his empire to his son. His son was wise and learned; he released all the believers whom his father had imprisoned, brought back those who (were) in exile, and wrote a letter to the patriarch and to all the bishops (where) he said to them: "Do not be afraid and open your churches. Nothing untoward will happen to you." This father lived what remained of his days in tranquility and in peace.
ጽሕ ፡ ላፅሌክሙ ፡ ወኢምንትኒ ፡ እምእኩይ ። ወሐይወ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ 'ዘተረፈ ፡ እመዋዕሊሁ፤፤ ፡ በህድአት ፡ ወበሰላም ። ወአስተርአየ ፡ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡፤ አብ ፡ ግዕዛት ፡ ብዙኃት ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሃይማ ኖት ። ወእምውእቱ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ አስተርአዩ ፡ በብሔረ ፡ አራቢያ፤?" ፡ ስሕቱ ፡ ወይቤሉ ፡ በእከየ ፡ ምክሮሙ፤፤፤ ፡ እስመ ፡ ነፍስ ፡ ትመውት ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ወእምዝ ፣ ትትነሣእ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ በፅለተ ፣፤ ትንሣኤ ። ወአስተጋብአ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡፣ ማኅበረ ፣ ወአውገዞሙ ፡ ወመተሮሙ ። ወካልኣን ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ በግዕዘ ፡ ኦሪጋኖስ ፡ ወስብልያ ኖስ፤ ። ወሶበ ፡ ልህቀ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ አስተርአየ ፡ 'ጳውሊ ፡ ስመይጸጢ ፡ ሊቀ፣። ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ፡ ዘክህዶ ፡ ለወልድ ፡ ወተጋብአ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ማኅበር ፡ በሀገረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ፡ ወኢበጽሐ ፤ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ በእንተ ፣ ርሥዕናሁ ። ወፈነወ ፡ መልእክተ ፡ እንተ ፡ ምልእት ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ጥበብ ፡፤ ወጸጋ ፡ ወከሠተ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ርትዕተ ፡ ወምክረ ፡ ቴረ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ርሥእና ፡ ሠናይት ፡ ፈለሰ ፡ ኀበ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ነበረ ፡ ዲበ ፡ መንበረ ፡ ማርቆስ ፡ ወንጌላዊ ፣ ፲ወፄዓመተ፤ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ አሜን ።
In the time of this father, numerous sentiments appeared concerning the faith. Among them, (there is this one): people appeared in the land of 'Aräbiyä (= Arabia) (who) wandered and said, in the malignancy of their opinion, that the soul died with the body, then resurrected with it on the day of the resurrection. (Diyonäsyos) gathered a council against them, excommunicated them, and cut them off (from the Church). Others still were in the sentiment of 'Origänos (= Origen) and of Sebelyänos (= Sabellius). When this father had become old, Päwli (= Paul) the Samosatene appeared, patriarch of the city of 'Ansokiyä (= Antioch), who denied the Son. A council gathered against him in the city of 'Ansokiyä, but this father did not come with them (= the conciliar fathers) because of his old age. He sent a letter which (was) full of all wisdom and grace; he exposed in it the right faith and the good opinion. When he had reached a beautiful old age, he went to God after having remained on the seat of Märqos (= Mark) the Evangelist for seventeen years. May God have mercy on us through his prayer, amen.


[p. 362] {#po.46.p362}
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ዕረፍቱ ፡ ለአብ ፡ ክቡር ፡ አባ ፡ ትርሲት ፡ ዘደብረ ፡ ሣራት ፡ ወልደ ፡ ዮሴፍ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ሠርፀ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ ብሩሃ ፡ ልቡና ፡ ወበዐለ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ትሩፋት ፡ ወዐምደ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ጽኑዕ ፡፤ ቄርሎስ ፣ ዳግማዊ ፣ ዘተርጐሞሙ ፡ ለብዙኃን ፣ መጻሕ ፍት ፤ በበጾታሆሙ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሀረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day also, (there is) the death of the illustrious father abbä Tersit of Dabra Sarät, son of Yosëf (= Joseph), son of Sarsa Hawaryät. (He was) of a luminous intelligence and had numerous virtues, a firm column of the faith, a second Qêrellos (= Cyril), (he) who explained numerous books, each according to its genre. May God have mercy on us through his prayer, amen.]

### READING FOR THE 14TH OF MAGGABIT
አመ ፡ ፲፪ለመጋቢት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። 'በዛቲ ፡ ፅለት፤ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ዕረፍቱ ፡ ለአብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጸጻሳት ፡ ዘእለ ፡ እስክንድ ርያ ፤ ቄርሎስ ፤ ዘይሰመይ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ዘፍቁራን ፡ ዘእሙር ፡ ቀሲስ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ወልደ ፡ ልቀልቀ፥ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ እምጐልቆሙ ፡ ለአበው ፡ ሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ድወ፳ ። ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ ፍዩም ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ተሠይመ ፡ ቀሲሰ ፡ ባቲ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ፈለሰ ፡ እምሀየ ፣ ወበጽሐ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ፊቅጦር ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ዘአፍአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ምስር ፡ በዓዘቅተ ፡ ኢትዮጵያዊያን ። ወነበረ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ዓመታተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትጋደል ፡ ገድለ፡ ፣ ዐቢየ ። ወዝንቱሰ ፡ አብ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ትሩፈ ፡ ወማዕምረ ፡ ወዓቀበ ፡ ብዙኃ ፣ መጻሕፍተ ፡ ወትምህርተቶ ፡ ወትርጓሜሆሙቶ ፡ በአእምሮ ፡ ሠናይ ። ወበጽሐ ፡ ዓውደ ፡ መንበሩ ለመዋዒ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ፍጹም ፤ እምቅድመ ፤ ሚመተ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጵጵስናሁ ። ወተዋሥእዎ ፡ ጉባኤ ፡ ዓበይት ፡ መምህራነ ፡ ተንበላት ፤ ወማእምራኒሆሙ ፡ ወረከቡ ፡ በኀቤሁ ፤ ተሠጥዎተ ፡ ጥበብ ፡ ወነገሮሙ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘተስእልዎ ፡ በሠናይ ፡ አውሥኦ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሶበ ፣፤ ተንሥአ ፣ ዜናሁ ፣፤ በአእምሮ ፡፣ ወቅድስና ፡ ወአኃዝዎ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡፤ ፈቃዱ ፡ ወሜምዎ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፤ ወዓቀበ ፣ መርዓዔቶ ፡ ወሠርዓ ፡ ቀኖና ፡ ዘይደሉ ፡ ለካህናት ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይትለአኩ ፡ ለቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ጸሎታት ፡ ወቅዳሴያት ። ወነበረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ በሊቀ ፡ ጵጵስናሁ ፡ ፪ዓመታተ ፤ ወ፪አውራኃ ፡ ወ፲መዋዕለ ፡ ወአእረፈ ፡ በሠለስቱ ፡ ሰዓት ፡ ዘመዓልት ፡ ወተቀብረ ፡ በደብረ ፡ ስምዕ ። በረከተ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ትኩን ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ወፍቅርቱሂ ፡ ኮቶሊካዊት ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አባ ፡ በጥል ። ወዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምአዕ ሩግ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ትሩፋን ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ዜና ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ መሐሪ ፡ ከመ፣ ኢይትዌከፍ ፡ ቃለ ፡ መስተዋድያን ፡ ላዕለ ፡ መነኮሳት?" ፡ ኃደገ ፡ ምኔቶ ፡ ወመጽአ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወጸሐፈ ፡ ስመ ፡ ኩሎን ፡ ዘማዊያቲሃ፤፤ ። ወአኃዘ ፡ ይትገበር ፡ ምስለ ፡ መስተገብራን ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ይነሥእ ፡ አስቦ፤ ፡ ይሣየጥ ፡ በ፳ጸሪቅ ፡ ተርሙሰ ፡ ይበልዕ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. [On this day, there is the death of the holy father, the patriarch of Alexandria Qêrellos (= Cyril III) who is called "son of the beloved" (and) who (is) known (by the name of) "priest Däwit (= David), son of Lequeleque" (= Ibn Laglaq); among the patriarch fathers, he is the seventy-fifth. This father was from the city of Feyum (= Fayoum), he had been ordained priest there. Then he had gone from there and had come to the monastery of Figtor (= Victor) the martyr which (is) outside the city of Mesr (= Cairo), near the Well of the Ethiopians. He remained there for many years leading a great (spiritual) combat. This father was virtuous and learned, he knew by heart numerous books and teachings, as well as their interpretation, according to a just science. He came to the court of Mawä'i (= Mu'awiya), the accomplished king, before being consecrated patriarch. A great assembly of doctors of the Muslims and of their scholars disputed with him; they found in him wise replies, and he gave them beautiful answers on everything they asked him. Then, when his reputation for science and wisdom had grown, he was seized against his will and appointed patriarch of the city of Alexandria. He guarded his flock and instituted a regulation that addresses the priests when they perform their ministry for the church at the time of prayers and divine services. This father remained in his patriarchal charge for seven years, nine months, and ten days; he died at the third hour of the day and was buried in the monastery of Sem'e (= Ech-Cham'a). May the blessing of his holy prayer be with his dear 'Atnätëwos and also his dear Kotolikäwit in the centuries of centuries.] [On this day also died abbä Batel. This saint was (among) the old virtuous monks. When he heard it said that the saint Yohannes (= John) the Merciful did not receive the word of those who slandered the monks, he left his monastery, came to Alexandria, and noted down in writing the name of all the prostitutes of it. He began to work with the laborers and,

when he received his salary, he bought for a small coin of lupin, ate, took what remained, went to a prostitute, gave (it) to her, and said to her: "Prepare yourself for this night, for I am going to spend the night with you." He spent the night at her place to preserve her from sin; he rose, prayed, made prostrations, and implored God. In the morning, he made her swear to tell no one his way of acting, then he went to his work. He went every day to (another) prostitute and acted thus. One of them revealed the thing: a demon took possession of her and she became mad. The people said among themselves: "God has acted well regarding him, for she lied about this wicked old man; she said: This old man is without stain, on the contrary he guards us so that we do not fornicate and he gives us money." When the prostitutes who remained heard (this), they were afraid to confirm her words and feared becoming mad like her. (The saint) began to say: "This night (is) the share of So-and-so, she is waiting for me." (The people) began to insult him and he said to them: "Do I not have a body like (other) men or would God have become angry with the monks (and) let them die in lust?" [They said to him: "Marry a woman and change your clothes, for (that) is better for you." He said to them: "Yes, I occupy myself with solicitude for the woman and (her) needs and I spend painful days. Go far from me, what do you want? Has God instituted you as judges against me? Occupy yourselves then with yourselves, for you, you will not be punished because of me. The Judge is unique and the day (of Judgment) also is unique." This saint was accepted by God and rejected by men. The prostitutes profited from him and abandoned fornication. Some became nuns and some married according to the law. One day, while (the saint) was leaving the house of one of them, a man who wanted to enter it met him. He slapped the saint saying: "Until when will this old man continue to act badly?" One day, someone entered the house of this old man and found him kneeling: he had fallen asleep and had surrendered his spirit to God. On the floor of his house (were) written (words) saying: "People of Alexandria, do not condemn anyone before his time and hour - until God comes." May his prayer and his blessing be with his dear 'Atnätëwos in the centuries of centuries.]
ፍቁሩ ፤ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ። በዛቲ ፡ ዐለት ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሲኖዳ ፡ ሰማዕት ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ ብህንሳ ፣ እምብሔረ" ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ወአስተዋደይዎ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መኩ ንን ፡ መክሲሞስ” ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ መኩንነ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ ዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ እስመ ፡ "ውእቱ ፤ ክርስቲያናዊ ፤ ወኢያመልክ፤ ፡ ጣዖታተ ። ወአብጽሖ ፡ ወተስእሎ ፡ በእ ንተ ፡ ሃይማኖቱ ፡ ወተአምነ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ዘበአማን ። ወአዘዘ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ከመ ፡ ያስፍሕዎ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ዲበ ፡
On this day, the saint Sinodä (= Shenoute) became a martyr. This saint was from the city of Behnesä (= Béhnésa) in the land of Egypt. This saint was denounced to the governor Maksimos (= Maximus), who had been (appointed) governor by Diyogletyänos (= Diocletian), and they said to him: "He is a Christian and does not worship idols." (Maksimos) had him brought and questioned him about his faith. (Sinodä) confessed before him our Lord Jesus Christ as being

ምድር ፡ ወይዝብጥዎ ፡ በአብትር ፡ እለ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እደዊሆሙ ፡ ለሐራ ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ ቦቱ፣0 ዘንተ ፡ እስከ ፡ ተሰጥቀ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ወውሕዘ ፡ ደሙ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ምድር?" ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሰሐ ብዎ ፡ በእግሩ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወገደፍዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ፡ ጸሊም ፡ዘጽዩእ፣ ጹናሁ ። ወአስተርአዮ ፡ መልአከ ፡፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ወአጽንዖ ፡ ወረሰዮ ፡ ጥዑየ ፡ ወፈወሰ ፡ ቆቀ“ስላቲሁ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ጽናዕ ፡ ወኢትፍራህ ፡ እስመ ፡ አስተዳለወ ፡ ለከ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ በእንተ ፡ ጸማከ ፡ ወሀለወከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትትኩነን ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወአነ ፡ እሄሉ ፡ ምስሌከ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዘኒ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ተኃብአ ፡ እም ኔሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ጽባሕ ፡ አዘዞሙ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ለሐራሁ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ርእይዎ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ዓላዊ ፡ ዘተዓደወ ፡ ትእዛዘ ፡ ነገሥት ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሞተ ፡ ውግርዎ ፡ ለከለባት ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ረከብዎ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይቀውም ፡ ወይጺጹሊ ፡ ወነገርዎ ፡ ለመኩንን ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ጥዑይ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፤ ሙስና ። ወአልቦ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ አሰረ ፡ ሕማም ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ይጴሊ ፡ በጻሕቅ ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ከመ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ፍሥሕ ፤ ወጥዑይ ፡ ዘኢይረክቦ ፡ ኩነኔ ፤ ዐቢይ ፡ ግሙራ ። ወአብጽሖ ፣ መኩ ንን ፤ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወአንከረ ፡ እምሕይወቱ ፤ ወእምዝ ፡ አዕረቀ ፡ አልባሲሁ ፡ ወርአየ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡እንዘ ፡ ጥዑይ ፡ዘእንበለ ፡ ሙስና ። ወደንገፀ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ እስመ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ሥራይ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ዘኢርኢኩ ፡ ዘከማሁ ፡ ግሙራ ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስቅልዎ ፡ ለቅዱስ፥፤ ፡ ቀርልጐሊተ ፡ ወያንድዱ"፤ ፡ መትሕቴሁ ፤ እሳተ ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ ቦቱ ፤ ዘንተ ። ወእምድኅ ረዝ ፡ ኩነንዎ ፡ በመንኩራኩራት ፡ ወዘበጥዎ ፡ በአስዋጣት ፣ ዝብጠታተ ፣ ማሕመሚት ። ወሶበ ፡ ደክመ ፡ መኩንን ፡ እምኩነኔሁ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምትርዎ ፡ በአስይፍት ፤ ወመተርዎ ፡ በአስይፍት ፡ ወወገርዎ ፡ ለከለባት ፡ ወኢቀረብዎ ፡ ከለባት ፡ ግሙራ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሌሊተ ፡ ነሥእዎ ፡ መሃይምናን ፡ በኅቡእ ፡ ወገነዝዎ ፡ በአልባ ሳት ፡ ሐዲሳት ፡'ወገነዝዎ ፡ በዕፍረታትቶ ፡ ብዙኃት ፡ ወአንበርዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሣጹን ፡ ወቀበርዎ ። ወኮነ ፡ እምሥጋሁ ፡ ተኣምራት ፡ ብዙኃት ፡ ወፈውስ ፡ ለድውያን ። ወነሥኣ ፡ ለአክሊለ ፡ ሕይወት፥ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡
true God. The governor ordered the saint to be stretched on the ground and to be struck with the sticks that (are) in the hands of the soldiers. They did this to him until his body was broken and his blood flowed to the ground. Then, he was dragged on the ground by his feet and thrown into an obscure prison with an infectious odor. The angel of God Mikä'el (= Michael) appeared to him, fortified him, made him safe and sound, healed his wounds, and said to him: "Be strong and do not be afraid, for (God) has prepared for you a crown of glory for your sufferings; you are going to undergo a great torture, but I, I will be with you as God has ordered me." Then he hid himself from his (sight). When it was morning, the governor gave an order to his soldiers and said to them: "(Go) see the infidel who has transgressed the order of the emperors; if he is dead, throw him to the dogs." When the soldiers arrived near him, they found him standing and praying. They told the governor that he was unharmed (and) without damage [and that there was no trace of pain on his body; he was praying with great zeal like a joyful and unharmed man whom a great torture has never affected. The governor had him brought before him and was astonished at his good condition. Then he (had) him stripped of his clothes and saw his body unharmed and without damage.] He was stupefied and said: "This is great magic, such as I have never seen the like." He ordered the saint to be hung upside down and fire to be lit under him, and they did this to him. After that, they tortured him with wheels and subjected him to a painful flagellation with rods. When the governor was weary of torturing him, he ordered him to be cut up with swords; he was cut up with swords and thrown to the dogs, but the dogs did not approach him at all. When it was night, believers took him in secret, shrouded him in new clothes, prepared him with numerous perfumes, placed him in a coffin, and buried him. Numerous miracles occurred from his body as well as healing for the sick. He received the crown of life in the kingdom of heaven. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.
ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ሰማዕታት ፡ አውጋንዮስ ፡ ወአውጋኑድሮስ ፡ ወአሊቢድዮስ ። እሉ ፡፣ ቅዱሳን ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ፣ እምአበዊሆሙ ፡ ወአበ ፡ አበዊሆሙ ፡እንዘ ፡ የሐውሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፍኖተ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፤ ወምሑራን ፡ በኩሉ ፤ ትምህርታት ፡ መለኮታዊትቶ፥ ። ወኀረዮሙ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አርሞስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወሜሞሙ ፡ ኤጴስ ፡ ቆልሳተ ፡ አኮ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ሚመታት ፡ ዕውቃት ፡አላ፣ ፈነዎሙ ፡ ይስብኩ ፡ በወንጌል ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አህጉር ፡ ወሰበኩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አህጉር ፡ ብዙኅ ። ወአኃዝዎሙ ፡ ከሐዲያን" ፡ ወቀሠፍዎሙ ፣ መቅሠፍተ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ምሕረት ፡ ወወገርዎሙ ፡ በአእባን ፡ ወአሰርዎሙ ፣ መታክብቲሆሙ ፡ ወዘበጥዎሙ ፡፣ እንዘ ፣ ይስሕብዎሙ0፤ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ እስከ ፡ አፅረፉ ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
On this day also, there are the holy martyrs 'Awgänyos (= Eugenius), 'Awgänudros (= Agathodorus), and 'Alibidyos (= Elpidius). These saints were Christians (by family) since their fathers and the fathers of their fathers, walking in the way of God and instructed in all divine teachings. The saint Armos (= Hermon), patriarch of the city of Jerusalem, chose them and consecrated them bishops; (he) did not (destine them) for determined seats, but sent them to preach the holy Gospel in all the cities, and they preached in numerous cities. The pagans seized them, subjected them without pity to a great flagellation, stoned them, tied their shoulders, and struck them while dragging them on the ground until they died. May the prayer of them all be with us, amen.

ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ተአምራተ ፡ ቶማስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ በላዕለ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ ዘአጽሐባ ፡ ትድምርተ ፡ ጋኔን ፡ መጠነ ፡ ጻዓመት፡ ። ሶበ ፡ ጸርሐት ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትብል ፡ ሐዋርያሁ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ አድኅነኒ ፡ እምጸላኢ ፡ ዘያመነድበኒ ፡ ጥቀ ፡ ወይብል ፤ በምንት ፡ ምክንያት ፡ ረከበኪ ። ወትቤሎ ፡ እንዘ ፡ እወፅእ ፣ እምቤተ ፡ ብለኔ ፡ ተቀበለኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘጐጐዓ ፡ በሁከት ፡ ወቃሉሂ ፡ በሊሕ ፡ ወቀጢን ፡ ወይቤለኒ ፡ ንዒ ፡ ንሳተፍ ፡ በበይናቲነ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ብእሲት ። ወእቤሎ ፡ ለዘፈኃረኒቶ፥ ፡ ጥቀ ፡ ኢይፈቅድ ፡ ተሳትፎ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ኅድግ ፡ ኪያከሰ ። ወዘንተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ እሄሊ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ደከስኩ ፡ በሌሊት ፡ መጽአ ፡ ወተደመረሂኒ ፡ ወኀበ ፡ ጐየይኩ ፡ ያመነድበኒቶ፥ ። እስመ ፡ አነ ፡ አአምር ፡ ከመ ፡ አጋንንት ፡ ሰይጣናት ፡ ይርዕዱ ፡ እምጸሎትከ ፡ ጸሊ ፡ ላዕሌየ ፡ ወአድኅነኒ" ፡ እምንዳቤየ ። ወሰሚዖ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ጸለየ ፡ ላዕሌሃ ፡ ወአውገዞ ፡ ለሰይጣን ፡ ወወፅአ ፡ እምኔሃ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ዓተባ ፡ በማዕተበ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወመጠዋ ፡ ምሥጢራተ ፡ ቅድሳት ፡ ወፈነዋ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አዝማዲሁ ። በረከቱ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ትኩን ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፤ አትናቴዎስ ፤ ወፍቅርቱሂ ፡ ኮቶሊካዊት ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day also Tomäs (= Thomas) the apostle performed a miracle on a woman whom a demon had forced into coitus (with him) for seven years. When she cried out to him saying: "Apostle of Christ, save me from the Enemy who torments me harshly," he said: "By what ruse did he find you?" She said to him: "While I was leaving the baths, he approached me like a man who was hurrying with agitation - but his speech (was) sweet and light - and he said to me: 'Come, let us unite in all the act of the woman.' I said to him: 'I do not even want to unite with him who is betrothed to me, much less with you!' While I was thinking about this, as I was falling asleep at night, he came, united with me, and, wherever I fled, tormented me. As I know that demons (and) devils tremble before your prayer, pray for me and save me from my torment." Having heard (this), the apostle prayed for her, cursed the devil, and (the latter) left her. Then he traced the sign of Christ over her, administered the holy Mysteries to her, and sent her back to her family. May his holy blessing be with his dear 'Atnätëwos and also his dear Kotolikäwit in the centuries of centuries, amen.]

### READING FOR THE 15TH OF MAGGABIT
አመ ፡ ፲፳ለመጋቢት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አፅረፈት ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ጸድቅት ፡ መስተጋድልት ፡ ሳራ ፡ መነኮሳይት ። ዛቲ ፤ ቅድስት ፡ ኮነት ፡ እምላዕላይ፤ ፡ ግብጽ ። ወኮኑ ፡ አበዊሃ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወያመልክዎ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወኮኑ፤ ፡ ብዑላን ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወኢኮነ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ዘእንበሌሃ ፡ ወሐጸንዋ ፡ ላዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ወመሀርዋ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ትምህርተ ፡ መሲሐዊተ ፡ ወመሐርዋ ፡ ጽሕፈተ ። ወኮነት ፡ ወትረ ፡ ታነብብ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ መጻሕፍተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወፈድፋደሰ ፣ ዜናሆሙ ፡ ለመነኮሳት ። ወበእንተ" ፡ አንብቦታ ፡ ወትረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዜናሆሙ ፡ ለመነኮሳት ፡ ፈተወት ፡ 'ከመ ፡ ትልበስ?" ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ መንፈሳዊተ ፡ ወዓርገት ፡ ኀበ ፡ ፳እምነ ፡ አድባራት ፡ ዘደናግል ፡ እለ ፡ በላዕላይ ፤ ግብጽ ፡ ወነበረት ፤ ውስቴቱ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ዓመታተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትትለአኮን ፣ ለደናግል ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ለብሰት ፡ አልባሰ ፡፤ ምንኩስና ፡ በውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ደብር ። ወነበረት ፡ እንዘ ፣ ትትጋደሎ ፡ 'ለሰይጣነ ፡ ፍትወት ፡ ፲ ወ፻ዓመተ ፡ እስከ ፡ ሞአቶ፣ ለሰይጣን ። ወሶበ ፡ ተሞአ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ወደክመ ፡ እምጽንዓ ፡ 'ወፍቅራ ፡ ለንጽሕና" ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያውድቃ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ትዕቢት ፡ ወአስተርአየ ፡ ላቲ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትቀውም ፡ ውስተ ፡ ተድባበ ፡ ቤታ ፡ ትጹሊ ፡ ወይቤላ ፤ ተፈሥሒ ፡ ኦሳራ ፡ እስመ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ሞእ ኪዮ ፡ ለሰይጣን ። ወአውሥአቶ ፡ ወትቤሎ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ ድክምት ፡ ኢይክል ፡ እሙኦ ፡ ወኢይመውኦ ፡ ወኢይሰድዶ ፡ እምኔየ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ እግዚእየ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስ ቶስ ። ወዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ኮነት ፡ ትሜህርሮን ፡ ለደናግል ፡ እለ ፡ ምስሌሃ ፡ ትምህርታተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ፤ ወበሞዔተ ። ወትቤሎን ፡ አንሰ ፡ ኢያነብር ፡ እገሪየ ፡ ዲበ ፡ መዓርገ!" ፡ ሰዋስው ፡ ወኣዓርጋ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ካልእታ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ አሐሊ ፡ ከመ ፡ አነ ፡ እመውት ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the holy (and) just Särä (= Sarah), the (spiritual) combatant nun. This saint was from Upper Egypt. Her parents were Christians and worshipped our Lord Christ. They were very rich and had no other child than her. They raised this saint, taught her all Christian teaching, and taught her to write. She constantly read all the books of the Church, especially the history of the monks. By constantly reading the history of the monks, she desired to put on the spiritual monastic garment. She went up to one of the monasteries of virgins that (were) in Upper Egypt and remained there for many years serving the virgins; then she put on the monastic garment in this monastery. She remained fighting the demon of concupiscence for thirteen years until she defeated the demon. When the demon was defeated and tired by her firmness and her love for purity, it wanted to make her fall into pride. It appeared to her then as she was standing in prayer on the roof of her cell. It said to her: "Rejoice, Särä, for behold you have defeated the demon." She answered it and said to it: "I am a weak woman, I cannot defeat it; it is only my Lord Jesus Christ who has defeated it and driven it far from me." This saint dispensed to the virgins who (were) with her numerous and profitable teachings. She said to them: "I do not place my foot on a rung of a ladder (nor) do I lift it to the second without thinking


[p. 370] {#po.46.p370}
እምቅድመ ፡ ኣዓርጋ ፡ ለእግርየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢያስሕተኒ ፡ ጸላኢ ፡ በነዊኅ ፡ መዋዕል ። ወትቤ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ይይስ ፡ ለብእሲ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይግበር ፡ ምሕረተ ፡ ወለእመ ፡ ኮነት ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሰብእ ። ወሶበ ፤ ይገብር ፡፤ ወትረ፤፤ ፣ ዘንተ ፣ ወድኅረ ፣ ይከውን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሥምረቱ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወተናገረት ፡ ቃላተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ፡ ዘጽሑፋት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዜና ፡ አረጋዊ ያን ፡ መነኮሳት ። ወነበረት ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትትጋደል ፡ ተጋድሎ ፡ ዓቢየ ። ወኮነት ፡ በዓታ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ጽንፈ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ወነበረት ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ 'ጽቿዓመተ ፡ ኢርእያ ፡ መኑሂ ፡ ወኢርእየታ ፡ ይእቲኒ ፡ ለባሕር ፡ አሐተ ፡ ፅለተ፤ ። ወሶበ ፡ ልህቀት ፡ 'ዛቲ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ወኮነ ፡ መዋፅሊሃ! ፡ ዓመተ ፡ ወተፈትሐት ፡ እማእሰረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡፤ ኃላፊ! ። ወአዕረፈት ፡ ወሖረት ፡ ውስተ ፤ ትፍሥሕት ፡ ዘለዓለም ። ጸሎታ ፡ ወበረ ከታ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ስምዓ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሶለፍኮስ ፡ ፈሃሪሃ ፡፤ ለቅድ ስት ፡ አስጠራጦኒቃ ። ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ክርስቲያናዊ"! ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ያብጽሕዎ ፡ ኅቤሁ ። ወበጺሖ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ይቤ፤" ፡ አንተኑ ፡ ሶለፍኮስ ፡ ፈሃሪሃ ፡ ለአስጠራጦኒቃ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ እወ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ለመኑ ፡፤ ታመልክ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ለኢየሱስ!" ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሕያው ፡ ሉቱ ፡ ስብሐት ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ይደይዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ግበ ፡ አክይስት ፡ እለ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ኅምዘ ፡ ወአድኃኖ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወእምዝ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ይምትሩ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ በሰይፍ ፡ ወመተርዎ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፤ ሕይወት ። በረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፣ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ወፍቅርቱሂ ፣ ኮቶሊካዊት ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
that I will die before lifting my foot, for fear that the Enemy might seduce me by (the hope of) living long days." She said again: "It is good for a man to practice mercy, even if (this) is exercised because of (the judgment of) men; if he does this continuously, he will then obtain the approval of God." She spoke many words that (are) recorded in the history of the old monks. This saint remained leading a great (spiritual) combat. Her cell was at the edge of the river, she remained there for sixty years. No one saw her and she herself did not see the river for a single day. When this saint had aged and her days were eighty years, she was freed from the bonds of this transitory world. She died and went into eternal joy. May her prayer and her blessing be with us in the centuries of centuries, amen. [On this day also became a martyr Solafkos (= Sulpicius), the fiancé of the saint 'Astarätonigä (= Stratonice). When the emperor learned that this saint was a Christian, he ordered him to be brought before him. When (the latter) arrived before him, he said: "Are you Solafkos, the fiancé of 'Astarätoniga?" (Solafkos) said: "Yes." (The emperor) said to him: "Whom do you worship?" (Solafkos) said to him: "Jesus Christ, Son of the living God, glory to him!" The emperor ordered him to be thrown into a pit (full) of venomous snakes, but God saved him. Then, (the emperor) ordered his head to be cut off with the sword; it was cut off (for him) and he received the crown of life. May his blessing be with his dear 'Atnätëwos and also his dear Kotolikäwit in the centuries of centuries, amen.]

### READING FOR THE 16TH OF MAGGABIT

In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day became a martyr the saint abbä Helyänos (= Elijah) who (was) from the city of 'Ehnäs (= Hnis) [as well as Safänyos and the new Giyorgis (= George)]. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen. On this day also died the holy father abbä Mikä'ël (= Michael I), patriarch of the city of Alexandria; among the patriarch fathers, he is the forty-sixth. This father was a monk at the church of abbä Magäres (= Macarius) in the desert of 'Asgêtes (= Scetis). He was an ascetic and devout. When the holy father abbä Téwodros (= Theodore) died, the patriarch who (had been before him), the bishop fathers sat gathered in Mesr (= Cairo) for many days: they sought who would be the best for this charge

until a just man, upon whom (was) the gift of prophecy, informed them and said to them: "I was in the church of the saint abbä Magäres praying, I heard a voice (coming) from heaven saying: This is fitting for Mikä'ël, he is the best for this charge." All the bishops thanked him and spoke of his (= Mikä'ël's) virtues and his justice. They agreed on him and received from the governor of Mesr a letter to bring him from (his) monastery. While (the messengers) were traveling, they met him near the city of Gäzä (= Giza) coming with old holy monks for a matter of the monastery. He was seized, bound, and consecrated patriarch on the seventeenth of the month of Maskaram [the ... year since Diyogletyänos (= Diocletian) - it is the day of the feast of the Cross in the monastery of abbä Magäres -, in the time of 'Elwalid son of Hasim (= El Walid ibn Yezid); in his time reigned Warwän 'Elgädën (= Merwan el Himar?)]. The people of the city of Alexandria had remained for many years without rain falling except in small quantity. The day on which this father was consecrated, as well as the next day and the day after, an abundant rain fell. In the time of this father, great misfortunes happened to the people of the Christians. Many believers fled from the land of Egypt and the number of those who denied Christ (was) twenty-four thousand men. Because of this, this patriarch father was in very great affliction until God made perish the one because of whom this occasion (of persecution) had occurred. In the time of this father, a patriarch by the name of Qozmos (= Cosmas) was appointed head of the Melkites. This father (= Mikä'ël) disputed with him on the union of the natures of Christ. Qozmos believed in accordance with what this father told him, wrote with his hand, and said (in this writing): "Christ, after the union, (was) one incarnate nature as the saint Qérellos (= Cyril) the patriarch said." His bishops wrote similarly with their hands, saying: "It is not fitting to speak of Christ after the union and to attribute to him two separate natures nor two persons." They agreed on this (formula of) faith and on the fact of making the holy Church, gathering of the apostles, one. Qozmos, the patriarch of the Melkites, accepted to be bishop of the city of Mesr, and all agreed on this project. A Melkite by the name of Anestäsyos (= Anastasius), from the people of the city of Alexandria, caused trouble between them, for he wanted to be appointed bishop and he was not appointed. As they did not agree, Quastantinos (= Constantine), bishop of Mesr, accepted to enter under the authority of abbä Mikä'ël.

Great misfortunes befell this father at the hands of iniquitous kings: he was subjected to great torture, inflicted with numerous scourgings, bound with iron (chains), and imprisoned for many days. He was brought before judges many times so that his head might be cut off, but God saved him so that he might guard the people, his flock. [He was greatly tortured during the time of the reign of Marwän 'Elgädë (= Merwän el Himar) under the governorship of Gabra negus, son of Musë, son of Neser (='Abd el Malik ibn Mousa ibn Nasir), who had separated himself from the Jews (to enter) into Islam. (The latter) imprisoned the patriarch, demanding money from him.] Afterward, he was driven out to go to Upper Egypt to beg for alms and to bring much money to the governor. This saint went to Upper Egypt, performed many miracles there, converted many believers who had denied Christ, and brought them (back) into the right faith. When the King of Ethiopia learned the story of this father, how the Muslim governors had tortured him and how they had imprisoned him, he was seized with divine zeal for him, descended toward the land of Egypt, arrived in Upper Egypt, and devastated and plundered many cities. There were with him, when he descended into the land of Egypt, one hundred thousand horses, one hundred thousand mules, (and) one hundred thousand camels. When the King of Egypt knew that all this was happening because of the patriarch, he delivered the latter from his chains and rendered him great honors; likewise, he greatly honored all the people of the Christians. Then, the King of Egypt asked this father to send a letter to the King of Ethiopia and to order him to return to his country. This father sent a letter to the King of Ethiopia, blessing him as well as his officers and his entire army. He said to him: "Behold, God has saved me through you from the fetters of chains. Now, return to your country in calm and peace, and may God reward you in the kingdom of heaven for what you have endured because of me." When the King of Ethiopia read the letter of this father patriarch, abbä Mikäa'el, he rose quickly and returned to his country in peace. When this father had finished his beautiful (spiritual) combat, he went to God whom he loved. May his prayer and his blessing be with us for ever and ever, amen.

### READING FOR THE 17TH OF MAGGABIT
ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። አመ ፡ ፲፪ ለመጋቢት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወጻድቅ ፡ አልዓዛር ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ዘአንሥኦ ፡ እግዚእነ፤ ፣ እመቃብር ፡፤ እምድኅረ ፡ ሞተ ፡ በ፪መዓልት ። ዝንቱ ፡ ጸድቅ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ወኮና ፡ እኅታቲሁ ፡ ማርታ ፡ ወማርያ" ፡ እንተ ፡ ቀብአቶ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ዕፍረተ ። ወኮኑ ፤ ኩሎሙ ፡ አርድእቱ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ።
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the holy and righteous 'Al'azär (= Lazarus), the friend of our Lord—whom our Lord raised from the tomb four days after he had died. This righteous man was of the children of Israel. Marta (= Martha) and Märyä (= Mary) who anointed the Lord with perfume were his sisters. All

were disciples of our Lord Jesus Christ; (the latter) loved them because of their beautiful deeds and (their) virtues. All were virgins and had not married. When the moment came for this righteous man to pay the debt that is assigned to the human race and to drink the cup that all men must drink, he paid it on the same (day) as today and died. Our Lord Christ—glory to him!—knew from the beginning of ages that this righteous man would die on the same (day) as today and that he was to raise him from the dead; but he let him die—and 'Al'äzär remained for four days in the tomb until he stank, and (the Lord) raised him, the miracle then was great—so that he might see during those four days the dwellings of the good and the sinners. Our Lord—glory to him!—who raised him came (then), as Yohannes (= John) the evangelist, the theologian, is witness to this, when he says: "Our Lord called him after he had remained in the tomb four days and said to him: 'Al'äzär, 'Al'äzär, come, come out'; and he came out with his hands, feet, and face wrapped as one wraps the dead." His (= 'Al'äzär's) coming out, hands, feet, and face wrapped, is a miracle like his resurrection. The reason for his coming out of the tomb, feet, hands, and face wrapped—he had not untied them (= the bandages) in the tomb—(is) that all people might recognize with certainty his death and that others might not think that this had been a trick (used) by an agreement between them. Our Lord ordered him to come out, wrapped just as he had been buried, and, because of that, the miracle was great. If other impious people think that he was alive in the tomb, how (if that were true) would he have come out, hands, feet, and face wrapped? But we, the people of the Christians, we believe and confess that our Lord Christ raised 'Al'äzär four days after (the latter) had died, for he has power over all things. To our Lord, our God, and our Savior Jesus Christ be glory, honor, and adoration with his excellent Father and the life-giving Holy Spirit for ever and ever, amen!

[On this day became a martyr the holy Téwogritos (= Theocrite) the reader. This saint was from the city of Romë (= Rome). When all the Christians were seized and they (wanted) to force them to sacrifice to idols, this saint Tëwoqgritos was seized with them and brought before the emperor. (The latter) said to him: "Will you sacrifice or will you experience torture?" Tëwogritos was fifteen years old; he said to the emperor: "I, I offer a sacrifice to our Lord Jesus Christ—glory to him!" The emperor said to him: "Sacrifice, Tëwogritos, so as not to die a bad death." (Tëwoqgritos) said to the emperor: "Come, O emperor—you and your wise men—to see my sacrifice." The emperor ordered him to be led
