into the house of his (= the emperor's) god to sacrifice there; as for the emperor, he stood at the door. When the saint Tëwoqritos prayed, there was a great shaking in that place and 'Asqaläpyos (= Aesculapius), their god, fell. Seeing that (Tëwogritos) was mocking, the emperor ordered his nose to be cut off and for him to be tied in prison. The next day, he was brought out and put in chains. A deep pit was also dug, wood (inflamed?) was spread there, and he was thrown into it until his flesh detached from his limbs and his marrow flowed from his bones. Here too, (the pagans) could not defeat him. They brought him back into the house of their gods and locked him there, imagining that their gods would avenge them, but the saint Tëwoqritos broke the gold of the idols. The angel of God opened the door for him, (Tëwoqgritos) took the gold of the idols and distributed it to the poor and the needy. When he had returned (into the temple), the door closed again as (it was) before. On the eighth day, when the emperor opened the door, he found his idols thrown (to the ground) and broken; as for the saint, he was mocking them. (The emperor) ordered the righteous man to be torn until his entrails appeared. He ordered him to be brought into the amphitheater; he was brought (there) and lions were released upon him, and the lions began to lick his wounds. The emperor ordered him to be put into an instrument of torture until his limbs were broken and his body hideously dislocated. There again (Tëwogritos) was fully victorious and he was not afraid. When the emperor saw his courage, he ordered his head to be cut off with a sword. The saint 'Émoryon (= Emilianus), the bishop, all the people, and the crowd followed the saint Tëwoqritos, assisting him with prayer, and embraced him with the kiss of the saints. Having taken his sword, the executioner cut his neck and (the saint) finished his martyrdom. May his intercession be with his dear 'Atnätëwos for ever and ever, amen.]
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካፅበ ፣ ተዝካሮሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳን ፡ ጊዮርጊስ ፤ መስተጋድል ፡፤ ወተላስዮስ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ወዮሴፍ ፡ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ወፍልሰተ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለኒቆር ፡ሊቀ ፡ ካህናት ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፤ በጸሎቶሙ ፡፣ ለኩሎሙ ፡ አሜን ።
On this day also, (there is) the commemoration of the saints Giyorgis (= George) the fighter (spiritual), Taläsyos (= Blaise) the martyr, and Yosëf (= Joseph) the bishop [and the translation of the body of Nigor the archpriest]. May God have mercy on us through their prayers to all, amen.

### READING FOR THE 18TH OF MAGGABIT
አመ ፡ ፲፳ለመጋቢት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ኮኑ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኢስድሮስ፤ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ቢጹ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ፃና ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ እስመ ፡ ፃናሰ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምነ ፡ ሐራ ፡ ትዕይንተ ፡፣ መኩንነ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ፈረማ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ እምሰብአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ደቂያስ ። "ወኮነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኢስድሮስ ፡ ዓርቁ ፡ የአንም ፡ አልባሳተ ፡ ፀምር ። ወኮኑ ፡ ክልኤሆሙ ፡ ይመጸውቱ ፡ ለነዳያን ፡ ወለምስኪናን ፡ 'በዘ ፣ ረብሑ፤ ። ወርእዩ ፡ ክልኤሆሙ ፡ ራእየ ፡ በአሐቲ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ ድን
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day became a martyr 'Isderos (= Isidore), the companion of the saint Däna (= Sina) the martyr. Dänä was one of the soldiers of the army of the governor of the city of Faramä (= Pelusium); he was of the people of the city of Dagiyäs (= Takinach). This saint 'Isderos, his friend, sewed woolen garments. Both gave as alms to the poor and the needy what they


[p. 380] {#po.46.p380}
ግል ፡ ወምስሌሃ ፡ አክሊላት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ታነብሮሙ ፡ ዲበ ፡ አርእስቲሆሙ ። ወሶበ ፡ ነቅሁ ፡ እምንዋሞሙ ፡ ተናገሩ ፡፤ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ ዘከመ ፡፣ ርእዩ ፣ ወተፈሥሑ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፤ ወአእመሩ ፡፤ ከመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ጸውዖሙ" ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩኑ ፡ ሰማዕታተ ። ወተን ሥኡ ፡ ወሖሩ ፡ ወበጽሑ ፡ ኅበ ፡ መኩንን ። ወፈትሐ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ፃና ፡ ቅናቶ ፡ ወገደፋ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወተአምኑ ፡ ክልኤሆሙ ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፣ ወሞቅሕዎሙ!" ፡ ወፈነወ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡፤ መልአኮ ፡ ወናዘዞሙ ።
earned. Both saw a vision one night: a virgin—with her (there were) crowns—placing them on their heads. When they awoke from their sleep, they told each other what they had seen; they rejoiced greatly and knew that God had called them to be martyrs. They rose, went, and arrived before the governor. The saint Dana undid his belt and threw it before the governor; both (= the saints) confessed our Lord Christ. (The governor) gave an order and they were imprisoned. God sent his angel and (the latter) consoled them.
ወእምድኅረዝ፤፤ ፣ ፈነዎ ፡፤ መኩንን ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ፃና ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወኀደጎ ፡ 'ለቅዱስ ፤ ኢስድሮስ፤'" ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ሕዳጥ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ሜጦ ፡ መኩንነ፣ እስክንድርያ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ፃና ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ፈረማ፤! ። ወተፈሥሑ ፡ ክልኤሆሙ ፡ኅቡረ ፡ በተራክቦቶሙ ፡ ወተናገሩ ፣፤ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ ለለ፳ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ። ወኩነኖሙ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይክረዩ ፡ ዓዘቅተ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ኢስድሮስ! ፡ ወያንድዱ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ እሳተ ፡ ወይውግርዎ ፡ ለቅ ዱስ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እሳት ፤፤ ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዘ ። ወሰአሎሙ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለሐራ ፡ ከመ፣ ይትዓገሥ!፤ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይጹሊ ። ወጸለየ ፡ 'ወሰአለ ፡ እምኅበ!" ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትወከፍ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ ወየሐሊ ፡ በሥጋሁ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ቀርበ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ዓዘቅተ ፡ እሳት ፡ ወወደየ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ወመጠወ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወኢበጽሐ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፤ ወኢምንትኒ ፡ እሙስና ፡ ወኢተወስጠ ፡ አርአያሁ ። ወኮነት ፡ እሙ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ፃና ፡ ትበኪ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ በእንተ ፣ ፍልጠቱ ፡ እምዓርኩ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ጾሩ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወአንበርዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካን ፡ ሠናይ ፡ እስከ ፡ ተፍጻሜተ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ስደት ። ወበጊዜ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኢስድሮስ፤ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ርእ የት ፡ እሙ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ፃና ፡ ለብዙኃን ፡ መላእክት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይነሥኡ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ኢስድሮስ ፡ ወአዕረግዋ" ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ በዓቢይ ፡ ስብሐት ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ ወይስረይ ፡ ለነ ፡ ኃጣውኢነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡
After that, the governor sent the saint Dänä to the city of Alexandria and released the saint 'Isderos. A short time after, the governor of Alexandria sent the saint Dänä back to the city of Faramä. Both (= 'Isderos and Däna) rejoiced together at their reunion and told each other what had happened to each of them. The governor subjected them to great torture. Then he ordered a pit to be dug for the saint 'Isderos, fire to be lit there, and the saint to be thrown into the fire, and it was done as he (had ordered). The saint asked the soldiers to wait a little until he had prayed. He prayed and asked our Lord Christ to receive his soul and to take care of his body. Then he approached the pit of fire, threw himself into it, and delivered his soul into the hand of God. No harm came to his body and his appearance was not changed. The mother of the saint Dänä was weeping over him because of his separation from his friend. Afterward, his (= 'Isderos's) body was carried away and placed in a beautiful place until the end of the days of the persecution. At the moment when the saint 'Isderos became a martyr, the mother of the saint Däna saw many angels taking the soul of the saint 'Isderos, and they made it ascend to the heavens in great glory. May God have mercy on us and forgive us our sins through the prayer of this saint, and may the blessing of the latter be with us for ever and ever, amen.

### READING FOR THE 19TH OF MAGGABIT
ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። አመ ፡ ፲8ለመጋቢት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሰባኪ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ አርስጡጳውሎስ፤ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምጐልቆሙ ፡ ለድ ወ፪አርድእት ፡፤ዘሐረዮሙ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ወፈነዎሙ ፡፤ይስብኩ፣ እምቅድመ" ፡ ሕማማቲሁ ፡ ወወረደ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ጳራቅሊጦስ ፡ ወኃይል ፡ ወጸጋ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ በበዓለ ፡ ዛ ። ወሖረ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፤ ወተለአኮሙ ፡ ወሰበከ ፤ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ በስብከተ ፡፣ ወንጌል ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ማሕየዊ ። ወሜ ጦሙ ፡ ለብዙኃን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ መድኃኒት ፡ ወአብኦሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፣ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ወአጥመቆሙ ፡፥ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ፡ ወአድኃነ ፡ ነፍሳቲሆሙ ፤
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the holy preacher, the apostle 'Arestupäwlos (= Aristobulus). This saint was among the seventy-two disciples whom our Lord chose and (whom) he sent to preach before his passion. The Holy Spirit, the Paraclete—as well as the power and the grace—descended upon him (who was) with the apostles during the feast of Pentecost. He went with the apostles, served them, and preached with them the holy life-giving Gospel. He converted many (people) to the way of salvation, brought them into the faith of our Lord Christ,


[p. 382] {#po.46.p382}
በዘ ፡ ወሀቦሙ ፡ እምትእዛዛት ፡ መለኮታዊት ። ወሜምዎ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኤጴስ ፡ ቆጾሰ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አብራጣብያስ ። ወሖረ ፡ ኀቤሃ ፡ ወአብኦሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አእምሮ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወገብረ ፣ በቅድሜሆሙ ፡ ተአምራተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ። ወበጽሖ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ እምነ ፡ አይሁድ ፡ ወእምዮናናዊያን ፡ ወሰደድዎ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ወወገርዎ ፡ በአእባን ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ ሑረቶ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ። ወናሁ ፡ዘከሮ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ በመልእክቱ ፡ ለሰብአ ፡ ሮሜ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፤ ተዝካሮሙ ፡ ለሰማዕታት ፡ እለ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ እለእስክንድ ሮስ ፡ ግብጻዊ ፡ ወእለእስክንድሮስ" ፡ ወአጋብዮስ ፣ እምሀገረ ፡ ጋዛ ፡ ወኒሞላስ" ፡ እምሀገረ ፤ ቡጦስ!" ፡ ወዲዮናስዮስ ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ ጣራብልስ ፡ ወሮሜሎስ ፡ ወተላስዮስ ፡ እምአእጸዳተ ፡ ግብጽ ። እሉ ፡ ፪ቅዱሳን!፤! ፡ ተሰናአዉ ፡ በፍቅር፤። ፡ መሲሐዊት!! ። ወበጽሑ ፡ ኅበ ፡ መኩንነ፤ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ቂሳርያ ፡ ዘፍልስጥኤም ፡ ወተአምኑ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፡ ከሓዲ ፣ ወኩነኖሙ ፡ ዓቢየ ፣ ኩነኔ ። ወነሥኡ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሀ ረነ ፤ በጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
baptized them with Christian baptism, and saved their souls by giving them the divine commandments. The apostles consecrated this saint bishop of the city of 'Abrätäbyäs. He went there, brought them (= the inhabitants) into the knowledge of our Lord Christ, and performed many miracles before them. Great misfortunes befell him at the hands of the Jews and the Greeks: they drove him out many times and threw stones at him. When he had finished his beautiful career, he died in peace. Behold, the apostle Päwlos (= Paul) mentioned him in his epistle to the Romans. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen. On this day also, (there is) the commemoration of seven martyrs who are 'Ela'eskenderos (= Alexander) the Egyptian, 'Ela'eskenderos (= Alexander), 'Agäbyos (= Agapius) of the city of Gäzä (= Gaza), Nimoläs (= Timolaos) of the land of Butos (= Pontus), Diyonäsyos (= Dionysius) of the city of Täräbles (= Tripoli), Romëlos (= Romulus), and Taläsyos (= Valesius), (both) of cities of Egypt. These seven saints agreed in Christian love; they came before the governor of the city of Qisäryä of Felset'em (= Caesarea of Palestine) and confessed before him our Lord Christ during the time of Diyogletyänos (= Diocletian) the renegade. (The governor) subjected them to great torture and they received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. May God have mercy on us through their prayer, and may their

blessing be with us, amen. [On this day also died the saint 'Askanäfer, his wife Märtä (= Martha), and his sons 'Argädyos (= Arcadius) and Yohannes (= John). This 'Askanäfer had much money and wealth; he observed the law of God and he raised his sons in spiritual discipline. When they had grown up, he sent them to the city that is called Tayrut so that they might learn wisdom and discipline. When they had finished (their) instruction, he sent for them to be brought so that he might rejoice in a feast in their (honor). While they were traveling by sea, their ship broke. When God had saved them from drowning, the waves threw Yohannes into one place and 'Argädyos into (another) place. When he had lost hope, Yohannes went to a monastery of monks. He became a monk and destroyed his body with fasting and prayer because of his brother 'Argädyos, thinking that the latter was dead. As for 'Argädyos, while he was weeping because of the death of his brother whom he loved, while he was carrying the burden of grief on the shoulders of his heart and making his food the bread of grief, Yohannes appeared to him in a vision and told him that he was alive. 'Argädyos went to the Holy Places and entered the desert of Yohannes (= John), became a monk under a holy old man, and remained three years fighting (spiritually). When he learned of the death of his sons,

'Askanäfer put on a sack—he and his wife—and they spread ashes (on their heads). One night, 'Askanäfer saw Yohannes, a crown of jewels on his head and a cross in his hands. As for 'Argädyos, (he had) on his head a crown of the appearance of a star. Having risen, ('Askanäfer) spoke to his wife and said to her: "Come, let us go to the sanctuary to seek news of our sons." Having arrived (there), they prayed, went to the (desert of) Yordänos (= Jordan) in the monastery of Yohannes the Baptist, and met the old man who had made their son 'Argädyos a monk. They fell at his feet and told him their grief. He said to them: "Do not grieve, beloved of Christ: when you return to the sanctuary, you will find your children alive." They returned rejoicing. As for Yohannes, he came in order to perform an act of adoration for the feast of the Cross. The old man called Yohannes and made him reunite with 'Argädyos his brother, then they embraced and wept. The old man called 'Askanäfer again and made him meet his sons; 'Askanäfer and his wife wept over 'Argädyos and Yohannes while embracing them. 'Askanäfer put on the monastic garment under the old man and brought his wife Märtä into a monastery of women. He freed his slaves, distributed his goods to the poor, put on a hairshirt, left for the desert, and remained in fasting and prayer. His blessed children and his wife Marta (did) the same, (then)
died in peace. May their holy blessing be with their dear 'Atnätëwos for ever and ever, amen.]

In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the holy father abbä Mikä'ël (= Michael III), patriarch of the city of Alexandria; among the patriarch fathers, he is the fifty-sixth. This father was accomplished in every beautiful deed. Misfortunes and afflictions befell him during the time of his office; this (happened) thus: (there was) a bad bishop who was in charge of the city of Säha (= Sakha); he loved the honors of this transitory world. There was in the city of Danawsär (= Danouachir), which (was) in his diocese, a church whose construction had been restored and which they wanted to consecrate. The leaders of the city, who had restored this church, asked this bad bishop to send to the patriarch father and to the bishops of the districts that surrounded them (= the inhabitants of Danawsär) so that (the latter) might be blessed by them and (to) consecrate their church. The bad bishop did not want to send to the patriarch, and they sent on their own initiative to the patriarch father abbä Mikä'ël against the will of the bad bishop. (The latter) left them in the church and went out to take care of the banquet for them—he did not remember the

word of our Lord that he said in the Gospel: "Märtä (= Martha), Märtä, you tire yourself and prepare many (things) and little suffices." As the moment of the consecration had passed and the bad bishop had not arrived and was very late, the bishops and the elders asked this patriarch father to begin the consecration so that they might read the Scriptures until the bishop arrived. As they pressed him, this father (= the patriarch) rose with the ornaments of the patriarchate—he (was) the father of all and (had) power over them—and began the consecration. When the bad bishop learned this, Satan entered his heart; he rose, came to the church in fury, entered the sanctuary, took the host from the paten, broke it, threw it on the ground, and went out in fury. The patriarch gave an order, another host was brought, he consecrated it, and administered the holy Mysteries to the people. The next day, this father gathered the bishops who were with him, as well as the priests and the doctors, deposed the bad bishop from his office, and consecrated another bishop in his place. Satan entered him (= the bishop); he went to the governor of Mesr (= Egypt), slandered this patriarch father to him, and said to him: "Know that there is much wealth, gold, and silver at the patriarch's. Likewise, the churches (are) full of sacred vessels, gold, and silver." The governor had this saint brought and demanded from him all the sacred vessels of the churches. (The patriarch) refused him, did not give them to him, and said to him: "Behold, my body (is) before you, do to me what you want; as for my soul, (it is) in the hand of my creator." (The governor) gave an order and he was chained and imprisoned. He remained in prison for a year and more; during this lapse of time, he fasted constantly and ate only bread and salt. After that, a scribe, Christian and believer, by the name of Yohannes (= John), came to the governor, stood surety for the patriarch father, and made the latter come out of prison so that he might give the governor twenty thousand gold pieces. (The patriarch) went to his house and remained (there) a few days. Afterward, the leaders of the believers (and) the monks of the desert of 'Asqêtes (= Scetis) gathered among themselves ten thousand gold pieces. And there were the people of the city of Alexandria: they sold (each?) one of their lands for a thousand pieces until that made ten thousand pieces. The patriarch gave (this sum) to the governor, and this father wrote with his hand to the people of the city of Alexandria a letter (certifying) that he would give them a thousand pieces each year. As for the other ten thousand pieces, he wanted to ask (them) from the believers. He went out of his house to go to the (other) cities, arrived at the city of Balbäs (= Belbeis), and remained there one day. A poor monk, dressed in rags, entered before the patriarch father and was blessed by him. He sat beside his (= the

patriarch's) disciple and said to the disciple: "Tell the patriarch father: Do not grieve in your heart, for in forty days from today you will be saved, you will (take) back the bond that binds you, and you will give nothing to the governor." The disciple informed this father and told him what the monk had said. They searched for the monk, but they did not find him. Before the forty days were finished, the governor died a bad death. His son was appointed in his place; he returned the bond to this patriarch father (after) having torn it, as the monk had prophesied. After having remained in great misfortunes and sorrows during the time of his office—twenty-nine years—this father died in peace. May his prayer and his blessing be


[p. 388] {#po.46.p388}
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፣ አንሥኦ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ለጻድቅ ፡ አልዓዛር ፣፤ እምውታን ፡ ወእምድኅረ ፡ ሞቱ ፡ በ8መዓልት ። ወአምኑ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዕበያ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ትእምርት ። ወስብሐት ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ እስከ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
with us, amen. On this day also, our Lord raised 'Al'azär (= Lazarus) the righteous from the dead four days after the death of the latter. Many believed in him because of the greatness of this miracle. Glory to our Lord Jesus Christ for ever and ever, amen!
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት፡ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ጽጌ ፡ ሥላሴ ፡ መስተጋድልት ። ጸሉታ ፡ ወበረከታ አድኅኖ ፡ እመንሱት ፡ ለፍቁራ" ፡አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day, (there is) the holy Segë Selläsé, the fighter (spiritual). May her prayer and her blessing save from temptation her dear 'Atnätëwos for ever and ever, amen.]
'ወበዛቲ ፤ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ፥፤ ፡ ኮነት?" ፡ ስምዓ ፡ አስጠራጣኒቃ" ፣፡ ቅድስት ፡ ወክብርት ። ለዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ኮነ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ መምለኬ ፡ ጣዖት ፡ ወይእቲሰ ፡ ማእምንት ፡ በክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ትጸውም ፡ ወትጹሊ ፡ በኅቡእ ፡ እስከ ፡ ደግደገ ፡ ሥጋሃ ፡ ወተወለጠ ፡ አርአያፃሃ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮና ፡ ፲ ወ ፳ዓመት ፡ ይቤልዋ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ወእማ ፡ ምንት ፤ ያደገድገኪ ፤ ወትቤሎሙ ፡ እጺሊ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብ ሐት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሥረይ ፡ ኃጢአትየ ። ወሰሚዖ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ጢባርዮስ ፡ ወነገሮ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ወለቱ ። ወአብጽሕዋቶ፥፤ ፡ ወዓልት ፡ ኀበ ፡ መኩንን ። ወጣ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ከመ ፡ ትስግድ ፡ ለጣዖት ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ዐበየት ፡ ወደይዋ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ። ወእማሰ ፡ ክርስቲያናዊት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ትመጽእ ፡ ኀቤሃ ፡ ወታፀንዓ ፡ በሃይማኖተ ፡ ክርስ ቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ። ወእምዝ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ይግበሩ ፡ ሰፈሪደ?" ፡ ዘሐጺን ፡እለ፣ ርሱናት ፡ በእሳት ፡ ወያጹርዋ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩርናዓቲሃ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይትላጸቅ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ገበዋ ቲሃ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አኃዘት ፡ በእደዊሃ ፡ ውእተ ፡ ሰፈሪደ ፡ ወሰበረት ። ወርእዮሙ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ አምኑ ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ መጠነ ፡ ጣወ፪፻ ሰብእ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ ሰማዕተ ፡ ወካዕበ ፡ አብእዋ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ገብሩ ፡ሦረ ፡ ዘኃጺን ፡ ዘቦቱ ፡ አጽፋረ ፡ ወአቅረቡ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንድፍዋ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ከርሣ ፡ ወይስቅልዋ ። ወሶበ ፡ ገብሩ ፡ ላዕሌሃ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ተከፍለት ፡ ሦር ፡ ለዝየ ፡ ወለዝየ ፡ በኃይለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ።
[On this day also became a martyr the holy and glorious 'Astarätänigä (= Stratonice). The father of this saint worshipped idols, but she herself believed in Christ—glory to him! She fasted and prayed in secret so that her body was emaciated and her appearance changed. When she was fifteen years old, her father and mother said to her: "What is making you thin?" She said to them: "I am praying to our Lord Jesus Christ—glory to him!—so that he may forgive me my sins." Having heard (this), her father went to Tibäryos (= Tiberius) and told him the affair of his daughter; the guards brought her before the governor. The governor (sought to) seduce her so that she would worship the idols. As she refused, she was thrown into prison. Her mother was a Christian; she came to her and strengthened her in the faith of Christ—glory to him! Then the governor ordered a ball of iron to be made that (was) reddened in the fire and to make her carry it in her arms until it adhered to her sides. Then (the saint) took the ball in her hands and broke it. Seeing (this), the people, to the number of four thousand seven hundred, believed in our Lord Christ—glory to him!—and became martyrs; she was brought back to prison. Afterward, a bull of iron was made that had claws and they approached (her) to nail her into its belly and to hang her (there). When they did this to her, the bull split (and the two halves fell) on either side by the power of God. When the people, to the number of four hundred eighty-five, saw this, they believed in Christ—glory to

ወሶበ ፡ ርእዩ ፤ ሕዝብ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ አምኑ ፡ በክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ መጠነ ፡ 8፻ጅወ፳ነፍስ ፡ ወኮኑ0 ፡ ሰማዕተ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ወደይዋ ፡ ውስተ ፡ግበ ፡ አክይስት ፡ እለ ፡ ምሉኣን ፡ ኅምዘ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ጸለየት ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ከመ ፡ፀበል ። ወካዕበ ፡ አምጽኡ ፡እብነ፥ ዐቢየ ፡ ዘይጸውርዎ ፡ ዳፅደው ፡ ወሰቀሉ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ክሳዳ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ጸለየት ፡ ኮነ ፡ እብን ፡ ሾክፍል ። ወእለ ፡ ይርእዩ ፡ አምኑ ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ፲ወ8፪ዓረማውያን ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ ሰማዕተ ። ወለቅድስትሰ ፡ ገብሩ ፡ ላቲ ፡ መቅጹተ ፡ ዘሐጺን ፡ ዘፍሉሕ ፡፤ ምስለ ፡ አረር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይክዓዉ ፡ ላዕሌሃ ። ወሶበ ፡ ለከፍዎ ፡ ለመቅፁት ፡ ተሰብረ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ወቀተሎሙ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ይኬንንዋ ። ወተምዕዓ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወአዘዘ ከመ ፡ ይምትሩ፥0፥፤ ፡ ርእሳ ፡ በሰይፍ ፡ ወበጺሓ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካነ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ ጸለየት ፡ ኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወመጽአ ፡ ቃል ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ ዘይብል ፡ ብፅዕት ፡ አንቲ ፡ ኦአስጠራጣ ኒቃ፤ ፡ እስመ ፤ ተጽሕፈ ፡ ስምኪ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ሕይወት ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተከለለት ፡ በሰይፍ ። ጸሎታ ፡ ወበረከታ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁራ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፤
him!—and became martyrs. Afterward, she was thrown into a pit of snakes that (were) very venomous. When she prayed, they became like dust. A large stone was also brought that forty men carried and they hung (it) around her neck. When she prayed, the stone split into three. Those who saw (this)—fourteen pagans—believed in our Lord Jesus Christ—glory to him!—and became martyrs. As for the saint, a cauldron of iron was made for her that was boiling with lead to pour (the contents) on her. When those who were torturing her touched the cauldron, it broke on them and killed them. The governor became angry and ordered her head to be cut off with a sword. Arrived at the place of martyrdom, she prayed to God, and a voice came from heaven that said: "Happy (are) you, 'Astarätänigä, for your name has been written in the Book of life!" Then she was crowned (with martyrdom) by the sword. May her prayer and her blessing be with her dear 'Atnätëwos and also her dear Kotolikäwit for ever and ever, amen.]
ወፍቅርታሂ ፡ ኮቶሊካዊት ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ቅዳሴ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ዘቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፣ አስቀራን ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፣ ወበጸሎቶሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳኒሁ ፡ አሜን ።
On this day also, (there is) the consecration of the church of the saint abbä 'Asqarän. May God have mercy on us through his prayer and through the prayer of his saints, amen.

### READING FOR THE 21ST OF MAGGABIT
አመ ፡ ፳8ለመጋቢት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ተዝካራ ፣ ለእግዝእትነ፤ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ድንግል ፡ ማርያም ፡ ወላዲተ ፤ አምላክ ። ጸሎታ ፡ ወበረከታ ፡ ወትንብልናሃ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አርዳኢሁ ፡ ቢታንያ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ አልዓዛር ፡ ዘአንሥኦ ፡ እምውታን ፡ አሐዱ ፡ እምእለ ፡ ይረፍቁ ፡ ምስሌሁ ። ወኮነት ፡ ማርታ ፡ እኅቱ ፡ ትትለአኮሙ ፡ ለሕዝብ ፡እለ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወማርያ ፡ እኅቱ ፡ ካልእታ ፡ እንዘ" ፡ ትቀብእ ፡ እገሪሁ ፡ ለእግዚእነ" ፡ ዕፍረተ ፡ ወትመዘምዞሙ ፡ በሥዕርታ ። ወወደሳ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ወአእመረ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሞቱ ፡ ቅሩብ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ እስመ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ዐቀበቶ”" ፡ ለዕለተ ፡ ቀበርየ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እስመ ፡ ነዳያንሰ ፡ በኀቤክሙ ፡[ኩሎ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ኢሀለውኩ ፡ ም[ስሌክሙ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ያኤምር ፤ በዝ[ንቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ቅሩ]ብ ፡ ስቅለቱ ፤ ወሞቱ ። ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ለዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡፤ ካዕበ ፡ ተማከሩ ፡ ሊቃነ ፡ ካህናት ፥ ከመ ፡ ይቅትልዎ ፡ ለአ[ልዓዛር ፤ እስመ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ በእንተ ፡ ፅ[በየ ፡ ትእ]ምርት ፡ ኮኑ ፡ የአምኑ ፡ በእ[ግዚ]እነ ፤ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘ[ሎቱ ፡ ስብ]ሐት ፡ ለዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day, (there is) the commemoration of our Lady the holy Virgin Mary, mother of God. May her prayer, her blessing, and her intercession be with us, amen. On this day also, our Lord Jesus Christ came with his disciples to Bitanyä (= Bethany) where was 'Al'azar (= Lazarus) whom he had raised from the dead; (it was) one of those who were at table with him. Märta (= Martha) his sister was serving the people who were with him, and Märyä (= Mary), his other sister, was anointing the feet of our Lord with perfume and wiping them with her hair. Our Lord praised her and made an allusion regarding his coming death; he said: "She has kept it for the day of my burial." He said also: "The poor (are) with you all the time, but me, I am not with you all the time." He was indicating by that that close (were) his crucifixion and his death—glory to him for ever, amen! On this day also, the leaders of the priests deliberated to kill 'Al'azär (= Lazarus), for many, because of the greatness of the miracle, believed in our Lord Jesus Christ to whom is due the glory for ever, amen.


[p. 392] {#po.46.p392}
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለላሜኅ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ማቱሳላ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ሐሙስ ። ወሐይወ ፡ ፻ወዉወቷ ዓመተ ፡ ወወለዶ ፡ ለኖኅ ፣ ወኮነ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ መዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለላሜኅ ፡ ፲፻ ዛወ፻ጀዓመት ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐሮ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ለፍቁሩ ፤ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፤ ተዝካሮሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳን ፡ ሰማዕታት ፡ ቴዎድሮስ ፡ ወጢሞቴዎስ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቶሙ ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day also, (there is) the commemoration of Lämëh (= Lamech), son of Mätusäläà (= Methuselah), on Thursday. He lived one hundred thirty-eight years and begot Noh (= Noah). All the days of Lämëh were seven hundred fifty-three years. May God have mercy through his prayer on his dear 'Atnätéwos for ever and ever, amen.] On this day also, (there is) the commemoration of the saints martyrs Téwodros (= Theodore) and Timotéwos (= Timothy). May God have mercy on us through their prayer, amen.

### READING FOR THE 22ND OF MAGGABIT
አመ ፡ ፳፪ለመጋቢት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ፅለት ፡ ቦአ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወዖደ ፡ ሆሣ እና ፤ ከመ ፡ ይትፈጸም ፡ ትንቢተ ፡ ነቢያት ፡ ዘተነበዩ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ለለ ፤ 88በበዘመኖሙ ፡ይቤ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ዘተመሰለ፤ ፡ በክርስቶስ ፡ የአስር ፡ በዕፀ ፡ ዘይት ፡ አድጎ ፤ ወበኃረገ ፡ ወይን ፡ ዕዋሎ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዘካርያስ ፡ ኢትፍርሂ፡ ፡ ወለተ ፡ ጽዮን ፡ ነዋ ፡ ንጉሥኪ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ ወይጹዓን ፡ ዲበ ፡ ዕዋለ አድግ ። ወኢሳይያስኒ ፡ ይቤ ፤ ይመጽእ ፣ ንጉሥኪ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ ዘዕሜቱ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወምግባሩ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ገጹ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጹዓን ፡ ዲበ ፡ ዕዋለ ፡ አድግ ። ወአብርሃምኒ ፡ ሰመያ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ በዓል ፡ በዐለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ፍሥሐ ፡ አመ ፡ ዖደ ፡ ምሥዋዓ ፡ ነሚኦ፤ ፀበርተ ፡ ተመርት ፡ ወበቀልት ። ወዳዊት ፡ ይቤ ፡ እምአፈ ፡ ደቂቅ ፡ ወሕፃናት ፡ አስተደሎከ ፡ ስብሐተ ። ወሰሎሞንኒ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ልሳናቲሆሙ ፡ ለሕፃናት ፡ ረሰየት ፡ ርትዕተ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ወይትዋነዩ ፡ በመርኅባ ፡ ለኢየሩሳሌም ፡ አፅሩግ ፡ ወሕፃናት ፡ ወኩሎሙ ፡፤ ምርጉዞሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እደዊሆሙ ፡ ወቦዘይቤ ፡ አርኅቢ ፤ መታክለኪ ፡ ወአንጦልዒ ፡ ደብተራኪ ። ወለፈጽሞ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ነገር ፡ ቦአ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ተፅዒኖ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ዕድግት ፡ ወዲበ ፡ ዕዋላ ፡ በከመ ፡ ጽሑፍ ፡ በወንጌል ፡ ወቀሪቦ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ መንገለ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ፋጌ፣፤ ወቢታንያ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደብር ፡፤ ዘስሙ ፡ ኤልዮን ፡ ፈነወ ፡ ክልኤተ ፡ እምአርዳኢሁ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ሑሩ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ዘቅድሜክሙ ፡ ወበዊአክሙ ፡ ትረክቡ ፡ ዕዋለ ፡ እሱረ ፡ ዘኢተፅዕኖ ፡ ሰብእ ፤ ፍትሕዎ ፣ ወአምጽእዎ ። ወእመቦ ፡ ዘይቤለክሙ ፡ 'ለምንት ፡ ትፈትሕዎ ፡ በሉ ፣፤ እግዚኡ ፡ ይፈቅዶ ። ወሐዊሮሙ ፡ እለ ፡ ተፈነዉ ፡ ረከቡ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. [On this day, our Lord Jesus Christ entered Jerusalem and (instituted) the procession (of the feast) of the Palms so that the prophecy of the prophets who prophesied each in his time might be fulfilled. Yä'qob (= Jacob) said regarding Yehudä (= Judah) his son who is compared to Christ: He ties his donkey to the olive tree and his colt to the vine. Zakäryäs (= Zechariah) also said: "Do not be afraid, daughter of Seyon (= Zion), behold your king comes, he is mounted on a young donkey." 'Isayeyäs (= Isaiah) also said: "Your righteous king is arriving, who (has) his reward with him and whose deeds (are) before him while he is mounted on a young donkey." 'Abrehäm (= Abraham) also called this feast "feast of the God (of) joy" when he went around the altar carrying branches of bushy trees and palms. Däwit (= David) said: "From the mouth of children and infants you have prepared glory." Solomon also said: "The tongues of infants institute what is right." It is said also: On the squares of Jerusalem play the old men—and the infants—all with their cane in hand. And there is one who says: "Give space to your stakes and extend your tent." To fulfill this purpose, Jesus entered Jerusalem, mounted on a donkey and a colt, just as it (is) written in the Gospel: Having approached Jerusalem by Bëta fägë (= Bethphage) and Bitänyä (= Bethany) near the mount whose name (is) 'Élyon (= Olivet), he sent two of his disciples and said to them: "Go to the city that (is) before you and, (having) entered (it), you will find a colt tied that (no) man has mounted. Untie it and bring it.
በከመ ፡ ይቤሎሙ ፡ ወፈትሑ ፡ ዕዋለ ። ወእንዘ ፡ ይፈትሑ ፡ ዕዋለ ፡ ይቤልዎሙ ፤ አጋእዝቲሁ ፡ ለምንት ፤ ትፈትሕዎ ፡ ወይቤሉ ፡ እግዚኡ ፡ ይፈቅዶ ። ወነሥኡ ፡ ወወሰድዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ወረኃኑ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ አልባሲሆሙ ፡ ወአጽዓንዎ ፡ ወብዙኃን ፡እለ ፡ ነጸፉ ፡ ቄጽለ ፡ መቲሮሙ ፡ ወቦ ፡እለ ፡ ነጸፉ ፡ አልባሲሆሙ ፡ውስተ፣ ፍኖት ፤ እለሂ ፣ ይመርሑ ፡ ወአለሂ ፡ ይተልዉ ፡ ወይኬልሑ ፡ ወይብሉ ፡ ሆሣፅና ፣ በአርያም ፡ ቡሩክ ፡ ዘይመጽእ ፡ በስመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወቡርክት ፡ እንተ ፡ ትመጽእ ፡ መንግሥት ፡ በስመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እንተ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ሆሣዕና ፡ በአርያም ። ወቦአ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ቤተ ፡ መቅደስ ፡ ወርእዮ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ሶበ ፡ መስየ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ቢታንያ ፡ ምስለ ፡፲ ወ፪ አርዳኢሁ ፤ ወበሳኒታ ፡ ወዒኦ! ፡ እምቢታንያ ፡ ርኅበ ፡ ወርእየ ፡ በለሰ ፡ እምርጐቅ ፡ ወባቲ ፡ ቄጽለ፤፤ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ይርአይ ፡ 'እመ ፡ ይትረከብ፤፤ ፡ ፍሬ ፡ በውስቴታ ። ወበጺሖ ፡ ኅቤሃ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘረከበ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ቄጽል" ፡ ባሕቲታ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡ ጊዜሁ ፡ ለበለስ ። ወአውሥአ ፡ ወይቤላ ፡ ለዓለም፤ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘይበልዕ ፡ ፍሬ ፥፡ እምኔኪ ፡ እንከ ፡ ለዓለም ። ወሰምዕዎ ፡ አርዳ ኢሁ ። ወበጺሖ፤፤ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወቦአ ፡ ቤተ ፡ መቅደስ ፡ ወአኃዘ ፡ ይስድድ ፡ እለ ፡ ይሠይጡ ፤ ወይሣየጡ ፤ በምኩራብ ፡ ወገፍትዓ ፣፡ ማዕዳቲሆሙ ፡ ለመወልጣን ፡ ወመናብርቲሆሙ ፡ ለእለ ፤ ይሠይጡ ፡ ርግበ ፡ ወከልአ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘየኃልፍ ፡ ንዋየ ፡፤ እንተ ፡ ምኩራብ ፤ ወመሀሮሙ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ አኮኑ ፡ ጽሑፍ ፡ ዘይብል ፡ ቤትየሰ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ጸሎት ፡ ይከውን፤ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ወአንትሙሰ ፡ ረሰይክምዎ ፡ በዐተ ፡ ፈያት ።
If someone says to you: Why are you untying it? say: The Lord needs it." (Having) gone, those who had been sent found (things) as he had told them, and they untied the colt. While they were untying the colt, its masters said to them: "Why are you untying it?" They said: "The Lord needs it." And they took (it), brought it to the Lord Jesus, spread their garments on it, and made him (= Jesus) mount. Many (were) those who spread foliage, (having) cut (it). Others spread their garments on the road. Those who preceded and those who followed were shouting and saying: "Hosanna in the highest of heavens! Blessed (be) he who comes in the name of God and blessed the kingdom that comes in the name of God, that of our father Däwit! Hosanna in the highest of heavens!" And the Lord Jesus entered Jerusalem into the Temple and, having seen everything, as it was evening, he went to Bitänyä with his twelve disciples. The next day, having gone out of Bitänyä, he was hungry; he saw from afar a fig tree (that) had leaves and went to see if there were fruits on it. Arrived near it, he found nothing but leaves, for it was not the time for figs. He spoke and said to it: "For ever, no one will eat fruit from you, from now on." His disciples heard him. Arrived at Jerusalem, he entered the Temple and began to drive out those who were selling and those who were buying in the Temple. He overturned the tables of the money changers and the seats of those who were selling doves. He forbade anyone to carry an object through the Temple. He taught them and said to them: "Is it not written (the statement) that says: My house will be a house of prayer for all nations, but you, you have made it a den of robbers!"

ወዮሐንስሰ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ አንሥኦ ፡ ለአልዓዛር ፡ ወበሳኒታ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ እለ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ለበዓል ፡ ሶበ ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ነሥኡ ፡ ፀበርተ ፡ ተመርት ፡ ዘበቀልት ፡ እምኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወተቀበልዎ ፡፤ ወኦሙ ፡ ይኬልሑ ፡ ወይብሉ ፡ ሆሳዕና ፡ በአርያም ፡ ቡሩክ ፡ ዘይመጽእ ፡ በስመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፤ ወንጉሦሙ! ፡ ለእስራኤል ። ወረከበ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ዕዋለ ፡ አድግ ፡ ወተፅዕነ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ በከመ ፡ ጽሑፍ ፡ ኢትፍርሂ ፡ ወለተ ፡ ጽዮን ፡ ነዋ ፡ ንጉሥኪ ፤ ይመጽእ ፡ ወይጹዓን ፡ ዲበ ፡ ዕዋለ ፡ አድግ ። ወዘንተሰ ፣፤ ኢያእመሩ ፡ አርዳኢሁ ፡ እምትካት ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ አመ ፡ ተሰብሐ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ አሜሃ ፡ ተዘከሩ ፡ አርዳኢሁ ፡ ከመ፤ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ጽሑፍ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ነገር ፡ ወከመዝ ፡ ገብሩ ፡ ሎቱ ። ወሰማዕተ ፡ ኮንዎ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ እለ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ ህየ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡፣ ጸውዖ ፡ ለአልዓዛር ፤ እመቃብር ፡ ወአንሥኦ ፡ እምነ ፡ ምውታን ። በእንተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡፤ ተቀበልዎ ፡፤ እሙንቱ ፡ ሰብእ ፤ ሰሚዖሙ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ዘገ ብረ ፤ ተአምራተ ፡ ወዘንተሂ ፡ ተአምረ ። ወይቤሉ ፡ ፈሪሳዊያን ፣ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡፣ ትሬእዩኑ እንከ ፡ ከመ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘትበቀ-ዑ?" ፡ ናሁ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ተለዎ ፡ ድኅሬሁ።
Yohannes (= John) says after (Jesus) had raised 'Al'azär: The next day, the many people who had come for the feast, when they learned that the Lord Jesus Christ was coming to Jerusalem, took branches of the palms of Jerusalem and welcomed him, having gone out. They were shouting and saying: "Hosanna in the highest of heavens! Blessed (be) he who comes in the name of God, and the king of Israel." The Lord Jesus found a young donkey and mounted it, as it is written: "Do not be afraid, daughter of Zion; behold your king comes, he is mounted on a young donkey." This—all this—his disciples did not understand (it) at first; but when the Lord Jesus had been glorified, then his disciples remembered that this word (had been) written thus about him and (that) thus they had done for him. The people who were there with him were witnesses that he had called 'Al'azär out of the tomb and had raised him from the dead. Because of that, the people welcomed him, having learned thus that he had performed miracles and also this miracle. The Pharisees said among themselves: "Do you see then that you gain nothing? Behold, all the world has gone after him."

እስመ ፡ ማቴዎስ ፡ ወማርቆስ ፡ ኢዘከሩ ፡ ግብራ ፡ ለተመርት ፡ አላ ፡ ይቤሉ ፡ ወካልኣን ፡ ይመትሩ ፡ አዕፁቀ ፤ እምውስትተ ፡ ዕፀው ፡ ወይነጽፉ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፍኖት ። ወሉቃስ ፡ ኢዘከረ ፡ ኢአዕፁቀ ፡ ወኢተመርተ ፡ አላ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ወእንዘ ፡ የሐውሩ ፡ ይነጽፉ ፡ አልባሲሆሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፍኖት ። ወዮሐንስ ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፡ ይቤ ፤ ነሥኡ ፡ ፀበርተ ፤ ተመ ርት ፡ዘበቀልት ፡ እምኢየሩሳሌም ፡ እስመ ፡ በቀልትሰ ፡ ኢሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም አላ ፡ አመ ፡ ሕፃን ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ወረደ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ምስለ ፡ እሙ፣ ቅድስት ፡ ማርያም ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ምድረ ፡ አስሞኒን ፡ ወረከቡ ፡ በህየ ፡ ተመርተ ። ወአዘዘ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትትመላህ ፡ እምሥረዊሃ ፡ ወትተከል ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ደብረ! ፡ ዘይት ፡ አሜሃ ፡ ሰረረት ፤ ውስተ ፡ አየር ፡ ወተተክለት ፡ ህየ ፡ ወእምኔሃ ፡ ነሥኡ። ፡ አቀሩ ጽለ ፡ ወተቀበልዎ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ አመ ፡ አርአየ ፡ ሁደተ ፡ ሆሣ ዕና ። ወእምአሜሃ ፡ አኃዞሙ ፡ ቅንዓት ፡ ለሕዝበ ፤ አይሁድ ፡ ወኃሠጮሠ ፡ በዘ ፡ ይቅት ልዎ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ሣህሉ ፡ ወምሕረቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ገብሩ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፤ ወእመቱሂ ፡ ኮቶሊካዊት ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
Matëwos (= Matthew) nor Märgos (= Mark) mention the affair (of the branches) of palms, but they say: And others cut branches from the trees and spread them on the road. Lugäs (= Luke) does not mention the branches nor the (branches of) palms, but he says: While they were advancing, they were spreading their garments on the road. Only Yohannes says: They took branches of the palms of Jerusalem: in fact, there were no (formerly) palms in Jerusalem. But when our Lord was a small child, when he descended into the land of Egypt with his mother, the holy Mary, they arrived in the region of 'Asmonin (= Ashmunein) and found palms there. Our Lord ordered that from their roots they be pulled up and be planted on the Mount of Olives. Immediately, (the roots) flew into the air and were planted there. It is from them that (the people) took branches, and (with these) that they welcomed our Lord Jesus Christ when he instituted the procession (of the feast) of the Palms. From that moment, jealousy seized the Jews and they sought by what (means) to kill our Lord Jesus Christ. May his mercy and his pity be with his servant 'Atnätéwos and also his servant Kotolikäwit for ever and
በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ መንፈሳዊ” ፡ አባ ፡ ቄርሎስ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ልህቀ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ትምህርተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፤ ወኮነ ፡ መምህረ ፡ ወማእምረ ፡ ፈድፋደ ። ወሶበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ መክሲሞስ ፡ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ኅኀረይዎ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ወሜ[ምዎ ፡ ኤ]ጴስ ፡ ቆጳሰ ፡ እምድኅሬሁ ። ወዓቀበ ፡ ሕዝቦ ፡ በሠናይ ፡ ተዓቅቦ ፡ ወዓቀቦሙ ፡ እም[ተኩላት ፡ መናንያዊያን ፡ ወአርዮሳዊ[ያን ። ወሶበ ፤ ተጋብኡ ፡ 'ማኅበረ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆኦሳት ፡ በሰርድቄ፤' ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፤ ወተቃረኖሙ ፡ ለሕዝበ ፡ አርዮስ ፡ ወሞኦሙ ፡ ወአውገዞሙ ፡ ወሰደዶሙ ፡ ወሰደዶ ፡ ለአካክዮስ ፡ እመንበረ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ቂሳርያ ። ወሖረ ፡ አካክዮስ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ቁስጠንጢኖስ ፤ ንጉሥ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ቄስጠንጥንዮስ ፡ ዘየዓቢ ። ወሰከየ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ዘበጽሐ ፡፤ ላዕሌሁ ፡ እምነ ፡ ማኅበር ፡ ወፈድፋደሰ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ቄርሎስ ። ወፈነወ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወሰደዶ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ቄርሎስ ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወለብዙኃን ፡ ኤሏኢስ ፡ ቆፅሳት ፤ ሰደዶሙ ፣ እመናብርቲሆሙ ። ወሖረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ተርሴስ ፡ ወተጋብአ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ስልዋኖስ ፡ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆጾሰ፣ ዚአሃ ፡ ወተራድኦ ፡ ወአንበሮ ፡ በኀቤሁ ፡ ሕዳተ ፡ መዋዕለ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተጋብኡ ፡ ማኅበር ፡ በሱልክያ"" ፡ ኮነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ እምእለ ፡ ጉቡኣን ፣ ውስቴቱ ፡ ወረገምዎ ፡ ለአካክዮስ ። ወሖረ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አካክዮስ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወነገሮ-፥፤ ፡ ዘበጽሐ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወአስተዋደዮ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ?" ፡ ቄርሎስ ፡ በኀቤሁ?" ፡ ወፈነወ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ወሰደዶ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሞተ ፡ ቄስጠንጢኖስ ፡ 'ወነግሠ ፡ ወልዱ፥ጎ ፡ ቄስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ወፈነወ ፡
ever, amen.] On this day died the holy (and) spiritual father abbä Qérellos (= Cyril), bishop of the city of Jerusalem. This father grew up in the teaching of the Church and became a doctor and very learned. When died the father Maksimos (= Maximus), the bishop of Jerusalem, they chose this father and named him bishop after him. He guarded his flock with a beautiful guard and preserved it from the Manichaean and Arian wolves. When the council of the bishops gathered at Sardegë (= Sardica), this father went (there), fought the Arians, defeated them, cursed them, and drove them out, and he drove 'Akäkyos (= Acacius) from the see of the city of Qisäryä (= Caesarea of Palestine). 'Akäkyos went to Quastantinos (= Constantius) the emperor, son of Quastantenyos (= Constantine) the Great, and incriminated before him what had happened to him on the part of the council and particularly on the part of this saint Qérellos. The emperor sent and drove this saint Qérellos out of the city of Jerusalem, and he drove many bishops from their sees. This saint went to the city of Tarsës (= Tarsus) and met with Selwänos (= Silvanus), bishop of that city; (the latter) helped him and made him stay with him some time. When a council gathered at Sulkeyä (= Seleucia), this father was one of those who (were) gathered there. They cursed 'Akäkyos. 'Akäkyos went again to the emperor, told him what had happened to him, and slandered this father Qérellos to him.


[p. 398] {#po.46.p398}
ወሜቦ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ እምስደት ፡ ኅበ ፡ መንበሩ ፡ ወሜጦሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆኦሳት ፡ ዘሰደዶሙ ፡ አቡሁ ። ወሐይወ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ በህድአት ፡ ወሰላም ፡ ዘተ ረፈ ፡ አመ]ዋዕሊሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ ነግሠ ፡ ቴዮዶስዮስ ፡ ዘየዓቢ ፡ ወአስተጋብአ ፡ ማኅበረ ፡ ፻ወ፻0በሀገረ ፤ ቆቀስጥንጥንያ ፡ በእንተ ፣ መቅዶንዮስ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፤ "ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ወተቃወሞሙ ፡ ለመቅይንዮስ ፡ ወሰብልዮስ ፣፤ ወአቡሊናርዮስ?፥' ፡ ወረገሞሙ ፡፤ ወአውገዞሙ ፡ ወለኩሎሙ> ፡ እለ ፡ ይተልውዎሙ ። ወወሰከ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ጸሎተ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡፤ ዘሠርዕዋ ፡ አበው ፡ ፻፻፲ ወ፳ቿ ፡ እምኀበ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ወነአምን ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እስከ ፡ ተፍጻሜታ ። ወደረሰ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ድርሳናተ ፤ ወተግሣጸጻተ ፡፤ ብዙኃተ ። ወደረሰ ፡ ካልአ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡፤ ዘውስቴቱ' ፡ ፲ወቿድርሳናት ፡ በእንተ ፡ ትርጓሜ ፡ ጸሎተ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ መጽሐፍ ፡ በቋዒ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ዘምሉእ4፥፤ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ጥበብ ። ወነበረ ፡ ዲበ ፡ መንበረ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅስና ፡ ቋ'ወ፻ዓመተ ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
(The emperor) sent again and drove the latter out. When Quastantinos died, his son Quastantinos reigned. He sent and made this father return from exile to his see, and he made all the bishops return whom his father had driven out. This father lived in tranquility and peace the rest of his days. When Tëyodosyos (= Theodosius) the Great reigned, he gathered the council of the one hundred and fifty in the city of Questentenyä (= Constantinople) regarding Magdonyos (= Macedonius). This father went (there) among them, fought Magdonyos, Sabelyos (= Sabellius), and 'Abulinäryos (= Apollinaris), cursed them, and excommunicated them as well as all those who followed them. He added to the Prayer of the faith (= the creed) that the three hundred and eighteen fathers (of Nicaea) had established (the words going) from where it is said "we believe in the Holy Spirit" until the end. This father composed by the Holy Spirit many homilies and exhortations. He composed another book in which (there are) eighteen homilies on the explanation of the Prayer of the faith. This book which (is) filled with all wisdom (is) very useful. He remained on the episcopal see thirty-three years and died in peace. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.

### READING FOR THE 23RD OF MAGGABIT
አመ ፡ ፳፻ለመጋቢት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ዳንኤል ። ዝንቱ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ ኮነ ፤ ወልደ ወለተ ፡ ዮናኪር! ። ወሶበ ፡ ፄወዋ ፡ ናቡክድናጾር ፡ ለኢየሩሳሌም ፡ አመ ፡ ፲ወ፪ዓመተ ፡ መንግሥቱ ፡፤ ነሥኦ ፡ ለዮናኪር ፡ ወልደ ፤ ኢዮአቄም ፡ ወለ፻ደቂቁ ፡ ወዳንኤል፡፣ ወልደ ፡ ወለቱ ። ወፄወወ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ብዙኃነ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ እምደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወዘውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡፤ በከመ ፡ ተነበየ ፡ ኢሳይያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ለሕዝቅያስ ፡ ንጉሥ ። ወኮነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ዳንኤል ፡ ንኡሰ ፡ መዋፅሊሁ ፡ ወተጋደለ ፡ ተጋድሎ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ፍጹመ ። ወወረደ ፡ ላዕ ሌሁ ፡ መንፈሰ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወተነበየ ፡ በሀገረ ፡ ባቢሎን ። ወበራብዕ ፡ ዓመት ፡ እምፄዋዌ' ፡ ርእየ ፤ ናቡክድናጾር ፡፣ ራእየ ፡ ግርምት ፡ በአርአያሃ ፡ ወበትርጓሜሃ ፡ ወደ[ንገፀ ፡ ፈድፋደ ። ወሶበ ፡ ነቅሃ ፡ እም[ንዋሙ ፡ ረስዓ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ራእይ ፡ ወአ[ስተጋብኦሙ ፡ ለሹሎሙ ፡ ጠቢባነ ፡ ባቢሎን ፡ ወኀሠ]ሠ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ከ[መ ፡ ይንግርዎ ፡ ይ]እተ ፡ ራእየ ፡፤ ወትርጓሜሃ ። ወኢያአ[መሩ ፡ ይእተ ፡ ራ]አየ ፡ ወኢትርጓሜሃ ፡ ወአዘ[ዘ ፤ ከመ ፡ ይቅትልዎሙ ፣፤ ለኩሎሙ ። ወ[ሶበ ፡ ሰ]ምዓ ፡ ዳንኤል ፡፤ ከመ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቅትልዎሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ጠቢ]ባነ ፡ ባቢሎን ፡'ወሎቱ ፡ካዕበ" ፡ ይቅትል[ዎ ፤ ወለአብያጺሑኒ ፡ ፻ደቂቅ ፡ ወሐተቶ ፡ዳን ኤል ፡ ለሊቀ ፡ መገብት ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሕልም ፡ ዘርእየ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ እስመ ፣ ኢተክህሎሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ጠቢባ!ነ ፡ ባቢሎን ፡ ወግብጽ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይተርጐምዎ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the great prophet Dän'ël (= Daniel). This righteous man was the son of the daughter of Yonäkir. When Näbukednäsor (= Nebuchadnezzar) seized Jerusalem the seventeenth year of his reign, he took Yonäkir son of 'Iyo'agëm (= Jehoiakim), his three sons, and Dän'el, the son of his daughter. He took captive with them many people among the children of Israel. He took all the wealth that (was) in the house of the king and in the house of God, as 'Isäyeyäs (= Isaiah) the prophet had prophesied to Hezgeyäs (= Hezekiah) the king. This prophet Dän'ël was young and led a great (and) accomplished (spiritual) combat. The spirit of God descended upon him and he prophesied in the city of Bäbilon (= Babylon). The fourth year of the captivity, Nabukednäsor saw a frightening vision, in its appearance and in its interpretation, and he trembled greatly. When he awoke from his sleep, he had forgotten this vision. He gathered all the wise men of Bäbilon and asked them to tell him the vision and (to) (give) the explanation. They could not know either the vision or its explanation, and he ordered them all to be killed. When Dän'ël learned that the king had ordered the wise men of Bäbilon to be killed and to kill him also [as well as his companions the three children, Dän'ël (therefore)

ወይፈክሩ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ኪያሁ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ዳንኤል ፡ ለሊቀ ፡ መገብት ፡ ንግሮ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ በእንቲአየ ፡ ከመ ፡ አነ ፡ እፌክር፤ ፡ ወእተረጉም ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ውእተ ፡ ራእየ ፡ ዘርእየ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ጸለየ ፡ ዳንኤል ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወ፻ደቂቅ ፡ አብያጺሁ ፡ ምስሌሁ ። ወአስተብቀዓ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይክሥት ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ራእዮ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ወትርጓሜሁኒ ፤ ወከሠተ ፡፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለዳንኤል ፡ ራእዮ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ወትርጓሜሁኒ ። ወቦአ ፡ ዳን ኤል ፡ኀበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወነገሮ ፡ ሕልሞ ፡ ወትርጓሜሁ ። ወከሠተ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ፣ በእንተ ፤ ነገሥት ፡ እለ ፤ ይነግሥ ፣ እምድኅሬሁ ፡ ወዘይከውን ፡ እምነ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ለለ፳6እምኔሆሙ ። ወአደሞ ፡ ለናቡከደነጾር ፡ ቃሉ ፡ ለዳንኤል ፡ ወሰገደ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወወሀቦ ፡ አምኃ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ወጸገዎ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ ወሜሞ ፡ ርእሰ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ጠቢባነ ፡ ባቢሎን ። ወእምዝ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ናቡከደነጾር ፥ ንጉሥ ፡ ካልአ ፡ ሕልመ ፡ ወተርጐመ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ዳንኤል ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ወአጠየቆ ፡ ወይቤሎ፤፤ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ትዕቢትከ፤፡ ፡ ዘሀሎከ!" ፡ ያወፅአከ ፡ እማእከለ ፣ ዘመድከ! ፡ እጓለ ፡ እመሕያው ፡ ወየኃድረከ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አራዊት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ዓመተ ፡ ትበልዕ ፡ ሣዕረ ፡ ምስለ'፤" ፡ እንስሳ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ይመይጠከ፤ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መንግሥትከ ። ወተፈጸመ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ትንቢቱ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ይቤሎ ።
questioned the steward; (the latter) said to him: "It is because of the dream that the king saw: none of the wise men of Bäbilon and of Egypt could explain it nor interpret it for him." Dän'ël said to the steward: "Speak of me to the king (saying) that I, I will interpret and explain to him the vision that he saw." Afterward, Dän'ël prayed to God, the three children his companions with him. He begged him to discover for him the vision of the king and its explanation; God discovered to Dän'ël the vision of the king and its explanation. Dän'ël entered before the king and told him his dream and its explanation. He discovered to him that it concerned the kings who would reign after him and what would become of each of them. The word of Dän'ël pleased Näbukadanasor; he prostrated himself before him, gave him many gifts, did him great favors, and established him chief of all the wise men of Bäbilon. After that, the king Näbukadanasor saw another dream. Dän'ël also explained (it) to him, made him understand (it), and said to him: "It is because of your pride that (God) is going to make you go out from the midst of your species, the human race, and live with the wild beasts in the desert seven years: you will eat grass with the animals. After that, he will bring you back to your royalty." The prophecy regarding him was fulfilled as (Dän'ël) had told him.

After the death of Näbukadanasor, he explained to Beltäsor (= Belshazzar) his son what the angel had written for him on the wall while he was drinking from the sacred vessels of the house of God. He taught him that what (was) written (was) "counted, measured, weighed." As for the explanation, (it was): "God has prepared your throne and your kingdom for another, for he has weighed you with a balance and found you insufficient. He will transfer your royalty to the royalty of the Mähi (= Medes) and to the royalty of Färs (= Persia)." His prophecy was fulfilled. God made him see in a vision all the kings who would reign until the end of the world and the appearance of the false Messiah. He also saw the glory of God and his greatness, and he saw the brightness of our Lord Christ and his divinity. He prophesied regarding his coming and attributed to it (a number) of years known. When these years were fulfilled, our Lord Christ—glory to him!—came as he (had) prophesied. He said: "Christ—glory to him!—will come and they will kill him. Jerusalem will not have a savior after him, for, after the crucifixion of our Lord Christ—glory to him!—, Jerusalem and the Temple will be ruined and the offerings and the sacrifices will be suppressed." All this was fulfilled as he (had) prophesied.
ወሀሎ ፡ በሀገረ ፡ ባቢሎን ፡ ጣዖት ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ቤል ። ወኮኑ ፡ ይሁብዎ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ አሚረ፣ ፲ወ፪መስፈርተ ፡ ሐሪጸ ፡ ስንዳሌ ፡ ወ ጓአባግዓ ፤ ወሄመሳብክተ ፡ ወይን ። ወኮነ ፡ ኮሮስ" ፡ ይሰግድ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ አሚረ ። ወኮነ ፡ ይሔሊ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ጣዖት ፡ ይበልዕ ፡ ወይሰቲ ፤ ዘይሁብዎ ፡ እምቤተ ፡ ንጉሥ ። ወገሠጾ ፡ ዳንኤል ፡፤ ለንጉሥ ፡ በእንተዝ ፡ ወይቤሎ"፤ ፡ እስመ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ጣዖት ፡ ኢይበልዕ ፡ ወኢይሰቲ ። ወተምዕዓ ፤
There was in the city of Bäbilon an idol by the name of Bël. They gave it every day twelve measures of fine flour, forty sheep, and six amphorae of wine. Koros (= Cyrus) prostrated himself before it every day. He thought that the idol ate and drank what they gave it from the house of the king. Dän'ël rebuked the king because of that and said to him: "This idol neither eats nor

drinks." The king was angered and questioned the priests about the idol Bel; he said to them: "Is it true that Bel neither eats nor drinks?" The priests said to him: "In truth, certainly, the idol Bel eats and drinks what we give him every day." Then they placed all the (usual) food and drink near the idol while the king watched (the scene) at the time of evening, and the priests went out. After the priests had gone, Dän'ël ordered that ashes be brought. They brought him some and he scattered them in the temple, the king watching with his own eyes. Then the king sealed it (= the temple) with his seal, together with Dän'ël, and they went to their dwelling. As for the priests of the idol, they had a (passage) dug under the earth. They entered through there, took the food and drink from before the idol, and went away. The next day, the king opened the temple and did not find the food and drink that had been deposited. The king cried out, saying: "You are great, Bel, (you) who have put Dän'ël to shame!" Dän'ël laughed at the king and showed him the footprints of men on the ashes. Before that, the king had said to Dän'ël: "Why do you not worship Bel, my god?" And Dän'ël had said to him: "I do not worship idols, but I worship the living God who created heaven and earth." Because of this, Dän'ël revealed to the king that the idol Bel neither ate nor drank, and he showed him the trace of the priests' feet on the ashes: Dän'ël said to the king: "Look, O king, on the ground (at the trace) of the feet of women, men, and adolescents." The king then became angry and (had) the priests of the gods, their wives, and their children killed. He gave the idol Bel to Dän'ël; Dän'ël broke it and tore down the temple.
ቤልሃ ፡ ጣዖተ ፡ ለዳንኤል ፡ ወሰበሮ ፡ ዳንኤል ፡ ወአንሀለ ፡ ምኩራበ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ከይሲ ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ ይሰግዱ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ባቢሎን ። ወይቤሎ፣7፣ ንጉሥ ፡ ለዳንኤል ፡፤ ኢትክል፤ ፡ ትበል ፡ ከመ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡ አምላክ ሕያው ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ዳንኤል ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ኢይሰግድ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ሕያው ፡ አላ ፡ ሀበኒ ፡ ሥልጣነ ፡ ኦንጉሥ ፡ ከመ ፡ እቅትሎ ፡ አነ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ከይሲ ፡ ዘእንበለ ሰይፍ ፡ ወበትር ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወሀብኩከ ፡ ኪያሁ ። ወነሥአ ፡ ዳንኤል ፡ ዝፍተ፣፤ ወፀጐረ ፤ ወሰብሐ ፡ ወአብሰሎሙ ፡ ኅቡረ ፡ ወረሰዮሙ ፡ አምሳለ ፡ ኅብስት ፡ ንኡሳን ፤ ወወደዮሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አፉሁ ፡ ለከይሲ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ውኅጦሙ ፡ ወሞተ ። ወይቤ ሎሙ?፤ ፡ ዳንኤል ፡ ርእዩ ፡ አምላክክሙ ፡ ኦሰብአ ፡ ባቢሎን ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ዘንተ ፡ ተጋብኡ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወከልሑ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ አይሁዳዊኑ ፡ ዳንኤል ፡ ኮነ ፣ ንጉሠ ፡ ወናሁ ፡ ሰበሮ ፡ ለቤል ፡ ወቀተሎሙ ፡ ለገነውት ፤ ወቀተሎ ፡ ለከይሲ ። ወይቤልዎ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኢወሀብከነ ፡ ለዳንኤል ፤ ወእመአኮ ፡ ንቀትለከ ፡ ወንቀትል ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ቤትከ ፡ ወሐዘነ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዳንኤል ፡ ፈድፋደ ። ወአኀዝዎ ለዳንኤል ፡ ወገደፍዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ግበ ፡ አናብስት ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ሀየ ፡ ፪መዓልተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምስጥዎ ፡ አናብስት ። ወሀለዉ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ግብ ፡ አናብስት ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ ይሁብዎሙ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ አሚረ ፡ ፪አብድንተ ፡ ሰብእ ፣፤ ወ፪አባግዓ ፣ ወበእማንቱ" ፡
There was also a great serpent and the people of Babylon worshipped it. The king said to Dän'ël: "You cannot say that this one is not a living god." Dän'ël said to the king: "I worship only the living God, but give me permission, O king, to kill this serpent without sword or staff." The king said to him: "I give it to you." Dän'ël took pitch, hair, and fat, boiled the (ingredients) together, shaped them into the likeness of small loaves, and threw them into the mouth of the serpent. It swallowed them and died. Dän'ël said: "See your god, people of Babylon!" When (the Babylonians) heard this, they gathered to the king and cried out, saying: "Has the Jew Dän'ël become king? Behold, he has broken Bel, killed the priests, and killed the serpent." They said to the king: "If you do not give us Dän'ël, we will kill you and we will kill the people of your house." The king was greatly distressed for Dän'ël. They seized Dän'ël and threw him into the lions' den. He remained there for six days so that the lions would tear him apart. There were seven lions in the den; they were given two human corpses and two sheep every day, but, on those days, they were not given anything to eat


[p. 404] {#po.46.p404}
መዋዕል ፤ ኢወሀብዎሙ ፡ ዘይበልዑ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይርሐቡ ፡ ወይብልዕዎ ፡ ለዳንኤል ። ወፈነወ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ መልአኮ ፡ ወጾሮ ፡ ለእንባቆም ፡ ነቢይ ፡ እምኢየሩሳሌም ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጸውር ፡ ሲሳዮሙ ፡ ለእለ ፡ የአርፉ ፡ እክለ ፡ ወአንበሮ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ግበ ፡ ባቢሎን ፡ ኀበ ፡ዳንኤል ፡ ወበልዓ ፡ ወተናዘዘ ፡ በቃሉ ፡ ለእንባቆምቶ፥ ። ወበይእቲ ፡ ሰዓት ፡ ሜጦ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለእንባቆም ፡ ኀበ ፣ ኢየሩሳሌም ። ወበሳብዕት ፡ ዕለት ፡ መጽአ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ግብ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይብኪ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ዳንኤል ፡ እስመ ፡ መሰሎ ፡ ከመ፣ ውእቱ ፤ ሞተ ፡ ወነጸረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ግብ ፡ ወርእዮ፥' ፡ ለዳንኤል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይነብር ፤ ወአናብስት ፡ ከመ ፡ ድመታት ፡ ይልሕሱ ፡ እገሪሁ ። ወጸርኃ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ በቃል ፡ ልዑል፤ ወይቤ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አምላኩ ፡ ለዳንኤልቶ፥ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡፣ አዕረግዎ፥ ፡ ለዳንኤል ፡ እምግብ ፡ ወአኀዝዎሙ ፡ ለጸላእቱ ፡ እለ ፡ አስተዋደይዎ ፡ 'ወፈቀዱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቅትል ዎሙ ፤ ወይግድፍዎሙቶ+" ፡ ውስተ ፡ ግብ ። ወበይእቲ ፡ ሰዓት ፡ በልዕዎሙ ፡ አናብስት ፡
so that they would be hungry and eat Dän'ël. God sent his angel and (the latter) transported from Jerusalem 'Enbägom (= Habakkuk) the prophet, who was carrying their food to those who were harvesting wheat, and he placed him above the den of Babylon near Dän'ël. (The latter) ate and was comforted by the word of 'Enbägom. At that very moment, the angel of God returned 'Enbägom to Jerusalem. On the seventh day, the king came near the den to weep for Dän'ël, for he imagined that he was dead. He looked into the den and saw Dän'ël sitting, the lions, like cats, licking his feet. The king exclaimed in a loud voice and said: "Great is the God of Dän'ël." Immediately, they brought Dän'ël up from the den; they seized his enemies who had denounced him and wanted to kill them and throw them into the den. At that very moment, the lions ate them before the king and before Dän'ël
በቅድመ ፡፣ ንጉሥ ፡ ወበቅድመ ፡ ዳንኤል+፥፤ ። ወነበረ ፡ ዳንኤል ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ባቢሎን ፡ እስከ ፡፤ አመ ፡ ተመይጡ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ፡፤ ኀበ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወይእቲ ፡ ድዓመት ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተጋብኡ ፡ ማኅበረ ፡ አይሁድ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ቀያፋ ከመ ፡ ይትማከሩ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ እግዚእነ ። ሣህሉ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ምስለ ፡ ገብሩ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
Dän'ël remained in the city of Babylon until the time when the children of Israel returned to Jerusalem - this (was) the seventieth year (of the captivity) - and he died in peace. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen. [On this day also, the assembly of the Jews gathered in the house of Qayäfä (= Caiaphas) to deliberate against our Lord. May his mercy be with his servant 'Atnätëwos in the centuries of centuries, amen.]

### READING FOR THE 24TH OF MAGGABIT
አመ ፡ ፳፪ለመጋቢት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፤ ወውእቱ ፡ እምጐልቆሙ ፡ ለአበው ፡ ሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ፃወ8፪ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፤ እምሀገረ ፡ እንተ ፡ ስማ ፡ ሶብራ፤ ። ወመነነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዓለመ ፡ እምንእሱ ፡ ወፈተወ፤ ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ምንኩስና ። ወቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ አስቄጥስ ፤ ወመንኩሰ ፡ በቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያኑ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ ወተጸምደ ፣ ወተጋደለ ፡ በምንኩስና ፡ ወተከሥተ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ትሩፋት ፡ ወምግባራተ ፡ ሠናይ ። ወሶበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አባ ፤ ቆዝሞስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፣፤ ዘእምቅድሜሁ ፡ ወተሰናአዉ ፡ ኤጴስ ፡ ቆልሳት ፡ በ፳ምክር ፡ ከመ ፤ ይሚምዎ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ወሜምዎ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳተ" ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፈቃዱ ። ወሶበ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ አስቄጥስ ፡ በከመ ፡ ልማዱ ፡ ወእንዘ ፡ የሐውር ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ዘተወልደ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ወሀለወት ፡ እሙ ፡ በሕይወታ ፡ ወኮነት ፡ ጸድቅተ ፡ ወንጽሕተ ፡ ወፈራሂተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እንዘ” ፡ ትጸልእ ፡ ክብረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ኃላፊ ፡ ወታፈቅር ፡ ስብሐተ ፤ ሰማያዌ ። ወቦአ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትአምኃ ፡ ለእሙ ።
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the holy father abbä Magäres (= Macarius), patriarch of the city of Alexandria; among the patriarch fathers, he is the fifty-ninth. This saint was from the city whose name (is) Sobrä (= Choubra). This saint despised the world from his childhood and desired the monastic habit. He entered the desert of 'Asqêtes (= Scetis), became a monk in the church of the holy abbä Magäres (Macarius), devoted himself to devotion and to the (spiritual) combat in the monastic state, and showed virtues and beautiful deeds. When abbä Qozmos (= Cosmas) died, the patriarch who (had been) before him, the bishops agreed with one sentiment to consecrate this father and they consecrated him patriarch against his will. When he went out from the city of Alexandria to go to the desert of 'Asqêtes according to his habit, he arrived on the way at the city where he was born; his mother was (still) alive, she was righteous, virtuous, and feared God, hating the honors of this transitory world and


[p. 406] {#po.46.p406}
ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓት ፡ እሙ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ በጽሐ ፤ ኢወፅአት ፡ ኀቤሁ ። ወሶበ ፤ ቦአ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤት ፡ ረከባ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትነብር ፡ ወትፈትል ፡ ወኢተንሥአት ፡ ሎቱ ፡፤ ወኢተአምኃቶ ። ወመሰሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ኢያእመረቶ ፡ ወይቤላ ፡ አነ ፡ ውእቱ!" ፣ ወልድኪ ፡ ለምንት ፤ ኢትትአምኅኒ፤፤ ፡ ኢያእመርኪኒኑ ። ወአውሥአቶ ፡ ወትቤሎ ፡ አንሰ ፡ አእመርኩከ ፡ ወአንተሰ፤ ፡ ኢያእመርከ ፡ ነፍሰከ ። ወአንሰ ፡ ኮንኩ ፡ እፈቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ እርአይከ፤ ፡ ምውተ ፡ ወግኑዘ ፡ ወኢይርአይከ፤ ፡ በሊቀ ፡፤ ጵጵስና፤ ። እስመ ፡ አንተ ፣፤ ትማልም ፡፣ ኮንከ ፣ ይትኅሠሠከ ፡ በእንተ ፣ ኃጢአተ ፡ ነፍስከ፤ ፣ ባሕቲትከ ፡፤ ወአንተ ፣ ዮም ፡ ይትኀሠሥሠከ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ኃጢአተ ፡ መርዔትከ ። ወእምዝ ፡ በከዩ ፡ ክልኤሆሙ ፡ ኅቡረ ፡ ወቦአ ፡ ነገራ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ልቡ ። ወኮነ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ አሚረ ፡ ይዜክር ፡ ነገራ ፡ ለእሙ ፡ ወይትጋደል ፡ 'ለነፍሱ ፡ በእንተ፤?” ፡ ዓቂበ ፡ መርዔቱ ። ወኮነ ፡ ያነቅሆሙ ፡ ለሕዝቡ ፡ በአንብቦ ፡ መጻሕፍት ፡ ወበተግሣጸት ፡ ወይትዓቀብ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይንበር ፡ እዴሁ ፣፡ ወኢይሚም"፤ ፡ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆፅሳተ ፡ ወኢካህናተ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ዘይጌይስ ፡ ለክህነት ። ወኢይነሥእ ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ እምንዋየ ፡ አብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናት ፡ ወኢዘኢይደልዎ ፡ ነሚአ ። ወኮነ ፡ ኢይሠይም ፤ መኔነሂ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ስምዓ ፡ ካህናት ፡ ጸድቃን ፡ ቡሩካን ። ወኮነ ፡ ወትረ ፡ ይኤዝዞሙ ፡ ለኤሏኢስ ፡ ቆኦሳት ፡ ወለቀሳውስት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይርአዩ ፡ ሕዝቦሙ ፣፤ ወይዕቀብዎሙ" ፡ በድርሳናት ፤ ወተግሣጻት ። ወነበረ ፡ ዲበ ፡ መንበረ ፡ ማርቆስ ፡ ወንጌላዊ ፡ ፳ዓመተ ፡ በህድአት ፡፣ ወበሰላም ። ወአብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናት ፡ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ርቱዓን ፡ ሥሩዓን ፣ በጸሎታት ፡ ወቅዳስያት ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ጽርዓት ። ወአዕረፈ ፡፤ በሰላም ። እግዚአብሔር ፡
loving the heavenly glory. This father entered that city to greet his mother. When his mother learned that he had arrived, she did not go out to meet him. When he entered the house, he found her sitting and spinning; she did not rise for him nor did she greet him. He imagined that she did not recognize him and said to her: "I am your son, why do you not greet me, have you not recognized me?" She answered him and said to her: "I, I know you, but you, you do not know yourself. I would like to see you dead and buried and not see you in the patriarchate; for yesterday you were examined only for your own sins, but today you are examined for all the sins of your flock." After that, they both wept together and her (= his mother's) word penetrated his heart: every day, he remembered his mother's word and fought (spiritually) for himself because of the guarding of his flock. He kept his people awake by the reading of the Scriptures and by exhortations. He guarded himself from laying hands on - and consecrating - bishops and priests other than those who were the best for the priesthood. He took nothing from the goods of the churches nor what he should not take. He named no one except on the testimony of righteous (and) blessed priests. He continually prescribed to the bishops and priests to lead their flock and to guard it by homilies and exhortations. He remained twenty years on the seat of Märgos (= Mark) the evangelist in tranquility and in peace. In his time, the churches (were) well established, without interruption regarding prayers and offices. He died in peace. May God have mercy on us by his prayer, amen.
ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ አሜን ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አእረፈ ፡ ወለተ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ወለተ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ልብነ ፡ ድንግል ፡ ወተቀብረት ፡ ውስተ ፡ መንፈሳዊት ፡ መቅደስ ፡ እንተ ፡ ትሰመይ ፡ ዳጋ ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ 'ካዕበ ፡ ተጋብኡ፥" ፡ ማኅበረ ፡ አይሁድ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ "ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ፤ ። ሥህሉ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ምስለ ፡ ገብሩ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
On this day died Walatta geddusän, the daughter of King Lebna Dengel; she was buried in the spiritual sanctuary which is called Dägaä. [On this day also, the assembly of the Jews gathered against our Lord Jesus Christ. May his mercy be with his servant 'Atnätéwos in the centuries of centuries.]

### READING FOR THE 25TH OF MAGGABIT
አመ ፡ ፳ወ፳ለመጋቢት ፡ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡፤ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ኦኒሲፎርስ' ፡ ዘእም ድወ፪አር ድእት ፡ ዘኀረዮሙ ፡ እግዚእነ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ኮነ ፡ እምደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ፡እምነገደ ፡ ብንያም ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ አበዊሁ ፡ ዓቃቢያነ ፡ ሕገ ፡ ኦሪት ። ወተለዎ ፡ ለመድኃኒነ ፡ እምጐልቆሙ" ፡ ለእለ ፤ ተጸምድዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይነጽር ፡፤ ምግባራቲሁ ፡ ወዕበየ ፤ ተኣምራቲሁ ፡ ወይስማፅ ፡ ትምህርታቲሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ ነበረ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ሕዳጠ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the great saint, the apostle 'Onisifores (= Onesiphorus) who (is part of) the seventy-two disciples whom our Lord chose. This saint was of the children of Israel, of the tribe of Benyäm (= Benjamin); his parents observed the Mosaic law. He followed our Savior - (being) among those who attached themselves to him - to see his actions and the greatness of his


[p. 408] {#po.46.p408}
እንዘ ፡ ይኔጽር ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ተኣምራተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ፡፣ ውሉጣተ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ አንስኦ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ለወልደ ፡ መበልት ፡ በሀገረ ፡ ናይን ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፣ እምጐልቆሙ ፥፣ ለሕዝብ ፡ እለ ፡ ነበሩ ፡ በህየ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ትእምርተ ፣ ኢኀሠሠ ፡ ምስ ሌሃ ፡ ካልአ ፡ ተአምረ ፡ ወኢጐንደየ ፡ አላ ፡ በጊዜሃ ፡ ሐደገ ፡ ተበርሆ ፡ በማሕቶተ ፡ ሕገ ፡ አይሁድ ፡ ወፈቀደ ፡ ፀሐየ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ ወቀርበ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስ ቶስ ፡ ወአምነ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ልቡ ፤ ወተጠምቀ ፡ በእዴሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ ነሥአ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ጳራቅሊጦስ ፡ በጽርሐ ፡ ጽዮን ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፣ ወተለዎሙ ፡ ለሐዋርያት ፤ ወተልእኮሙ ፡ ወሰበከ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አህጉር ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ወሜምዎ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፤ ኤዲስ ፡ ቆፅሰ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ በሐውርተ ፡ አቴና ፡ ወሰበከ ፡ ውስቴቶሙ ፡ ወአጥመቆሙ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ፡፤ ወአብርሀ ፡ ነፍሳቲሆሙ ፡ በትምህርቱ ፡ ወተግሣጹ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ይፌውስ ፡ ነፍሳቲሆሙ ፣፤ ወሥጋሆሙ ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ ተጋድሎቶ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ በሰ ላም ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስብሐት ። ወኮነ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ሕይወቱ ፡ ድዓመት፣፤ ወነበረ ፡እምኔሃ ፡ ፳ ወ፪ ዓመተ ፡በሕገ ፡ አይሁድ ፡ጣወ ኔ5ዓመተ ፡በሕገ ፡ክርስቲያን ። ወናሁ ፡ ዘከሮ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ በውስተ ፣፤ መልእክቱ ፡ ለጢሞቴዎስ ፡ ወውስተ ካልኣን ፡ መልእክታት ፡ ወተአምፕ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። አመ ፡ ፳ወፄሄለመጋቢት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ፍርፍዮስ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
miracles, and to hear his teachings. When he had remained thus for some time, constantly seeing numerous (and) varied miracles, he was with our Lord - among the people who were there - when (the latter) raised the son of the widow in the city of Näyen (= Nain). When he had seen this miracle, he did not ask for other signs in addition; he did not delay any longer, but immediately renounced being enlightened by the lamp of the law of the Jews and desired the sun of truth. He approached our Lord Jesus Christ, believed in him with all his heart, and was baptized by his hand. When he had received the grace of the Holy Spirit, the Paraclete, in the upper room of Zion with the apostles, he followed the apostles, served them, and preached in many cities. The apostles instituted him bishop in the territories of 'Atëna (= Athens). He preached there, baptized them (= the inhabitants) with Christian baptism, and illuminated their souls by his teaching and his exhortations - he cared for their souls and their bodies. When he had finished his (spiritual) combat, he died in peace and received the crown of glory. All the days of his life were seventy years, of which he spent twenty-nine in the law of the Jews (and) forty-one in the law of the Christians. Behold, the apostle Päwlos (= Paul) mentions him in his epistle to Timotëwos (= Timothy) and in other letters and greets him. May God have mercy on us by his prayer in the centuries of centuries, amen. READING FOR THE 26TH OF MAGGABIT. In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day, (there is) the commemoration of the saint Ferfeyos (= Porphyry?). May his prayer be with us, amen.

On this day also died the holy (and) blessed virgin 'lyoparägesyà (= Eupraxia). This saint was the daughter of a great dignitary of the palace of the city of Romë (= Rome) of the family of 'Anoryos (= Honorius) the emperor. When the moment of her father's death arrived, (the latter) asked 'Anoryos the emperor to guard her. After the death of her father, the emperor 'Anoryos betrothed her to the son of one of the principal dignitaries. In those days, her mother went to the land of Egypt to collect the revenues of her lands and the fruits of the orchards that her husband had left her. She took with her this saint, her daughter, aged nine years. When they arrived in the land of Egypt, they stayed in a monastery of virgins until they had finished their business. The virgins of this monastery were very devout and

ascetic, eating nothing in the way of meaty foods, nor fat, nor tasty fruits, not tasting wine at all, and sleeping on the ground without a mattress. lyopärägesyä loved this monastery and became friends with the steward who (was) in this monastery. This steward said to her: "Promise me not to leave this monastery, not to return, and not to seek your fiancé." She made a promise to her about this. When her mother had finished her business and wanted to return to her country, this saint did not want to go with her mother. She said to her mother: "I have made a vow to my Lord Jesus Christ; henceforth, I do not desire weddings of this transitory world, for I have a true spouse, my Lord Jesus Christ." When her mother recognized that (her daughter) would not go with her, she gave all her goods to the poor and the needy and remained with her daughter 'lyopärägesyà in the monastery for many years. When 'Anoryos the emperor learned that the mother of 'lyopärägesyà was dead, he sent to look for her daughter to marry her to the one to whom he had betrothed her. The saint sent word to 'Anoryos the emperor and said to him: "Know, O emperor, that I have vowed myself to my Lord Christ and that it is not possible for me to transgress the promise I have made." When the emperor learned her message, he shed many tears and admired her wisdom: the (saint) was indeed small in age

The saint 'lyopärägesyä led a very great (spiritual) combat. She fasted two days in a row and three days in a row, then four days and, after that, she fasted a week in a row. During the great holy fast, she did not eat food that had been cooked on fire. Satan was jealous of her and struck her on the foot with a painful blow, and she remained suffering (from it) for many days. Then God had mercy on her and healed her of her ailment. God granted her the gift of healing the sick and she healed many sick people. She obeyed and submitted with humility to the abbess and to her sisters the virgins; she was loved by all. One night, the abbess saw a vision: they were preparing palaces and crowns. She was astonished and said: "For whom is this?" They said to her: "These palaces and crowns (are) for 'Éwopärakes your daughter, she is going to come here now." The abbess told the virgins the vision she had seen and ordered them not to tell it to 'lyoparägesyä. God wanted to cause her to die and she was struck by a light illness. The virgins, the abbess, and the steward who had made a pact with her gathered near her, and all asked her to make mention of them. Then she died in peace; the virgins wept for her and were very distressed to have lost this
ደናግል ፡ ወሐዘና ፡፤ ፈድፋደ ፡ እስመ ፡ እማንቱ ፡ ኃጥኣነ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት ። ወእምድኅሬሃ ፡ አዕረፈት ፡ መጋቢት ፡ ፍቅርታ ፡ ወእምድኅሬሃ ፡ በሕዳጥ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ሐመት ፤ እመ ፡ ምኔት ፡፤ ወአስተጋብአቶን ፡ ለደናግል ፡ ወትቤሎን ፡ ኅርያ ፡ ዘትሠ ይማ ፡ ላዕሌክን ፡ እመ ፡ ምኔተ ፡ እምድኅሬየ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኢዮጳራቅስያ ፡ ሰአለት ፡ በእንቲአየ ። ወይእዜኒ ፡ ፅፅዋ፤ ፣ ኖኅተ ፡ ወሖራ ፡ ወገብራ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዘቶን ። ወበሳኒታሁ ፡ በጽሓ" ፡ ወረከባሃ ፣ በዘ ፤ አዕረፈት ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎታ፤ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ኢዮጳራቅስያ ፡ ወጸሎቶንቶ ፡ ለኩሎን ፡ ደናግል ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፤ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ወብፁዕ ፡ እስጢፋኖስ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፤ ምስለ ፤ ፍቁሩ ፣፤ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
saint. The steward, her friend, died after her and, a few days later, the abbess fell ill. She gathered the virgins and said to them: "Choose the one whom you will put at your head as abbess after me, for 'lyopärägesyà has interceded for me. Now, close the door and go." They did as she had ordered them. The next day, they came and found her dead. May God have mercy on us by the prayer of this saint 'lyopärägesyä and may the prayer of all the virgins be with us, amen. [On this day, (there is) the commemoration of the saint and blessed 'Estifanos (= Stephen). May his prayer and his blessing be with his dear 'Atnätëwos in the centuries of centuries.]


[p. 412] {#po.46.p412}
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ሐፀበ ፣ እግዚእነ ፡ እግረ ፥ አርዳኢሁ ፣ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ አንትሙኒ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ግበሩ ፡ ለቢጽክሙ ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አግብኦ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ ሜጦ ፡ ቋብሩረ ፡ ከመ ፡ ተነበዩ ፡ ነቢያት ። ሣህሉ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ምስለ ፤ ፍቁሩ ፤ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
[On this day also, our Lord washed the feet of his disciples and said to them: "You too, act thus with your neighbor." And that night again, Yehudä (= Judas) betrayed our Lord and received as a price thirty (pieces of) silver, as (the prophets) had prophesied. May his (= Jesus') mercy be with his dear 'Atnätéwos in the centuries of centuries.]

### READING FOR THE 27TH OF MAGGABIT
አመ ፡ ፳ወ፪ለመጋቢት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ፤ ፡ ወብፁዕ ፡ ማኅቶተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ አበ ፡ ዙሎሙ ፡ መነኮሳተ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ አስቄጥስ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ ዘየዓቢ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምሰ ብአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ሰሱዊር፤ ፡ እምደወለ ፡ መኑፍ ፡ እምደቡበ ፡ ግብጽ ። ወኮኑ ፡ አበዊሁ ፡ ራነ ፡ ወጻድቃነ ፡ ወስሙ ፡ ለአቡሁ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ ወስማ ፡ ለእሙ ፡ ሳራ ፣ ወይእቲ ፣፤ ከመ ፡ ሳራ ፡ ወኤልሳቤጥ ፡ ወተሐውር ፡ በትእዛዘ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በትሕትና ። ወኮነ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ካህን ፡ክቡር ፡'እንዘ ፡ ይትለአክ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ መቅደስ ፡ወትረ ፡ በፈሪሀ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡፤ በንጽሕና ፡፤ ወበቅድስና ። ወባረከ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡፤ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ወአብዓሎሙ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ምግባሮሙ ። ወኮኑ ፡ ይሁቡ ፡፤ ምጽዋተ ፡ ለነዳያን ፡ ወይሜህርዎሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወይትጋደሉ ፡ ወትረ ፡ በጾም ፡ ወበጸሎት ። ወኢኮነ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ወልደ ፤ ወአስተርአየ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ለአቡሁ ፡ ራእይ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአጠየቆ ፡ ከመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ የሀቦ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ዘይከ ውን ፡ ዝክሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አጽናፈ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወይወልድ ፡ ውሎድ ፡ መንፈሳዊያን ። ወወሀቦ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ 'መቃርስ ፡ ዘበትርጓሜ ፡ ስሙ!" ፡ ብፁዕ ። ወኮነ ፡ጸጋ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ እምንእሱ ፡ ወይትኤዘዝ!! ፡ ለአበዊሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ ልህቀ ፡ በቁመቱ ፡ ፈቀዱ ፡ አበዊሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያስተዋስብዎ"" ፡ ወኢኮነ ፡ ይፈቅድ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ግብረ ። ወአገበርዎ ፡፤ ከመ ፡ ይግበር ፣ ፈቃዶሙ ፡ ወተአዘዘ ፡ ሎሙ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the holy and blessed father, the lamp of the desert, the father of all the monks of the desert of 'Asgêtes (= Scetis), abbä Magäres (= Macarius) the Great. This saint was of the people of the city of Sasuwir (= Chechouir), of the district of Manuf (= Manouf) in the north of Egypt. His parents were virtuous and righteous. The name of his father (was) 'Abrehäm (= Abraham) and the name of his mother, Särä (= Sarah); the latter (was) like Sarah and 'Élsäbët (= Elizabeth) and she followed the commandment of God with humility. His father was an honored priest, officiating continually at the sanctuary (of the church) in the fear of God, purity, and holiness. God had blessed them and filled them in all their actions. They gave alms to the poor, instructed all the people, and led a constant (spiritual) combat in fasting and in prayer. They had no child. A vision appeared to his (= Magäres') father from God and informed him that God was going to give him a son whose mention would reach all the ends of the earth and (who) would beget spiritual sons. God gave him this saint Magäres whose name, in translation, (means) "blessed". The grace of God was upon him from his childhood; he obeyed his

፡ ወይቤላ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወሬዛ ፡ ሶበ ፤ ተስእለኪ ፡ አቡኪ ፡ ወይቤለኪ ፡ መኑ ፡ዘአማሰነ ፡ ድንግልናኪ ፡ በልዮ ፡-እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ቀሲስ ፡ ባሕታዊ፣ ዘአማሰነ ፡ ድንግልናየ ። ወሶበ ፡ አእመረ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ፀንሰት ፡ ወተስእላ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ወይቤላ ፡ መኑ ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ ብኪ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ኃፍረተ ። ወአውሥአቶ ፡ ወት ቤሎ ፡ በአሐቲ ፡ ፅለት ፡ ሖርኩ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ባሕታዊ ፡ ቀሲስ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ ወሰከበ ፡ ምስሌየ ፡ ወፀነስኩ ፡ እምኔሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ አበዊሃ" ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ተምዑ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወሖሩ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፣ ወምስሌሆሙ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወአውፅእዎ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፤ እምበዓቱ ፣ ወውእቱ ፡ ኢያእመረ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ወዘበጥዎ ፡ ዝብጠተ ፡ ዓቢየ ፤ ማሕምመ ፡ እስከ ፡ ቀርበ ፡ ለመዊት ። ወኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ይስእሎሙ ፡፣ እንዘ ፡ ይብል" ፡ ምንት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ኃጢአትየ ፡ እስመ ፡ አንትሙ ፡ ትዘብጡኒ ዘእንበለ ፡ ምሕረት ። ወእምዝ ፡ አሰሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ክሳዱ ፡ ሐብለ ፡ ወዲቤሁ፣፤ ሰቀሉ ፡ገልዓተ ፡ ዘአጽለምዎሙ ፡ በፍሕም ። ወኮኑ ፡ ይስሕብዎ ፡ ለፌ ፡ ወለፌ ፡ ከመ ፡ አብድ ፡ ወይኬልሐ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘአማሰነ ፡ ድንግልናሃ ፡ ለወለትነ ። ወበውእቱ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ አስተርአዩ ፡ መላእክት ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ ሰብእ ፣ ወይቤል ዎሙ ፡ ለእሉ ፡እኩያን ፡ምንተ ፡ዘገብረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፣ መስተጋድል ፡ ወነገርዎሙ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ወለት ። ወይቤልዎሙ ፡ እሎንቱ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ነገር ፡ ሐሰት ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እስመ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ናአምሮ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ እምንእሱ ፡ እስከ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ቴር ፡ ወጻድቅ ። ወቀርቡ ፡ እሎንቱ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡
parents. When he had grown in stature, his parents wanted to marry him; he did not want this thing. They forced him to do their will, he obeyed them, and they married him against his will. When he entered the bridal chamber, he acted as if he were sick and remained many days thus. After that, he asked his father and said to him: "Let me go to the desert so that I may recover a little from this illness." Every day, he asked our Lord Christ, at the time of his prayer, to guide him in the execution of the will of God. Then he went to the desert of 'Asqêtes. When he was in the desert, he saw a vision: a six-winged cherub was taking him by the hand, making him go up to the summit of the mountain, showing him the whole desert to the east and to the west, its length and its width, and saying to him: "Behold, God has given you this desert as an inheritance for you and for your sons." When he returned from the desert, he found the young girl sick. Then she died while being in the state of a virgin. He thanked our Lord Christ greatly. Some time later, his parents died and he gave all the goods they had left him to the poor, (to) the needy, and (to) the destitute. When the people of Sasuwires, his city, saw the righteousness and purity of the saint abbä Magäres, they instituted him priest over them and built him a dwelling outside the city; the people of the city went to him and received from him the holy Mysteries. He had a man (who) served him and sold the work of his hands. There was a young girl in that city - she was a virgin -, she fornicated with an adolescent and became pregnant. The adolescent said to her: "When your father questions you and says to you: Who is the one who defiled your virginity? say to him: It is the solitary priest who defiled my virginity." When her father knew that she was pregnant, he questioned her and said to her: "Who is the one who did you this shame?" She answered him and said to him: "One day, I went to the solitary priest whose name (is) Magäres, he lay with me and I became pregnant from his works." When her parents heard this story, they were greatly angered and went to the saint abbä Magäres - and with them, many people. They made the saint come out of his cell - the latter did not know what was happening - and struck him with harsh (and) painful blows until he was near death. The saint questioned them, saying: "What is my sin that you strike me without pity?" Then, they tied a rope to his neck and hung from it shards that they had blackened with coal. They dragged him here and there like a madman and cried out against him, saying: "This is the one who defiled the virginity of our daughter." At that moment, angels appeared in the guise of men; they said to these wicked ones: "What has this (spiritual) combatant done?" (The wicked ones) told them how he had acted against the young girl. The angels said to them: "This story is a lie, for we have known this man since his childhood until this day, and he (is) virtuous and righteous." The
