angels approached him, delivered him from his bonds, and threw the shards off him. The wicked ones said to them: "We will not let him go until he gives us someone who will be a guarantor for him." The man who sold the work of his (= Magäres') hands came and stood as guarantor for him before them that he would give the young girl her food until she gave birth. They (= the people) drove him (= Magäres) away and he went to his cell. He exhorted himself and said: "O Magäres, behold, now, you have a wife and a child; you must struggle night and day for your food and their food." He was continually making baskets and giving them to the man who served him so that he would sell them, and he gave the woman her food. He was working thus until the moment of the woman's delivery approached. (The labor) was very painful for her and she remained in great suffering for four days (and) four nights. She was near death and did not give birth. Her mother said to her: "What has happened to you? (Tell it,) for behold, you are going to die now." (The young woman) said to her mother: "Yes, for me, I deserve to die, for I fornicated with such and such an adolescent and I spoke falsely against the servant of God, the holy priest, abbä Magäres the solitary." When her parents heard this, they were greatly distressed; they all gathered with the people of the city and deliberated to go to the saint and ask him to forgive them the fault they had committed against him. When the saint learned that the people of the city wanted to come and ask him for forgiveness, he remembered the vision he had seen in the desert, consecrated the eucharist, and received the holy Mysteries. The six-winged cherub appeared to him, took him by the hand, and guided him until he had brought him to the desert of 'Asqêtes, (a word) which, translated, (means) "balance of hearts". The saint abbä Magäres said to the cherub: "My lord, fix me a place where I will dwell." The cherub answered him and said to him: "I will not fix you a place: so that you do not go out of the one that I had fixed for you and so that you do not transgress the commandment of God, behold, this whole desert (is) yours; dwell where you will." The saint abbä Magäres dwelt in the inner desert, at the place of the (future) monastery of the saints Maksimos (= Maximus) and Damätëwos (= Domitius). When (the latter) arrived near him, they dwelt in his proximity. After their death, the angel of God ordered him to go and dwell at the place which is now his own monastery. The angel said to him: "This place will be called by the name of your (spiritual) sons Maksimos and Dimätéwos." And, until this day, it is called the monastery of Bermos, which, translated, (means) "monastery of Rome". The saint abbä Magäres made himself a cell and dwelt there. He led a great (spiritual) combat in fasting, in prayer, in prostration
ወሰጊድ ፡ ወትጋህ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ጽርዓት ። ወኮኑ ፡ ሰይጣናት ፡ ያስተርአዩ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ገሃደ ፡ ወይፀብእዎ ፡ በሌሊት ፡ ወበመዓልት ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ዓመት ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይትጋደል ፤ ወይጸሙ ፡፤ ወያመነድብዎ ፡ ሰይጣናት ፣ ወኢረከበ ፡ ዕረፍተ ። ሐለየ ፡ በልቡ ፡ ወይቤ ፡፣ እንዘ ፡ ሀለውኩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወሰማዕኩ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ ለአብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ እንጦንዮስ ፡ እትነሣእ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ ወአሐ ውር ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምሐረኒ ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ ወየሀበኒ፥ ፡ አእምሮ ፡ ወል ቡና ፡ ከመ ፡ እለቡ ፡ ምክሮሙ ፡ ለሰይጣናት ፡ ርኩሳን ። ወተንሥአ ፡ ወጸለየ ፡ ወሖረ ፤ ውስተ ፡ ገዳመ ፣ ምሥራቅ ፡ እስከ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ እንጦንዮስ ። ወተወከፎ፥ ፡ በዓቢይ ፡ ፍሥሐ ፡ ወከሠተ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ሕሊናሁ ፡ በፍ ሥሐ ፡ በከመ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ 'ወኢሀብኦ ፡ ወኢምንተኒ" ። ወአረጋዊሰ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ እንጦንዮስ ፡ ተአምኃ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኦወልድየ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ አንተሰ ፡ ትሰመይ ፡ ብፁዓ ፡ በከመ ፡ ትርጓሜ ፡ ስምከ ፡ በልሳነ ፡ ዮናናዊያን ፡ እስመ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አምላኪየ ፡ ከሠተ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ግብረከ ፡ ወምጽአተከ ፡ ኅቤየ ፡ ወበእንተዝ ፡ እጸንሕ ፡ ምጽአተከ ። ወአረጋዊሰ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ እንጦንዮስ፤ ፡ መሀሮ፣ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ ቅድስተ ፡ ወአጽንዖ ፡ በነገር ፤ ብዙኅ ፡ ዘይደሉ ፡ በገቢረ ፤ ጽድቅ ። ወከሠተ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ጸቢኦቶሙ ፡ ለሰይጣናት ፤ ወይቤሎ” ፡ እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ሀለዎሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጸብኡከ ፡ በኅቡእ ፡ በሕሊና ፡ ከንቱ ፡ ወበምግባራት ፡ ገሃደ ፡ እስከ ፡ ለመዊት ፡ ከመ ፡ ትኩን ፡ አንተ ፡ ፍጹመ ፡ ሶበ፣ ይጸብኡከ ፡ ወትትዓገሥ፤ ፡ እስከ ፡ ለመዊት ። ወሰአሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ፣ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኅድገኒ ፡ እንበር ፡ በኅቤከ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ እንጦንዮስ ፡ ሖር ፡ ኀበ፣ መካን ፡ዘወሰነ ፡ ለከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወተዓገሥ ፡ ውስቴቱ ። ወሶበ" ፡ ነበረ ፡ በኅበ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ አባ ፡ እንጦንዮስ ፡ ሕዳጠ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትመሀር ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ሥርዓተፆ ፡ ምንኩስና ፤ ወገቢረ ፡ ጽድቅ ፣ ወተንሥአ ፤ ወተመይጠ ፡ ኅበ ፡ መካኑ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትፌሣሕ ፤ ወይትሐሠይ ፡ በሥርዓት ፡ ወትምህርት ፡ ማሕየዊት ፡ ዘተመሀረ ፡ እምነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ እንጦንዮስ ። ወተራከበ”" ፡ ምስለ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ሰራቢዮን?፤ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆኦስ ፤ ወይቤሎ ፡፣ ኩሎ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ዘነበርኩ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አቡየ ፡ አባ ፣ እንጦንዮስ ፣
and the vigil without respite. The demons appeared to him openly and attacked him night and day. After three years (spent) fighting and toiling - the demons tormenting him -, he found no rest. He meditated in his heart and said: "While I was in the world, I heard the story of the holy father abbä 'Entonyos (= Anthony); I am going to rise now and go to him so that he may teach me the way of the monastic state and give me knowledge and penetration so that I may penetrate the design of the impure demons." He rose, prayed, and walked in the eastern desert until he had arrived at the holy old man, abbä 'Entonyos. (The latter) welcomed him with great joy and (Magäres) revealed his thought to him with joy as with his father and hid nothing from him. The holy old man abbä 'Entonyos kissed the head of the saint abbä Magäres and said to him: "My son Magäres, you will be called blessed, in accordance with the meaning of your name in the language of the Greeks: the Lord my God has revealed to me what concerns you and your coming to me. Because of this, I was watching for your coming." The holy old man, abbä 'Entonyos, taught the saint abbä Magäres the whole holy monastic state and fortified him with many words that are appropriate in the accomplishment of righteousness. He revealed to him the attacks of the demons and said to him: "They will attack you in secret with vain thoughts and openly with acts until death, so that you become perfect when they attack you and that you be constant until death." The saint abbä Magäres made a request to him and said to him: "Permit me to dwell near you." The holy abbä 'Entonyos said to him: "Go to the place that God has fixed for you and stay there." When he had dwelt near the old man abbä 'Entonyos for a few days, learning from him the monastic rule and the practice of righteousness, he rose and returned to his dwelling, rejoicing and exulting in the rule and the life-giving teaching that he had learned from the holy abbä 'Entonyos. He met with the holy abbä Saräbiyon (= Serapion) the bishop and said to him: "All the days that I dwelt near my father abbä 'Entonyos, I never saw him sleep!"


[p. 418] {#po.46.p418}
ኢርኢክዎ ፡ ይነውም ፡ ግሙራ ። ወነበረ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካኑ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትጋደል ፡፤ ወይጸመድ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኪሩቤል ፡ ይሔወጸ”፡ ፡ ገሃደ ፡ ወትረ ። ወበአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ቃለ ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ ዘይብሎ ፤ ኦመቃርስ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘሰማዕከ ፡ ትእዛዝየ ፡ ወመጸእከ ፡ ኅቤየ ፡ ወኃደርከ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፤ መካን ፡ ናሁ ፡ ኣስተጋብእ ፡ አነ ፤ ውስተ ፡፤ ዝንቱ ፡ መካን ፡ አሕዛበ ፡ ዘኢይትጐለቀ፦ ፤ እምኩሉ ፤ ትዝምድ?” ፡ ወእምኩሉ ፡ በሐውርት ፡ ወልሳናት"፤ ፡ ወይትለአኩኒ ፡፤ ወይባርኩ ፡ ስምየ ፣ በምግባራቲሆሙ ፡ ሠናይት ። ተወከፎሙ ፡ ወምርሖሙ ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ ጽድቅ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ጸንዓ ፡
The saint abbä Magäres remained in his dwelling for many days, giving himself to asceticism and devotion in the monastic state, and the cherub visited him ceaselessly and openly. One day, he heard a voice (coming) from heaven which said to him: "Magäres, because you have obeyed my order, (that) you have come to me, and (that) you have dwelt in this place, behold, I, I will gather in this place an innumerable people of all races and of all countries and languages. They will serve me and bless my name by their beautiful deeds. Welcome them and teach them the way of righteousness." When the saint Magäres heard this, he was full of strength and power in his heart. While he was standing at the time of prayer, at night, God


[p. 420] {#po.46.p420}
ወተኃየለ ፡ በልቡ ። ወእንዘ ፡ ይቀውም ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ጸሎት ፡ በሌሊት ፡፣ ከሠተ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፤ ወሰምዖሙ ፡ ለሰይጣናት ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይትማከሩ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ ወይ ቤሉ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኀደግናሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ይኅድር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ውእቱሰ ፡ ይመርሖሙ ፡፤ ለብዙኃን ፡ አሕዛብ ፤ ወየሐድሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ገዳም ፣ ወይሬስይዋ ፤ ላቲ ፡ አህጉረ ፡ ሰማያዊያነ ፥ እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ይትዌከሉ ፡ በሕይወት ፡ ዘለዓለም?" ፡ ወይሰድዱነ ፡ እምዛቲ ፡ ገዳም ፤ በሕማመ ፡፣ ኩነኔ ፤ ጸሎታቲሆሙ ። ንዑ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ ንትጋባእ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ዮጊ ፡ ንክል ፡ ንስድዶ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ መካን ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ጸንዓ ፡ በልቡ ፡ ወተሐየለ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሰይጣናት ። ወኮነ ፤ ይባርኮ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘከሠተ ፡ እዘኒሁ ፡ እስከ ፣ ሰምዓ ፣ ምክሮሙ ፣ ለሰይጣናት ፤ ወአእመረ ፡ ድካሞሙ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ተጋብኡ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ሰይጣናት ፤ ወጸብእዎ ፡ ጸብአ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ ወአንደዱ" ፡ እሳተ ፡ ብዙኃ፤ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ኖኅተ ፡ በዐቱ ። ወኮኑ ፡ ይነሥኡ ፡ እሳተ ፣ ወይወድዩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ በዐቱ ፡ ወኮነት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ እሳት ፡ ትጠፍዋእ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተመውኡ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ ወወደዩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ሕሊና ፡ ዝሙት ፡ ወትካዘ ፡ ልብ ፡ 'ወሕማመ ፡ ወትዕቢተ ፡ ወፍ ክረ ፡ ክብረ ፡ ከንቱ ፡ ወፍርሀተ ፡ ወትምክሕተ ፡ ወሀኬተ ፡ ወጽርፈተ ፡ ወኃጢአ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ወቀቢጸ ፡ ተስፋ ፡ እምነ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወዘይፈደፍድኒ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡
opened (his ears) and he heard the demons deliberating among themselves; they said: "If we let this man dwell in this desert, he will lead many people (there); they will dwell in this desert, will make of it heavenly cities - for they have confidence in eternal life - and will drive us from this desert by the torturing pain of their prayers. Come! let us gather now against him, perhaps we will be able to drive him from this place." When the saint abbä Magäres heard this, he was fortified in his heart and full of power against the demons. He blessed God who had opened his ears so well that he had heard the design of the demons and learned their weakness. After that, the demons gathered against him, engaged in a violent combat against him, and lit a great fire at the door of his cell. They took burning (brands) and threw them into his cell; the burning (brands) were extinguished by the prayer of the saint abbä Magäres. When they were defeated by this (means), they put in his heart the thought of lust, the sadness of heart, suffering, pride, the love of vain glory, fear, boasting, laziness, blasphemy, lack of faith, despair regarding God, and what goes even beyond all that; they fought him with that, as the holy abbä 'Entonyos had told him.

When he had dwelt for long days (thus), the demons attacking him with these shameful acts, he rose again and went to the holy abbä 'Entonyos. When the holy abbä 'Entonyos saw him from afar, he spoke to his disciples and said: "This one (is) a true Israelite without guile. My sons, do you see this man: he will be a straight and great staff for many people and he will have tasty fruits from the mouth of the God of hosts." When (Magäres) was near the holy abbä 'Entonyos, he prostrated himself on the ground before him. Abbä 'Entonyos raised him quickly, embraced him, and kissed him, for he (= Magäres) had a changed face - like the face of someone who is sick - because of the violence of the combat that the demons were waging against him. After they had prayed and sat together, the holy abbä 'Entonyos spoke with cheerfulness and said to him: "Are you in good health, my son Magäres?" The saint Magäres answered him and said to him: "Behold, God has already revealed to you everything that has happened to me." Then ('Entonyos) taught him, fortified him, and said to him: "Be strong and do not fear, for it is necessary for us to endure thus all these trials that our enemies bring against us. Because of this, it is our duty to be masters for the many people who love the spiritual wisdom which is the monastic state." He said to him (again): "My son Magäres, remember the word that God said to you while you were going to draw water." When the saint abbä Magäres heard this, he was greatly astonished and knew that his actions and his secrets were revealed to the holy abbä

በመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወነበረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ አባ ፡ እንጦንዮስ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትባረክ ፡ እምኔሁ ፤ ወይትመሀር ፡ ትእዛዛቲሁ ። ወኀሠሠ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያልብሶ ፤ አስኬማ ፡ ቅዱሰ ፡ ወጸለየ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወአልበሶ ፡ ኪያሁ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ተሰምየ ፡ ረድኡ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ እንጦንዮስ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ አባ፣ እንጦንዮስ ፤ በኅቡእ ፡ ኢትትሀከይ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትምጻእ ፡ ኅቤየ ፡ ዝየ ፡ እስመ ፡ አነ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ሕዳጥ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ አሐውር ፡ ኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ወሰገደ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወሰአሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንበር ፡ በኀቤሁ ፡ ወይረስዮ ፡ ድልወ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንሣእ ፡ በረከቶ ፡ መንፈሳዊተ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አባ ፡ እንጦን ዮስ ፡ ንበር ፡ በኅቤየ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ በኀቤሁ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አባ ፡ እንጦንዮስ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ሕዳጥ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ያዐርፈከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እምብዝኃ” ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ሕሊና ፡ እኪት ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ይጸብኡከ ፡ ገሃደ ፡ በከመ ፡ ገብሩ ፡ ላዕሌየ ፡ ጽናዕ ፡ ወተዓቀብ ፡ ወኢታሕዝናኖ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ኪሩቤል ፡ ዘረሰዮ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ምስሌከ ፡ ለረድኤትከ ፡ ከመ ፡ የሀሉ ፤ ምስሌከ ፡ እስከ ፡ ተፍጻሜተ ፡ መዋዕሊከ ፡ ወየዐቅበከ'" ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዞ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አምላክነ ። ወወሀቦ” ፡ በትረ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ወተአምሣ ፡ በአ ምኃ ፡ ቅድሳት ፡ ወአዕረፈ ። ወቀበረ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ በመካን ፡ ኅቡእ ፡ ዘኢያአምር ፡ መኑሂ ፤
'Entonyos by the Holy Spirit. He dwelt near the old man abbä 'Entonyos for many days, being blessed by him and learning his instructions. He asked him to clothe him with the holy habit. ('Entonyos) prayed over him and clothed him with it. Because of this, (Magäres) was called a disciple of the holy abbä 'Entonyos. Then, the old man abbä 'Entonyos said to him in secret: "Do not have scruples about coming to me here, for I will go in a few days to God." When abbä Magäres heard this word, he rose, prostrated himself before him, and asked him to stay near him, and (that 'Entonyos) make him ready to receive his spiritual blessing. Abbä 'Entonyos said to him: "Stay near me," and he stayed near him. Abbä 'Entonyos said to him: "In a few days, God will give you rest far from these many evil thoughts. After that, (the demons) will attack you openly as they (did) against me; be strong, guard yourself, and do not grieve the cherub whom God has established with you to help you, so that he may be with you until the end of your days: he will guard you as the Lord our God has ordered him." ('Entonyos) gave him his staff, embraced him with a holy kiss, and died. (Magäres) buried his body in a hidden place whose location no one knows.
መካኖ ። ወተመይጠ ፡ ቅዱስ!" ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ አስቄጥስ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካኑ፤ቦ፤ ፣ ወተሰምዓ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ ውስተ ፤ ኩሉ ፡፤ አጽናፈ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወገብረ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዲበ ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ተአምራተ፤። ፡ ዓበይተ ። ወቦ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ እስመ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ አንጾኪያ ፡ ፈነዋ ፡ ለወለቱ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ዘይጹዓን ፣ ዲቤሃ ፣ መንፈስ ፡ ርኩስ ፡ ወበጽሐት ፡ ኀቤሁ!0 ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ ወሬዛ ። ወአእመረ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ወለት ፡ ወፈ ወሳ ፡ ወፈነዋ ፡ ኃበ ፤ አቡሃ፤0 ። ወሶበ ፡ ወሀብዎ ፡ ወርቀ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ኢነሥአ ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ። ወሀሎ ፡ ፳መነኮስ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ውሲም!፤" ፡ ስሕተ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ትንሣኤ ፡ ምውታን ፡ ወአስሐቶሙ ፡ ለብዙኃን ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ አሚኖቶሙም ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ይትወከፉ ፡ ቃሎ ። ወሖረ ፡ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ውሲም ፡ ኀበ ፡ አባ ፤ መቃርስ ፡ ወነገሮ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ አስሐቶሙ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ ለሕዝበ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ወሰአሎ ፡ ስእለተ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይርድኦ ። ወተንሥአ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ውሲም ፡ ወርእዮ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ባሕታዊ ፡ ዘውስቴቱ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ርኩስ፤" ። ወሶበ ፡ ተናገረ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ትንሣኤ፤‹ቦ ፡ አውሥኦ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ባሕታዊ ፣፤ ወይቤሎ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ኢየአምን ፡ ከመ ፡ ምውታን ፡ ይትነሥኡ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኢያንሣአከ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ እምውስተ ፡ መቃብር ። ወጸለየ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ ወሰአለ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡፤ ወተንሥአ፤ ፡ ፳ብእሲ ፣፤ እምውታን ፡፣ ወኮነ፤" ፡ እምከሓዲያን ፡ ቀደምት ። ወአምነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ባሕታዊ ፣ ወተመይጠ ፡ እምስሕተቱ ፡ ወከማሁ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ እለ ፡ አስሐቶሙ፣ ተመይጡ ። ወሰአሎ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘተንሥአ፤፤ ፡ እምውታን ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፣ መቃርስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያጥምቆ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ፣ ወአጥመቆ ፡ ወአልበሶ ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ ወነበረ ፣ በኀቤሁ ፡ ሄዓመተ ፡ ወአዕረፈ ።
The saint abbä Magäres returned to the desert of 'Asqêtes and dwelt in his habitation. His fame spread to all the ends of the earth and God performed great miracles by his hands. Among these, there is (this one): the prince of 'Ansokiyä (= Antioch) sent to him his daughter who had been seized by an impure spirit. She arrived near him in the guise of a young man. He recognized that she was a girl, cared for her, and sent her back to her father. When they (wanted) to give him much gold, he took nothing. There was a monk in the city of Wesim (= Ausim); he wandered and said: "There is no resurrection of the dead." He led many people into error and, because of their faith in him, they received his words. The bishop of the city of Wesim went to abbä Magäres, told him how the solitary had led his own people into error, and asked him with much insistence to help him. The saint abbä Magäres rose and went with the bishop to the city of Wesim; he saw the solitary in whom (was) the impure spirit. When he spoke with him about the resurrection, the solitary answered him and said to him: "For me, I will not believe that the dead rise if you do not raise for me a man from the tomb." The saint abbä Magäres prayed and implored God, and a man rose from the dead - he was of the ancient pagans. The solitary believed and turned away from his error; likewise, all the people he had led into error turned away (from it). The man who had been raised from the dead asked the saint abbä Magäres to baptize him with Christian baptism. He baptized him, clothed him with the monastic habit, and (the man) dwelt near him for six years and died.


[p. 424] {#po.46.p424}

After that, the saint abbä Magäres rose and penetrated into the inner desert to know if there had been people in the desert before him. He saw two naked men and was afraid of them, for he imagined that they were demons. They said before him the prayer "Our Father who (art) in heaven", called him by his name, and said to him: "Do not be afraid, Magäri." He recognized that they were holy hermits. They questioned him about the people of the world and their way of acting. He answered them and said to them: "God, in his mercy, takes care of everyone." Then he asked them if the cold of winter chilled them and (if) the heat of the sun at the time of summer burned them. They answered him and said to him: "God has taken care of us during these forty years that we have dwelt in this desert. The winter has not chilled us and the summer has not burned us." The saint abbä Magäres said to them: "How will I become like you?" They said to him: "Dwell in your cell, weep for your sins, and you will become like us." He was blessed by them and returned to his dwelling. When the monks multiplied near him, they dug a well for them and walled it for them. When the saint went down into it to wash himself, the demons made it collapse on him to kill him. The monks came and took him out of it.
ወአውጽእዎ ፡ እምኔሃ ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዕረፍቶ ፡ ፈነወ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ኪሩቤልሃ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ይሔውጸ፤፤ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ተደለው ፡ እስመ ፡'ንሕነ ፡ ንመጽእ ፡ ኀቤከ ፡ ወንነሥአከ፤፤ ። ወርእዮሙ ፡ ለአባ ፤ እንጦንዮስ ፤ ወለአባ ፣ ጳኩሚስ ፤ ወለማኅበረ፤፡" ፡ ቅዱሳን ፣ ወለኩሎሙ ፡ ኅይላት ፡ ሰማያዊያን ፡ እስከ ፡ መጠወ ፡ ነፍሶ ። ወኮነ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ሕይወቱ ፡ ፲ወ፪ ዓመተ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ስምዓ፤፤ ፡'አባ ፡ በምኖዳ፤= ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ርእየ ፥ ነፍሶ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ተዓርግ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወሰምዖሙ ፡ ለሰይጣናት ፡ ይኬልሑ ፡ ድኅሬሁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ሞእከነ፤ ፡ መቃሪ ፡ ሞእከነ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ቅዱስ፤፤ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ "ሀለውኩ ፡ ምስሌክሙ" ።፤ ። ወሶበ ፡ ቦአ፣ ቅዱስ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ!፤፤ ፡ ከልሑ ፡ በቃል ፡ ልዑል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ሞእአከነ ፡ ኦመቃርስ፤፤" ። ወይቤ ፡ ቅዱስ፤ ፡ ይትባረክ ፡ እግዚእየ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ፤። ፡ ዘአድኀነኒ ፤ እምእደዊክሙ፤፤ ። ወኮነ፤፤ ፡ አዘዞሙ ፡ ለውሉዱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኅብኡ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወመጽኡ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ 'እምሀገሩ ፡ ሳሱዊር፤ ፡ ወወሀቡ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ለረድኡ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ይጌሥጾ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ተዓቀብ ፡ እምአፍቅሮ ፡ ንዋይ ። ወመርሖሙ፤፤ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወአርአዮሙ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ መቃርዮስ፤ ፡ ወነሥእዎ ፡ ወወሰድዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገሮሙ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ በህየ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ መዋፅለ፤፦-፤› ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ሖሩ ፡ ውሉዱ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገሩ ፡ ሰሱዊር፤ ፡ ወፈቀዱ ፡ ከመ፣ ይንሥኡ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፤ ወተንሥኡ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ምስለ ፡ መኩ
When God wanted him to die, he sent him the cherub who visited him and (the latter) said to him: "Prepare yourself, for we are going to come to you and are going to take you." (Magäres) saw abbä 'Entonyos, abbä Päkuemis (= Pachomius) as well as the assembly of the saints and all the heavenly hosts until he gave up the ghost. All the days of his life were ninety-seven years. Abba Bamnodä (= Paphnutius) testified that he had seen the soul of the saint abbä Magäres go up to heaven and (that) he had heard the demons crying behind him, saying: "You have defeated us, Magäri, you have defeated us!" And the saint said to them: "Until now, I am (still) with you." When the saint penetrated into heaven, (the demons) cried out in a loud voice, saying: "You have defeated us, Magäres!" And the saint said: "Blessed be my Lord Jesus Christ who has saved me from your hands." He had prescribed to his (spiritual) sons to hide his body. The people of his city, Säsuwir, came and gave money to his disciple Yohannes (= John) whom the saint was rebuking all the time - he was saying to him: "Guard yourself from the love of money" -, and he guided them and showed them the body of the saint Magäryos. They took it, carried it to their city, and it remained there for many days. After that, his (spiritual) sons the monks went to his city, Sasuwir, and wanted to take the body of the saint.


[p. 426] {#po.46.p426}
ንን ፡ ወከልእዎሙ ። ወበይእቲ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ አስተርአዮ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፣ መቃርስ ፡ ለመኩንን ፤ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኅድገኒ ፤ እሖር ፤ ምስለ ፤ ውሉድየ ። ወበጽባሕ ፡ ጸውዖሙ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ለመነኮሳት ፡ ወአዘዞሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጹሩ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለአቡሆሙ ፡ ወጾርዎ፤፤ ፡ ወወሰድዎ ፡ ወአንበርዎ፤ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ቤተ፤0 ፣ ክርስቲያን ፣ በዝማሬ ፣ ወማኅሌት ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ አመ ፡ ፲ወ፪ለወርኃ ፡ ነሐሴ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ እምኔሁ ፤ ተኣምራተ ፤ ወመንክራተ ፡፤ ዐበይተ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስ ሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተሰቅለ ፣ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ በሥጋ ፡ በእንተ ፡ መድኃኒተ ፡ ዓለም ። ወጸልመት!፥፤ ፡ ፀሓይ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእየት ፡ ፈጣሪሃ ፡ ስቁለ ፡ በፈቃዱ ፡ ወምንጡሉዓ፤፡ ፡ 'ውስተ ፡ ሥጋ! ፡ ዘያስተርኢ ። ወኮነ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ጽልመታ ፡እምጊዜ ፡ሰዓት ፡እስከ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ዘፀ፪ሰዓት ። ወበውእቱ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ አጽነነ!፣ ርእሶ ፣ እግዚእነ ፤ በፈቃዱ ፤ ወመጠወ ፡፤ ነፍሶ ፡ ለባዊተ ፡ ነባቢተ ፡ ዘነሥኣ ፡ እምእግዝእትነ ፡ ቅድስት ፡፤ ድንግል ፤ ማርያም ። ወተፈልጠት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ነፍስ ፡ እምሥጋሁ ፡ ወሖረት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሲኦል ፡ እንበለ ፡ ፍልጠት ፡ እመለኮት ። በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ምውት ፡ በሥጋ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሕያው ፡ በመንፈስ ። ወበውእቱ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ስቁል ፡ ዲበ ፡ ዕፀ ፡ መስቀል ፡ እንበለ ፣ ፍልጠት ፡ እመለኮት ፡ ወከማሁ ፡ ነፍስ ፡ ወረደት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሲኦል ፡ ከመ ፤ ትፍትሖሙ ፥ ለሙቁሓን ፡ ወይእቲ! ፡ እንበለ ፡ ፍልጠት ፡ እመለኮት ። ወኮነ ፡ ልዑል ፡ መልዕልተ ፥ ሰማያት ፤ እንዘ ፡ ይነብር ፡ ምስለ ፣ አብ ፣ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ወክ ብር ፡ ወክሂል ፡ ዘቤዘወነ፤ ፡ በርእሱ ፡ ወአርኃወ ፡ ለነ ፡ ኖኅተ ፡ መንግሥቱ ፡ ዘሎቱ ፡
The inhabitants of the city rose against them with the governor and prevented them (from doing so). That night, the saint abbä Magäres appeared to the governor and said to him: "Let me go with my sons." In the morning, the governor called the monks and gave them a mandate to take the body of their (spiritual) father. They took it, carried it away, and put it in the church with many chants and canticles on the nineteenth of the month of nahasë. Great miracles and wonders occurred from it. May the prayer and the blessing of this great saint abbä Magäres be with us, amen. On this day also, our Lord Jesus Christ - glory to him! - was crucified according to the flesh for the salvation of the world. The sun darkened when it saw its creator suspended by his will and wrapped in visible flesh. The time when it was darkened was from the sixth hour until the ninth hour. At that moment, our Lord inclined his head by his will and gave up his reasonable (and) intelligent soul which he had received from our Lady the holy Virgin Mary. This soul separated from his body and went to hell without separation from the divinity, as the apostle Päwlos (= Paul) says: He is dead in the flesh, he is living in the spirit. At that moment, the body was suspended on the wood of the cross without separation from the divinity. Likewise, the soul descended into hell to deliver the chained while it was without separation from the divinity. (Our Lord) (was) raised above the heavens, seated with the Father and the Holy Spirit. Glory, honor, and power to him who has redeemed us by his person and has opened for us the door of his kingdom, to whom are appropriate glory and adoration in the centuries, amen!
ይደሉ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ወሰጊድ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ ክላሌ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ መፍቀሬ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ገላውዴዎስ ። ወውእቱ ፡ ተሐፅነ ፡ በተግሣጸ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ርቱዓ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ልብነ ፡ ድንግል ። ወሶበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ አንገሦ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ፳ተንበላታዊ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ግራኙ ፡ ዘሰመየ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ዘኢድልወቱ ። ወአእመነት ፡ ሰብአ ፤ ኢትዮጵያ ፡ በኀይሉ ፣ ወአስተሳተፎሙ ፡ በሃይማኖቱ ፡ ወእለ ፡ ተርፉ ፡ በክርስትና ፣ ረሰዮሙ ፣ መስተገብራነ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወነሠተ ፡ አብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናት ፤ ወፄወወ ፡ መብዝጎኅታ ፡ ለኢትዮጵያ ፡ ወሜጣ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዘፈቀደ ። ወይቤ ፣ እምይእዜሰ ፡ አልቦ ፤ ዘይትቃወመኒ ፡ ቀነይክዎን ፡ ለኩሎን ፡ አህጉር ። ወእምዝ ፡፤ አንሥኦ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡፤ ገላውዴዎስ ፤ ወአኀዘ ፡ ይትቃተል ፡ ምስለ ፡ ዐበይት ፡ መኳንንቲሁ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ዕልው ፡ ወሞኦሙ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ግራፒ ፡ ተቄጥዓ ፡፣ ወመጽአ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አእላፍ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ አፍራስ ፡ ወትርኩ ፣ ወተፃብኡ ፡ ወቀተሎ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፤ በእዴሁ ፡ ወአጥፍኦ ። ወተመይጡ ፡ ፄውዋን ፡ ወተሐንፃ ፡ አብያተ ፤ ክርስቲያናት ፤ ዘተመዝበራ ፡ ወረትዓት ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ።
[On this day also, King Galäwdëwos (= Claudius) friend of God was definitively crowned with martyrdom. He had been raised in the discipline of his father, the king with the right faith Lebna Dengel. When his father died, God made him king while there was a Muslim by the name of Grägni who called himself king, which did not suit him. He had brought the people of Ethiopia to his faith by his power and had associated them with his faith. As for those who had remained in the Christian faith, he made them workers for him. He destroyed the churches, took the greater part of Ethiopia prisoner, and sold it to whom he wanted. He said: "Henceforth, no one will resist me, I have subdued all the cities." After that, God raised up King Galäwdëwos; (the latter) began to wage war against the great dignitaries of this impious one and defeated them. When Grägni learned (of it), he became furious and came toward him with tens of thousands of horsemen and Turks. They fought and God killed him by his (= Galäwdëwos') hand and destroyed him. The captives returned, the churches which
ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ጸእምተንበላት ፡ ምስለ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ፀብእ ፡ ወተራከቦ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ገላውዴዎስ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ውሑዳን ፡ ሰብእ ። ወይቤልዎ ፡ ንትገኃሥ ፡ ወኢንትቃተል ፡ እስከ ፡ ይመጽኡ ፡ መኳንንተ ፡ ፀብእነ ። ኢይትገኃሥ ፡ ወኢይሬኢ ፡ ተፄውዎተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወሙስናሆን ፡ ለአብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ቦአ ፡ ማእከለ ፡ ጸብእ ፡ ወጸንዓ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትቃተሎሙ ፡ ወድኅረኒ ፡ ዐገትዎ ፣ ኩሎሙ ፡፥ ተንበላት ፡ ወዘበጥዎ ፡ በአስይፍት ፡ ወረገዝዎ ፡ በብዙኅ ፡ አርማሕ ፤ ወአውደቅዎ ፡ እመልዕልተ ፡ ፈረሱ ፡ ወአዕረፈ ። ወመተሩ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ክቡረ ፡፣ ወወሰድዎ ። በረከቱ ፡ ቅድስት ፤ ወሞገሰ ፡ ስምዑ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
had been devastated were (re)built, and the faith of Christ - glory to him! - was restored. After that, one among the Muslims came with many men of war to meet King Galäwdëwos while (the latter) was with few men. (The soldiers of the latter) said to him: "Let us withdraw and not fight until our army chiefs have arrived." "I will not withdraw nor will I see the captivity of the Christians and the ruin of the churches." Saying (this), (Galäwdëwos) entered into the middle of the combat and remained firm in fighting them (= the Muslims). After (that), all the Muslims surrounded him, struck him with swords, pierced him with many spears, and made him fall from his horse, and he died. They cut off his glorious head and carried it away. May his holy blessing and the
grace of his martyrdom be with Yohannes (= John) in the centuries of centuries, amen.]

### READING FOR THE 28TH OF MAGGABIT

In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the saint Quastantinos (= Constantine), the righteous emperor. The name of the father of this righteous one was Qonstä (= Constantius), which means "green", and the name of his mother, 'Eléni (= Helena). Qonstä his father had reigned over the country of Barnatyä (= Britain); Maksemyänos (= Maximian) had reigned over the country of Romyä (= Rome) and Diyogletyänos (= Diocletian) had reigned over the city of 'Ansokiyä (= Antioch) and the country of Egypt. This Qonstä was a pagan, of the sentiment of the Greeks, but he was good and without malice; he loved to do good and (there was) in him pity and mercy. He went to the city of Rohä (= Edessa), saw 'Eleni, and married her. She was a Christian, she conceived this emperor Quastantinos. Then, his (= Quastantinos') father Qonstä left 'Eleni in the city of Rohä and returned to the country of Barnatyä. She gave birth to this saint Quastantinos and gave him a good education; she taught him all the sciences, she sowed in his heart mercy and pity for the people of the Christians. She did not dare to have him baptized with Christian baptism and did not tell him that she was a Christian. He grew up and became skilled in the (art) of riding horses. He left the city of Rohä and went to his father. His father rejoiced because of him when he saw him full of wisdom and science and skilled in the (art) of riding horses. His father made him emperor and crowned him with the imperial crown. Two years later, his father died and (Quastantinos) received the entire empire. He exercised justice and law and removed from the people the servitudes to which the emperors who had
ወተአዘዙ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ ወአፍቀርዎ ፡ ወገረሩ ፡ ሎቱ ። ወተሰምዓ ፡ ዜና ፤ ኩነኔ ፡ ፍትሕ ፡ ዘይገብር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አህጉር ። ወፈነዉ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ልሂቃነ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ሮሜ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይስእልዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምጻእ ፡ ኀቤሆሙ ፡ ወይባልሖሙ ፡ እምግብርናተ፤ ፣ መክስሚያኖስ ። ወሶበ ፡ አንበበ ፡ መጽ ሐፈ ፡ መልእክቶሙ ፡ ሐዘነ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡ ዘበጽሐ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ። ወኮነ ፡ ይሔሊ!፤ ፡ ወይቴክዝ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ዘይገብር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይባልሖሙ ፡ እምሐዘኖሙ ። ወእንዘ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይነብር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዐውደ ፡ ምኩናን ፡ በመንፈቀ ፡ መዓልት ፡ አስተርአየ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ መስቀል?፤፤ ፡ ወዲቤሁ ፡ ጽሑፍ ፡ በልሳነ ፡ ዮናናዊያን ፡ ኒከስከጣጦን ፡ ዘበትርጓሜሁ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ትመውእ" ። ወአንከረ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ብርሃኖ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ መስቀል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ከደነ፤ ፡ ብርሃነ ፡ ፀሓይ ፡ ወሐለየ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘጽሑፍ ። ፡ ዲቤሁ ፡ ወአስተርአዮሙ ፡ ኪያሁ ፡ ለሊቃነ ፡ ሐራሁ ፤ ወለልሂቃነ ፤ መንግሥቱ ፡ ወአንከሩ ፡ ወኢያእመሩ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ምንት ፡ አስተርአየ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መስቀል ። ወበይእቲ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ አስተርአየ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ መልአከ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለቄስጠንጢኖስ ፡ በራእይ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ግበር፤ ፡ ትእምርተ ፡ አምሳለ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ትእምርት ፡ ዘርኢኩ ፡ በመንፈቀ ፡፣ መዓልት ፡ ወባቲ ፡ ትመውሥሙ ፡ ለጸላእትከ ። ወሶበ ፡ ነቅሃ ፣ እምንዋሙ ፡ ፀንዓ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ወገብረ ፡ መስቀለ ፡ ዘወርቅ ፡ ወረሰዮ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ መንግሥቱ ፡ ወአዘዘ" ፡ ከመ ፡ ይግበሩ ፡ መስቀላተ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ሐቅል ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ በከመ" ፡ አዘዘ ። ወኮነ ፣ ዝንቱ ፡ አመ ፡ ሳብዕ ፡ ዓመተ ፤ መንግሥቱ ፡ በሀገረ ፡ በርነጥያ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አስተጋብኦሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ሰራዊቱ ፡ ወወፅአ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይባልሖሙ ፡ ለሰብአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ሮሜ ። ወተሰምዓ ፡ ዜና ፡ ኅበ ፡ መክስምያኖስ ፡ ወገብረ ፡ ተንከተመ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ወዐደወ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ቁስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትቃተል ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ተጻብኡ፥፤ ፡ ወተቃተሉ ። ወኮነ ፡ 'ሶበ ፡ ቀደመ ፡ ትእምርተ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ይትመውኡ ፡ ሰራዊቱ ፡ ለመክሲምያኖስ ። ወቀተሉ ፡ ሰራዊተ፥፤ ፡ ቁስጠንጢኖስ ፣ እምሰራዊቱ ፣ ለመክስምያ ኖስ ፡ ብዙኃነ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ዘአልቦ ፤ ጐልቄ ፡ ወጐየ?፥ ፡ መክስምያኖስ ፡ ወእለ ፡ ተርፉ ፡ እምሰራዊቱ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወተጽዕነ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ተንከተም ፡ ከመ ፡ ይባእ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ሮሜ ፡ ወተንሕደ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ተንከተም ፡ ወተሰጥመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወሰራዊቱ ፡ በከመ ፡ ፈርዖን ፡ ወሰራዊቱ ። ወቦአ ፡ ቁስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ሮሜ ፡ ወተቀበልዎ?” ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ዚአሃ ፡ በመኃትው ፡ ወበዕንዚራት ፡ ወአክሊላት ፡ ዘስርግው ፡ በዕንቄ ፡ ባሕርይ ። ወኮኑ ፡ ጠቢባን ፡ ወማእምራን ፡ ይዌድስዎ፥ ፡ ለመስቀል ፡ ክቡር ፡
(reigned) before him had forced them. All the people obeyed him, loved him, and submitted to him. The reputation of the righteous judgment that he rendered spread to all the countries. The notables of the city of Rome sent word to him, asking him to come to them and to deliver them from the slavery of Maksemiyänos (= Maxentius). When Quastantinos read their letter, he was distressed because of the misfortune that was happening to them. He was preoccupied and worried about what he would do to deliver them from their pain. While he was sitting at the praetorium, in the middle of the day, a cross appeared to him; on it, (it was) written in the language of the Greeks Nikaskatäton, which, translated, (means) "by this you will conquer". He was astonished when he saw the light of this cross having covered the light of the sun and reflected on what (was) written on it. He showed it to his generals and to his ministers; they were astonished and did not know why this cross had appeared. That night, the angel of God appeared to Quastantinos in a vision and said to him: "Make a sign in the likeness of the sign that you saw in the middle of the day and, by it, you will conquer your enemies." When he awoke from his sleep, his heart was fortified; he (had) a cross of gold made, put it above his imperial crown, and ordered crosses to be put on all the weapons; they did as he (had) ordered. This took place in the seventh year of his reign in the country of Barnatyä. Then, he gathered all his troops and left to deliver the people of the city of Romë. The news (of it) reached Maksemyänos; (the latter) made (a) bridge over the river and crossed toward Quastantinos to fight him. After that, they engaged in battle and fought each other. When the sign of the cross advanced, the troops of Maksimyänos were defeated. The troops of Quastantinos killed many people - innumerable - of the troops of Maksemyänos. Maksemyänos fled - and with him what remained of his troops - and mounted the bridge to enter the city of Romë. The bridge collapsed with him and he was engulfed, he and his troops, like Pharaoh and his troops. Quastantinos entered the city of Romë; its inhabitants came to meet him with torches, musical instruments, and crowns adorned with precious stones. The wise and the learned celebrated the glorious Cross and called it the savior of their city. Then they held a great feast for the glorious Cross for seven days.

When Quastantinos reigned in the city of Romë, he was baptized with Christian baptism, he and all his army, by the hand of the saint Salpëtros (= Sylvester), patriarch of the city of Romë, the eleventh year of his reign (which) is the fourth year after the glorious Cross had appeared to him. He reigned with a Christian royalty. He sent word throughout his empire and

ክርስቲያናዊት ፤ መሲሐዊት ፡ ወፈነወ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ መንግሥቱ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፍትሕዎሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ሙቁሓን ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያክብርዎሙ ፡ ለክርስቲያን። ፡ ወኢያኅሥርዎሙ ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ የሀብዎሙ ፡ ለካህናት ፣ ኩሎ ፡፣ ንዋየ ፡ አብያተ ፡ ጣዖታት ፡፣ ወምድሮሙ ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሰለጡ ፡ መኳንንት ፣ መሲሐዊያን ፡ ወይሠየሙ ፡ላዕለ ፡: ሕዝበ ፡ አረማዊያን ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይግበር ፡ መኑሂ ፡ ግብረ ፡ በሰሙነ ፡ ሕማማት ፡ ወዘእምድኅሬሁ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዙ ፡ ሐዋርያት ። ወእምዝ ፡ ፈነዋ ፡ ለእሌኒ ፡ እሙ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ከመ ፡ ትሕትት ፡ በእንተ ፡ ፅፀ ፡ መስቀል ፤ ማሕየዊ ፡ ዘቦቱ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ረድ ኤቱ ፡ ወመዊኦቱ ። ወአመ ፡ ፲ ወዷዓመተ ፡ መንግሥቱ ፡ አስተጋብአ ፡ ማኅበረ ፡ ጉባኤ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ፻፻፲ ወ ፳ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆኦሳት ። ወተሠርዓ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ሥርዓተ ፡ መሲሓዊያን ፡ በሠናይ ፡ ሥርዓት ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሐለየ ፡ 'በሀገረ ፡ በርነጥያ ፡ ወሐነጸ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ዓባየ ፡ ወሰመያ ፡ ቀስጥንጥንያ ፡ በስሙ!" ፡ ወሐነጸ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ በስመ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ድንግል ፡ ማርያም ፡ ወሰመያ ፡ አጊያሶፍያ ። ወአሰርገዋ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ስርግው ፡ ሥጋዊ ፡ ወመንፈሳዊ ፡ እስመ ፡ አስተጋብአ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡
He ordered the release of all who were imprisoned because of the faith in our Lord Jesus Christ. He also ordered that Christians be honored and not insulted. He ordered that all the goods of the idol temples and their lands be given to the priests. He ordered that Christian officers be invested with power and placed above the pagans. He ordered that no one perform any task during Passion Week and the week that follows it, as the apostles prescribed. Then he sent Eleni his mother to Jerusalem to search for the wood of the life-giving Cross by which he had had help and victory. In the seventeenth year of his reign, he gathered the assembly of the council of the three hundred and eighteen holy bishops. All the institutions of the Christians were fixed there according to a beautiful order. Then he occupied himself with the city of Barnatyä, built a great city, and called it Questentenyä (= Constantinople) after his name. He built there a great church in the name of our Lady the holy Virgin Mary and called it 'Agiyäsofyä (= Hagia Sophia); he adorned it with all material and spiritual ornaments: he gathered there the bodies of many apostles, martyrs, and saints.
ሥጋሆሙ ፡ ለብዙኃን ፡ እምሐዋርያት ፡ ወሰማዕታት ፡ ወቅዱሳን ። ወሶበ ፡ ተጋደለ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ተጋድሎ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ሐመ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ በሀገረ ፡ ኒቆምድያ ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ ባቲ ። ወገነዝዎ ፡ ወአንበርዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሣፁን ፡ ዘወርቅ ፡፣ ወጾርዎ ፡ ወአብጽሕዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፣፡ ሦስጥንጥንያ ። ወወፅኡ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ወካህናት ፡ ወኩሎሙ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡፤ ወተቀበልዎ ፡ ወገነዝዎ ፡ በጸሎታት ፤ ወበዝማሬያት ፡ ወማኅሌ ታት ፡ መንፈሳዊት ። ወአንበርዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ መቅደስ ፡ ዘሐዋርያት ፡ ቅዱሳን ። ወኮነ ፡፤ ኩሉ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ሕይወቱ ፡ ፳ወ፳ዓመት ። ወእምቅድመ ፡ ይንግሥ ፡ ወወ፻ዓመት ፡ ወዲበ ፤ መንበረ ፡ መንግሥቱ ፡ ጠወ፪ዓመት ። ወእምዓመት' ፡ ዛ፻ ወቿ፻8 ወ8ዓመት ፡ ለፍጥረተ ፡ ዓለም ። በረከቱ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ወስብሐት ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ለዓለም ፤ አሜን ።
When this (emperor) had fought a beautiful (spiritual) fight, he had a small illness in the city of Niqomedyä (= Nicomedia) and died in it. They buried him, placed him in a golden coffin, carried him away, and brought him to the city of Questentenyä. The patriarch went out with the priests and all the people, they came to meet him, buried him with prayers, canticles, and spiritual songs, and placed him in the sanctuary of the holy apostles. All the days of his life were sixty-five years, thirty-three years before reigning and thirty-two years on his imperial throne, from the year 5834 of the era of the creation of the world. May the blessing of this just emperor be with us and glory to our Lord Christ forever, amen!
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ ይስሐቅ ፡ ወያዕቆብ ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሮሙ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፤ ወፍቅርቶሙሂ ፡ ኮቶሊካዊት ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day also, there are 'Abrehäm (= Abraham), Yeshaq (= Isaac), and Yä'qob (= Jacob). May their prayer and their blessing be with their dear 'Atnätëwos and also their dear Kotolikäwit in the centuries of centuries, amen.]

### READING FOR THE 29TH OF MAGGABIT
አመ ፡ ፳፪8ለመጋቢት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ዕለት ፡ኮነ ፡ በዓል ፡ 'በዘ ፡ ዜነዋ፤ ፡ እንተ ፡ ምልእት ፡ መድኃኒተ ፡ ዘአብሰራ፣ መልአክ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ገብርኤል ፡ ለእግዝእትነ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ድንግል" ፡ ማርያም ፡ በከመ ፣ ኮነ ፡ ስምዓ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ወበሳድስ ፡ ወርኅ ፡ ዝብሂል ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ፅንሰታ ለኤልሳቤጥ ፡ በሳድስ ፡ ወርኅ ፡ ተፈነወ ፡ ገብርኤል ፣ መልአክ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day, there is the feast which is full of salvation, which the glorious angel Gabr'el (= Gabriel) announced to our Lady the holy Virgin Mary while he was speaking to her, as the holy Gospel is witness to this, saying: In the sixth month, that is to say, the sixth month after 'Élsäbët (= Elizabeth) had conceived,


[p. 434] {#po.46.p434}
እምኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገር ፡ እምደወለ ፡ ገሊላ ፡ እንተ ፡ ስማ ፡ ናዝሬት ፡ ኀበ ፡ ድንግል ፡ እንተ ፡ ተፍኅረት ፡ ለብእሲ ፡ ዮሴፍ ፡ ዘእምቤተ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ወስማ ፡ ለድንግል ፡ ማርያም ። ወሶበ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ኀቤሃ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ይቤላ ፡ ተፈሥሒ ፡ ኦምልእተ፣ ጸጋ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ምስሌኪ ። ወሶበ ፡ ደንገፀት ፡ እምቃሉ ፡ ወተአምጥቶ ፡ላቲ፤" ፣ ይቤላ ፡ ኢትፍርሂ ፡ ኦማርያም ፡ እስመ ፡ ረከብኪ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ በኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወአ ንቲ ፡ ትፀንሲ ፡ ወትወልዲ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ወይሰመይ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ወይከውን ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወይሰመይ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ልዑል ። ወይሁቦ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አምላክ ፡ መንበረ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ አቡሁ ፡፤ ወይነግሥ ፡'ላዕለ ፡ ቤተ፤! ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ እስከ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ማኅለቅተ ፡ ለመንግሥቱ ። ወአውሥአቶ ፡ ድንግል ፡ ወትቤሎ ፡ እፎ ፡ ይከውነኒ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዘብሂል ፡ እፎ ፡ እፀንስ ፡ ወአንሰ ፡ ኢያአምር ፡ ብእሴ ፡ ግሙራ ። ወአውሥኣ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ወይቤላ ፥ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ላዕሌኪ ፡ ወኀይለ ፡ ልዑል ፡ ይጹልለኪ ። ዘብሂል ፡ እስመ፣ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ይከውነኪ፤፤ ፡ አምሳለ ፡ ዘርአ ፡ ሰብእ! ። ወዘይትወለድ፤ ፡ እም ኔኪ ፤ ቅዱስ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወይሰመይ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወእምዝ ፡ ወሀባ ፡ ትእምርተ ፡ በእንተ ፡ አሚነ ፡ ብስራቱ ፡ ላቲ ። ወይቤላ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ኤልሳቤጥኒ ፡ ዘመድኪ ፣ ፅንስት ፡ ይእቲ ፤ በልህቃቲሃ ፤ ወርሥአቲሃ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ እንተ ፡ ትሰመይ ፡ መካን ። ዘብሂል ፤ ለእመ ፡ ኮንኪ ፡ ታስተዓፅቢ'!፤ ፡ ፀኒሰ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ዘርእ፤ ፡ 'ወአንቲ ፡ ውርዙት ፡ ወድልውት ፡ ለተወክፎ ፡ ዘርእ ፡ እስመ ፡ ዘየዐቢ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ ፅንሰታ ፡ ለመካን ፤ እንተ ፡ ልህቀት ፡ ወረሥዓት!፤ ፡ እስመ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ነገር ፡ ዘይሰአኖ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወበጊዜሃ ፡ አውሥአቶ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ወትቤሎ ፡ ናሁ ፡ አነ ፡ ዓመቱ፣
Gabr'ël the angel was sent by God to a city of the region of Galilà (= Galilee) by the name of Nazrêt (= Nazareth) to a virgin who was betrothed to a man, Yosëf (= Joseph), who (was) of the house of Däwit (= David). The name of the virgin (was) Mary. When the angel entered to her, he said to her: "Rejoice, O full of grace, God is with you." As she was troubled by his word and the greeting he had addressed to her, he said to her: "Do not fear, Mary, for you have found grace with God. You will conceive and you will give birth to a son, he will be called by the name of Jesus. He will be great and will be called Son of the Most High. The Lord God will give him the throne of Däwit his father, and he will reign over the house of Yä'qob (= Jacob) forever, and there will be no end to his reign." The virgin answered him and said to him: "How will this happen to me, that is to say, how will I conceive, while I do not know a man at all?" The angel answered her and said to her: "The Holy Spirit will come upon you and the power of the Most High will overshadow you - that is to say, the Holy Spirit will be for you in the likeness of the seed of man; he who will be born of you will be holy and will be called Son of God." Then he gave her a sign to (show) the truth of what he had announced to her and said to her: "Behold, 'Élsäbet, your relative, is pregnant in her old age and her advanced years, she who was called sterile - that is to say: if you find it astonishing to conceive without seed, while you are young and fit to receive seed, surely the pregnancy of a sterile (woman) who is aged and old is greater than that, for there is no thing that is impossible to God." Then, Mary answered him and said to him: "Behold, I (am) the servant of God; may it happen to me according to what you have said to me."
ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ይኩነኒ ፡ በከመ ፡ ትቤለኒ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተወክፈት ፡ ማርያም ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ፅንሰ ፡ መለኮታዊ ፡ ወረደ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ዋሕድ ፡ ሀላዌ ፡ ቃለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ 8እምኮህላዊ ፡ ዘህልው ፡ ለዓለም፤' ፡ ወኃደረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ከርሣ ፡ ለድንግል ፡ ማርያም ፡ ኅድረተ ፡ ዘኢይትዓወቅ ፡ ለእጓለ ፡ እመሕያው ፡ እፎ ፤ ውእቱ ። ወነሥአ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ትስብእተ ፣፤ ፍጽምተ ፡፤ ዘኢይከውን ፣ እምድኅሬሁ ፡ ፍልጠት ፡ ግሙራ ። ወኮነ ፡ መለኮቱ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ትስብእቱ ፡ ፳ዘእንበለ" ፡ ፍልጠት ፡ ወኢትድምርት ፡ ወኢውላጤ ። ዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ በኩረ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ በዓ ላት ፡፤ ወቦቱ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ጥንተ ፡ መድኃኒተ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
When Mary received this divine pregnancy, the only-begotten Son, the person (helläwë) of the Word of God, who is one of the three persons existing forever, descended, dwelt in the womb of the Virgin Mary with a dwelling whose mode is not known to men, and then took from her a complete human nature from which, after that, he never separated. His divinity became one with his human nature without separation nor confusion nor mixture. This day constituted the firstfruits of all feasts; in it is the beginning of the salvation of the world. Glory to our Lord Jesus Christ in the centuries of centuries, amen!
ወበዛቲ ፡ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ተፍጻሜተ ፡ መድኃኒት ፡ በትንሣኤ ፡ ቅድስት ። እስመ፣ እግዚእነ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ሶበ ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ ኪነ ፡ ጥበቡ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ በመጠነ ፡ ቋወ፻ዓመት ፡ ወሐመ ፡ በፈቃዱ ፡ አመ ፡ ፳ወጻለዝንቱ ፡ ወርኃ ፡ መጋቢት ፡ ወተን ሥአ ፡ እምውታን ፡ በከመ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አመ ፡ ፳ወ፪ለዝንቱ ፡ ወርኅ ፡ ከመ ፡ዛቲ ፤ ፅለት ፡ ዘዜነዎሙ ፡ ለሰብአ ፡ ዓለም ፡ በትስብእቱ ፡ ዘኮነ ፣ ይጸንሕዎ፤፤ ። ከማሁ ፡ ካዕበ ፡፤ በይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ፤ ተፈሥሑ ፡ ሕያዋን ፡፤ ወምውታን ፡ በድቲን። ፡ እምሲኦል ፡
On this day also, there is the completion of salvation by the holy Resurrection when our Lord - glory to him! - had completed the operation of his wisdom on earth in the space of thirty-three years and had suffered by his will on the 27th of this month of maggäbit, he rose from the dead on the same (day) as today - the 29th of this month -, the same day on which (the angel) announced his incarnation to the inhabitants of the world who were waiting for him. Likewise also, on this day, the living and the dead rejoiced to be saved from hell (and) from the


[p. 436] {#po.46.p436}
እምእዴሁ፤ ፡ለጸላኢነ ፡ ሰይጣን ። ወለእመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ድቲነ ፡ እምሲኦል ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ዓርብ ፡ አላ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ እሑድ ፡ ጠየቁ" ፡ ሕያዋን ፡ ዘንተ ፣ ወአእመሩ ፡ ትንሣኤ ሆሙ ፡ በትንሣኤ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡፤ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዝውእቱ ፤ በሹሮሙ ፡'በትንሣኤ ፤ እምውታን ። በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ እስመ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ በኩረ ፡ ለምውታን ፡ በትንሣኤሁ ፡ ቅድስት ። በእንተ ፡ ዕበየ ፡ ሣህሉ ፤ ወምሕረቱ ፡ ዘሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ እስከ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡ ወአሜን ።
hand of our enemy Satan. If the deliverance from hell took place on Friday, it is on the other hand on Sunday that the living were assured of this and knew their resurrection by the resurrection of the body of our Lord Christ who is the firstborn among them by his resurrection from the dead, as the apostle Päwlos (= Paul) says: Christ has been the firstborn for the dead by his holy resurrection. It is by the greatness of his mercy and his pity that to him (belongs) the glory in the centuries of centuries, amen and amen.
ወበዛቲ ፡፤ ዕለት ፡ ሰበከ ፤ ጳውሎስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ወሪቆን ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ ተፍጻሜተ” ፡ ምድር ። በረከቱ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ወሀብተ ፡ ረድኤቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day, Päwlos (= Paul) the apostle preached in the city of Warigon which is the end of the earth. May his holy blessing and the gift of his help be with his dear 'Atnätëwos in the centuries of centuries, amen.]

### READING FOR THE 30TH OF MAGGABIT
አመ ፡ ለመጋቢት ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ይደልወነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንግበር ፡ በዓለ ፡ ለመልአክ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ገብርኤል ። በእንተ ፡ዕበየ ፡ ክብሩ፤ ፡ በኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ደለዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጹር ፡ ዜና ፡ ወልዱ፣፤ ኀበ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ወላዲቱ ። 'ወበእንተ ፡ ዘተሣሀለ" ፡ ላዕሌነ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በዛቲ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ወክብርት ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ወይደልወነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንክብሮ ፡ ወንወድሶ ። ወውእቱ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ዘዜነዎ ፡ ለዳንኤል ፡ በእንተ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ይጹሊ ፡ ወይስእል ፡ በእንተ ፤ ተመይጦቶሙ ፡ ለሕዝብ ፡ እምፄዋዌ ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ ምጽ አቱ ፡ ለመድኃኒነ ፡ አስተርአየ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወወሰነ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ሱባኤያቶ ፡ ወከሠተ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ በተፍጻጸሜሆሙ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ንጽሖሙ ፡ ለንጹሓን ፡ ወይቀትልዎ ፡ ወኢይከውና ፡ ለኢየሩሳሌም ፡ መድኅነ ፡ እምድኅሬሁ ። ወእምድኅሬሁ ፡ ይሠዐሩ ፡ መሥዋዕት ፡ ወሞርባናት ፡ ለደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ። 'ወበእንተ ፡ ዘረሰዮ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፤ መልአክ ፡፤ ድልወ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይዜንዋ ፡ ለእግዝእትነ ፡ ማርያም ፡ በእንተ ፤ መድኃኒተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዓለም ፤ ወበእንተዝ ፡ ይደልወነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንግበር ፡ ሎቱ ፤ በዓለ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ወንስአሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይተንብል ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይባልሐነ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እምእዴሁ" ፡ ለሰይጣን ፡ ጸላኢነ ፡ ወይርድአነ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ምግባራቲነ ። ትንብልናሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ገብርኤል ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ወስብሐት ፤
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day, it is fitting that we celebrate a feast for the glorious angel Gabr'ël (= Gabriel). Because of the greatness of his glory before God, it fell to him to bring the news of his Son to Mary his mother; and because God had pity on us with this great and glorious grace through the mediation of this angel, it is fitting that we honor him and render glory to him. It is he also who informed Dän'ël about Christ when he was praying and imploring for the return of the people from captivity and for the coming of our Savior. He appeared to him, fixed the weeks for him, and revealed to him that, at their expiration, Christ, purity of the pure, would come, (that) they would kill him, and (that) there would no longer be after him a savior for Jerusalem. After that, the sacrifices and offerings of the sons of Israel would be abolished. Because our Lord Christ made this angel worthy to announce to our Lady Mary the salvation of the whole universe, for this (reason) therefore, it is fitting that we make a feast for him at all times and that we ask him to intercede for us so that God may deliver us from the hand of Satan our enemy and help us in all our actions. May the intercession of this glorious angel Gabr'ël be with us and glory to our Lord Jesus Christ in the centuries of centuries, amen!
ለእግዚእነ ፣፤ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለሳምሶን ፡ ፳መስፍን ፣ እመሳፍንተ ፡ ደቂቀፆ ፡ እስራኤል ። ለዝንቱ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ለአቡሁ ፡ መኖሄ!፤" ፡ እምነገደ ፡ ዳን ፡ ወኮ ነት ፡ እሙ ፡ መካነ ። ወመጽአ ፡ ኅቤሃ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወዜነዋ ፡ በልደቱ ፡ ወአዘዛ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትርሐቅ ፡ እምርስሐት፤! ፡ ወእምበሊዓ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ወእምሰትየ ፡ ወይን፤፡ ፡
On this day also, (there is) the commemoration of Samson (= Samson), one of the Judges of the children of Israel. The name of the father of this just man was Manohë (= Manoah), of the tribe of Dän (= Dan); his mother was sterile. The angel of God came to her, announced to her his (= Samson's) birth, and


[p. 438] {#po.46.p438}
ዘያሰክር ፤እንዘ ፡ ሀለወት ፡ ፅንስት ፡ እስከ ፡ አመ ፡ ትወልዶ! ፡ ወኢትላጺ ፡ ሥዕርተ ፡ ርእሱ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይከውን ፡ ብፅዓተ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወበእደዊሁ ፡ ይከውን ፡ ድኅነቶሙ ፡ ለደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ እምሰብአ ፡ ፍልስጥኤም ። ወሶበ ፡ ነገረቶ ፡ ለምታ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ዜነዋ! ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሰአለ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ያርእዮ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ መልአክ ። ወአስተርአየ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ አዝዛ ፡ ለብእሲትከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትዕቀብ ፡ ዘአዘዝክዋ ። ወፀንሰት ፡ ወወለደቶ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ ወባረኮ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወወረደ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ መንፈሰ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ።
ordered her to keep herself away from defilement, from the ingestion of meat, and from the absorption of wine that intoxicates when she would be pregnant until the moment when she would give birth to him; (he also ordered her) not to shave his hair, for he would be vowed to God and, by his hands, the children of Israel would be saved from the people of Felset'êm (= Palestine). When she told her husband what the angel of God had announced to her, he asked God to let him see the angel. The angel of God appeared to him and said to him: "Order your wife to observe what I have ordered her." She conceived and gave birth to this just man. God blessed him and the grace of the spirit of God descended upon him.

Once, he rose against a lion and tore it into two parts. Another time, he killed eighty men among the people of Felset'êm and burned their harvests. The people of Felset'êm rose against the children of Yehudä (= Judah) to fight them and (so) that they would deliver Samson into their hands. The children of Yehudä told Somson what the people of Felset'êm had told them. Somson said to the children of Yehudä: "Swear to me that you will deliver me into their hands and (that) you will not kill me." When they had sworn (it) to him, he delivered himself to them. They bound him with two iron chains and delivered him to the people of Felset'êm; these rose against him to kill him. The spirit of strength descended from God upon him and he broke the iron chains like a cotton thread that has been burned by fire. After that, he found a donkey's jawbone and killed with it three hundred horsemen among the Philistines. As he was thirsty and was near to dying, he implored God and (God) made water flow from the donkey's jawbone, sweet water, and he regained his strength. As the children of Yehudä were watching for him in the city of Gäzä (= Gaza) to seize him, he rose at night, took a gate of the city of Gäzä, and carried it on his shoulders to the top of a mountain.

After that, (the Philistines) sent to his wife and promised her to give her much money; they said to her: "Learn from your husband Somson by what he is so strong." (Somson) spoke to her and told her that his strength was in his hair, for he was vowed to God. She spoke to his enemies and said to them: "His strength is in his hair. Watch for him in a hidden place until he is asleep." When he was asleep, they entered to him and shaved his hair. His strength weakened, they bound him, took him to their country, humiliated him, and blinded his eyes. After that, his hair grew (back) and his strength returned to him. He entered the temple on the day of the feast of the idol of the people of Gäzä; they had all gathered in the temple to sacrifice to their idol. Somson stood in the temple, grasped the two columns, one with his right hand and one with his left hand, and lifted them both. The

columns collapsed and the temple collapsed immediately. All the people who were in the temple died. Those who died in the temple by this massacre were much more numerous than those whom (Somson) had killed during all the days of his life. Then he died also with them, for he had thought that, by his death, many people among the enemies of God would die. All the days he judged the children of Israel (were) twenty years. Then he died in the peace of God to whom (belong) the glory and the power in the centuries of centuries, amen. On this day also, (there is) the translation of the body of the saint Yä'qob (= James) the Intercis; behold, we have written his (spiritual) fight on the twenty-seventh of the month of hedär. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen and amen. Here is finished what is read in the month of maggäbit in the peace of God the Father to whom (belongs) the glory in the centuries of centuries, amen. [On this day, there is the (spiritual) fight of abbä Gabra Manfas Qeddus, the star of the desert, whose fame (is) illustrious and old age, beautiful, the blessed and virtuous abbä Gabra Manfas Qeddus, the hermit who rose from the city of Nehisä (= Nahisa) in the district of the Lowlands, in the north of Egypt. He remained in the desert three hundred years. Having left there, he traveled through the deserts of Ethiopia and remained in Zequälä and, near there, he went into the land of Kabd. He remained naked two hundred and sixty-two years without drinking water nor eating food. It is not like men but like angels that he remained on earth. He finished his (spiritual) fight on the fifth of maggabit, on Sunday, during the feast of Pêtros (= Peter) and Pawlos (= Paul). May God have pity on us by his prayer, amen. The father and mother of this saint were among the virtuous and believing people, of illustrious family. The name of his father (was) Sem'on (= Simon) and the name of his mother, 'Aqlësyä; they were just before God. They remained sterile thirty years, 'Aglësyä wept for not having obtained a child and her husband also wept. One day, the Holy Spirit came to where she was, at the door of the entrance, having taken the appearance of a priest who lived in a castle (?). He said to her: "Greetings to you! What makes you weep? Your groans are

reached before God." 'Aglësyä said to him: "I have rejoiced in all things in the Lord my God, but I groan because of (the lack of) a child." At that moment, 'Aglësyä conceived - the twenty-ninth of maggäbit; and she gave birth on the twenty-ninth of tahsäs. The angel by the name of Gabr'ël (= Gabriel) came, having taken the appearance of a man, and said to 'Aglësyä: "May the name of this child of yours be Gabra Manfas Qeddus." Then the angel disappeared. The third day, the child rose, descended to the ground from his mother's womb, stood up, and prostrated himself three times for the Father, three times for the Son, and three times for the Holy Spirit. He also said: "Glory to the Father, glory to the Son, glory to the Holy Spirit, (you) who brought me out of darkness into light!" At that moment, the listeners who were there were astonished. His mother also was astonished, was stunned, and remained (thus) for three hours. God gave an order to the angel Gabr'ël and said to him: "Go to the house of Sem'on, take the child from his mother's womb, and make him enter the desert where there are many monks. Deposit him at their door, say to the superior: 'Take the child at the entrance of the door' and introduce (him) into the sanctuary, like Mary my mother who grew up in the sanctuary and remained twelve years in the hands of the angels until my coming into the world. I became incarnate from her, for I accepted her among all women; him also (= the child), I have accepted, for I made and created him pure: he is of the Spirit and his food nor his drink will be on the earth, but in the kingdom of the heavens."

The angel of God descended quickly to where the child was, in his mother's womb. He took him and lifted him on his wings like a woman who carries her child. His mother trembled and those who were there saw the angel carrying the child on his wings. By kissing him on the mouth, (the angel) made him ascend to the seventh heaven and brought him before God. God blessed him and said to the angel: "Lead him to my mother so that she may bless him and kiss him as she herself kissed me. Lead him also to the patriarchs 'Adäm (= Adam), 'Abreham (= Abraham), Yeshaq (= Isaac), and Yä'qob (= Jacob), to all the prophets and apostles so that they may greet him, and to all the martyrs and monks. Lead him also to the children who were killed because of me by the hand of Hërodes (= Herod). Say to them all, so that they may know that I, I will reward them on the Last Day, during the end of the whole world: 'You all will be with him.'" He said again: "Bring him to me." At the very moment, (the angel) led him to where God had ordered (him). He deposited him near our Lady Mary, and she kissed him and took him in her arms. He also led him to where the whole assembly of the just was, and all greeted him. Then he made him return and brought him to God (as?) before. Our Lord said to him: "I, I will be with you; be strong in everything that will happen to you. All human souls will be saved by you."
ወሶበ ፡ ተሰምዓ ፡ ነገሩ ፡ ወዜናሁ ፡ ወሃይማኖቱ ፡ ይመጽኡ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወካህናት ፣፤ ወኤሏስ ፡ ቆፅሳት ፤ ወእምግብጽ ፡ ወእምስር ፡ ወእምንሒሳ ፡ ወእምሀ
When his actions, his history, and his faith had spread, many people, priests and bishops, came to him from Egypt, from Mesr

(= Cairo), from Nehisä, and from the city of Sabsër (= Schabschir?); he loved travelers. One day, (the angel) came to where our father Gabra Manfas Qeddus was in prayer and said to him: "Come, ascend to heaven just as I made you ascend (there) formerly." He took him, lifted him on his wings, made him ascend into the heavens, and brought him before God. Our Lord made 'Abrehäm, Yeshaq, Yä'qob, the fifteen prophets, the twelve apostles, and all the martyrs come; he made all the saints come. Our Lord said to them, (to them all) together: "Take to greet him the one who was born after you; he will fight (spiritually) like you and his glory will be exalted. I, I will be with him until my coming; he will be joined to you, he himself is similar to me by purity, like the angels. He will sit at my right and you, you will be with him." He sent to our father his own person in the Trinity, stretched out his hands, took him in his arms, kissed his mouth, and said to him: "There are many souls that you will bring out of damnation by your prayer and your supplication. Go, distance yourself from men; the blind and the sick will be healed by your prayer. Henceforth, go aside, penetrate into the interior of the desert, and remain with the lions and the leopards. The number of the lions (will be) sixty and (that) of the leopards likewise." (The saint) said to our Lord: "The lions and the leopards, what will they be fed with and what will they eat?" (The Lord) said to him: "If you tread the earth with your feet, they will lick the dust of your feet while being satisfied until they have (other) food, until your coming to me." All those who were there - the excellent angels - heard this speech, they were astonished. When they saw him naked without clothing, they gave him kisses and embraced him. (The Lord) ordered Gabr'ël to make him descend (to earth), and (the angel) made him descend quickly to his previous dwelling. His hair came out and grew on his limbs. His hair reached seven cubits (of a cubit and) a palm and his beard, one cubit (of a cubit and) a palm. Each day, he heals the blind, the lepers, and the sick; their number is fifty thousand. But our father Gabra Manfas Qeddus was very afflicted that one knew his way of acting and his labor. He said: "I am going to rise and go away from this place so that men may not know me and that vain praise may not reach me in this world." He went away from this place, penetrated into the interior desert, and remained there. When the people were deprived of him, they were afflicted because of the miracles that he had done for them (and would no longer do); they wept and broke out in great lamentations. He remained in such a way of acting more than one hundred years until he had reached old age.
ንግባእኬ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ ነገር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ንቤ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ ተጋሂሦ ፡ እምሰብእ ፡ ወነበረ ፤ በየማን ፣ ብዙኃ ፡ ዓመታተ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሐሩረ ፡ ሐጋይ ፤ ወሞረ ፡ ክረምት ፣
Let us return therefore to (our) first subject where we were saying that he had penetrated into the desert, having distanced himself from men. He remained on the (bank)

right (of the Nile) for many years in the heat of the sun and the cold of winter. He did not clothe his body with a garment, but he was naked. His loins (were) girded with a sack of hair. He prayed standing, naked, in the cold of the plateau. By the rigor of the frost, he weakened his body and dried out his bones. He said to his soul: "Know that you will have to stand naked before God." He devoted himself to prayer, to fasting, to prostration, and to many vigils, innumerable, day and night, without interruption, until his body dried up and his skin stuck to his bones. He had no food, whether it be the fruit of trees, roots, or plants, or whether it be grass and other products on which hermits feed. He did not give any pretext (to rest?) to his body and the angels visited him, for he was like them by his word and by his way of acting. Who can be compared to him among the first saints and the last who has pushed labor so far on earth? None has not eaten earthly bread, nor has ever drunk water, nor has not worn a garment, nor has prepared anything for his body. In truth, Gabra Manfas Qeddus has made himself similar to the birds of the heaven; he has not thought of food nor of drink in this world; he has had hunger and thirst for God. For this therefore, his food was of heavenly bread and his drink, of water of paradise. Knowing that the word that God had pronounced was not lying, abbä Gabra Manfas Qeddus was full of courage, did not doubt, did not clothe himself, and did not desire a garment. God made (hair) grow on his whole body like the hair of sheep, which served for his body. Like a cloak (was) his beautiful beard that covered his body, black as a raven and spun like linen. His majesty (was that of) a lion - frightening and terrifying. His height (was) like (that of) a palm tree, and his odor, sweet like the perfume of a pound of pure (nard) of a great price. His sweet odor (was that of) the food of the desert (?). His face (was) like the face of an angel of God. His beard and his white hair around his mouth resembled a crown. When I think of saying the number of his words, I cannot (do it), for (they are) more numerous than the sand of the seashore and than the drops of rain. [Which of your works shall I admire more than all? Above all men have raised you the heaven, the earth, the sun, the moon, and the stars. They are not sufficient for a single step of your feet. The prophets and the apostles say of you: "(He is of) our race." The angels carry you on their wings. Mary, our Lady, says of

God as the saints who (lived) before me saw him, as the apostles saw him, as also the martyrs saw him while they were thrown into the streets, and as the just in the desert and the cells saw him." Having heard his speech, (the angels) went to our Lord Jesus Christ and told him everything he had said. Immediately, the heaven opened, and was unveiled the tent of fire that the four Animals carry without touching his (= God's) throne, as well as the twenty-four priests of heaven, holding censers, standing before him. Our Lord appeared to him as well as the archangels seated each according to his order, the Father, the Son, and the Holy Spirit being seated on the holy throne according to each of their three persons. (The saint) saw the three persons and the unique nature: the Father, the Son, and the Holy Spirit; all the angels and the archangels, each according to his rank, glorified him. When he saw (this), our father Gabra Manfas Qeddus was afraid and trembled. Our Lord said to him: "Be firm and do not be afraid: if you have seen me, it is because I have given you a firm heart so that you might see me in person, as (John, the author of) the Apocalypse saw me. What do you want and what do you ask of me? I have come to you so that you might see me. Now, what do you want me to do for you, Gabra Manfas Qeddus, my chosen one and my beloved, (you) whom I chose before you came into existence and (whom) I have made pure and chosen, (you) who have remained in the desert for seventy and eighty years, who have wandered in the deserts without doubting, have not feared to remain with the lions, and have been enduring and constant until dying? Now, in truth, I tell you, what you will have asked of me will not be struck with nullity: what do you want me to do for you?" Hearing this great mystery from (the mouth of) God, our father Gabra Manfas Qeddus rejoiced and said: "My Lord and my God, I am not worthy that you do (this) for me, (do it) for others who (are) like me. Now, give me ten (proofs of) mercy. Have mercy for me on the people of the land of Gabotä, they are sinners: you did not come into this world to call the just who do not need penance to repent but to turn the sinners toward penance. Remember, O Lord, those who are in punishment, those who make their tears flow like the water of winter and gnash their teeth because Satan has seduced them, they who acted without knowing. Have pity on them, O Lord, and be merciful toward them." Our Lord said to our father Gabra Manfas Qeddus the blessed - admirable is his speech: "It has been heard until now that I have not acted (thus) for other holy people who were pure and who have long labored on earth. Just as Latsun the monk implored me for a sinner with many misdeeds and many sins after (this sinner) had entered the (infernal) fire and had become the deep bottom of hell, when (Latsun) had begged me with painful labor, I brought out (of

hell) the soul of this man, I dispersed it in the wind, and the trace of it was not found during the resurrection of the dead, neither in the dwelling of the just nor in the dwelling of the sinners; on the other hand, for you, it will not be so, but I will show mercy for you according to my mercy, for I have given you a pact according to which I must do everything that you will ask of me." Hearing this, our father Gabra Manfas Qeddus rejoiced and prostrated himself on his face, and all the heavenly beings rejoiced and said: "Salvation (is) to our God, amen, alleluia!" Our father said again to God: "How much (will I have) for mercy my Lord?" (God answered:) "Each (of your merciful actions) will have double fruit as the length of one of your days (will be two)." The days of abbä Gabra Manfas Qeddus were three hundred years since he was born.
ወይቤሎሙ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ለሊቃነ ፤ መላእክት ፡ ሑሩ ፡ ወአምጽኡ ፡ ነፍሳተ ፡ እምደይን ። ወፍትሕዎን ፡ ወወሀብዎን ፡ ለብፁዕ ፡ ገብረ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወወረዱ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ኅበ ፡ ገሀነመ ፡ እሳት ፣ መካነ ፡ ኃጥኣን ። ወበይእቲ ፡ ሰዓት ፣ መጽአ ፡ አብድልማኮስ ፡ መልአከ ፡ ገሀነም ፡ ዘሥሉጥ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ስቃየ ፡ ኃጥኣን ፡ ወአውፅኦሙ ፡ ለሰብአ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ገቦታ ። ወኮነ ፣ ጐልቆሙ ፡፣ ለእሙንቱ ፡ ነፍሳት ፡ ፻፻፻ ፡ ወፅኡ ፡ እምደይን ። ወአተንተንዎን ፡ ለእሉ ፡፣ ነፍሳት ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይመርህዎን ፣ ሚካኤል ፡ ወገብርኤል ፡ በበጾታሆሙ ። ሩፋኤል ፡ ወኡራኤል ፡ በበነገዶሙ ። ሳቁኤል ፡ ወራሙኤል ፡ በበዕበዮሙ ። ወፋኑኤል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይገብር ፡ ይባቤ ፣ በቅድሜሆሙ ። ወካልኣን ፡ መላእክት ፡፣ እንዘ ፡፣ ይገብሩ ፤ ውውዓ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይበጽሑ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ መንበሩ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ በሰማያት ። ስብሐት ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ በሰማያት ፡ ወሰላም ፣ በም ድር ፡ ሥምረቱ ፡ ለሰብእ ።
Our Lord said to the archangels: "Go and bring the souls out of hell; release them and give them to the blessed Gabra Manfas Qeddus." The seven angels descended into the hell of fire, the place of the sinners. At that moment arrived 'Abdelmäkos, the angel of hell who has power over the torment of the sinners, and he brought out the people of the land of Gabotä. The number of these souls was thirty thousand, they came out of hell. While guiding these souls, Mikä'ël and Gabr'ël - each with his order - filled them with astonishment, as well as Rufa'el (= Raphael) and 'Urä'el (= Uriel) - each with his tribe - and Säqu'êl and Rämu'ël - each with his greatness -, Fänu'ël (= Phanouel) shouting cries of joy before them and other angels shouting cries of jubilation, until they had arrived before the throne of God in the heavens. (They shouted:) "Glory to God in the heavens and peace on earth, (may) his benevolence (be) to men!"
ወዓዲ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ገብረ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወይእዜኒ ፡ ኢይርአዩከ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እለ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ እምካህናት ፡ ወእመነኮሳት ፡ ወመሃይምናን ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ከማከ ፡ ጌራን ፡ እለ ፡ ይሔውጽዎሙ ። ወኢያስተርኢ ፡ ለመላእክት ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡-ፈቃድከ ። ይኩን ፡ ሰረገላከ ፡ ነፋስ ፡ ወይኩን ፡ ሩጸትከ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣ ድምፅ ። ወስርር ፣ እምሥራቅ ፡ እስከ ፡ ምዕራብ ፣፤ ወእምደቡብ ፡ እስከ ፡ ሰሜን ፡ ወድኅረ ፡ እምድር ፡ ወእስከ ፡ ደቡብ ፡ ነፋስ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘትክል ፡ በጺሐ ። ወእምይእዜሰ ፡ ብፁዕ ፡ ገብረ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ፍቁርየ ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ፈቀድከ ፡ ረአየኒ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ለአቡየ ፡ ወለመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ከመዝ ፡ ወትረ ፡ በትሥልስትነ ። ወዓዲ ፡ ሖር ፡ ምድረ ፡ ኢትዮጵያ ፡ ወበህየኒ ፡ ሀለወከ ፡ ነፍሳት ፡ ታወፅኦሙ ፡ እምደይን ። ወሰሚዖ ፡ አቡነ ፡፤ ነገሮ ፤ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ በርሀ ፡ ገጹ ፡ ወተፈሥሐ ፡ ሕሊናሁ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እግዚእየ ፡ ወአምላኪየ ፡ መኑ ፡ ይመርሐአኒ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ። በአይቴ ፡ ኣአምር ፡
God said again to our father Gabra Manfas Qeddus: "Henceforth, let them not see you, the men among the priests, the monks, and the faithful who are in the century, apart from the virtuous like you who will visit you, and I will not show (you) to the angels without your will. May your chariot be the wind and may your race be like it - without noise. Fly from the east to the west, from the north to the south, then from the earth to the high regions (?), (like) the wind, everywhere you will be able to reach. Henceforth, blessed Gabra Manfas Qeddus, my beloved, at the moment you want, see me, me, my Father, and the Holy Spirit - always thus in our Trinity. Go also into the land of Ethiopia; there also, you will have to bring souls out of hell." When our father heard the speech of God, his face illuminated and his thought rejoiced. He said to him: "My Lord and my God, who will guide me toward this land? How will I know the way? I have heard it said since my childhood
