ፍኖቶ ። ወሰማዕኩ ፡ እምንእስየ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ርቅ ፡ ብሔር ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እግዚ እነ ፡ ሖር ፡ አነ ፡ አበጽሐከ ፡ በሥልጣንየ ። ወመላእክትኒ ፡ ኢይርኅቁ ፡ እምኔከ ፡ እስመ ፣ ኮንከ ፡ ክቡረ ፡ ከማሆሙ ። ወሊተኒ ፡ በከመ ፡ ፈቀድከ ፡ ትረክበኒ ፡ ወበከመ ፡ ሰአልከኒ ፡ እገብር ፡ ለከ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ወሀቦ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኪዳነ ፡ ለገብሩ ፡ ወተሰወረ ፡ እምኔሁ ። ወተመይጡ ፡ሠራዊተ ፡ መላእክት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ በይባቤ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ዳዊት ፤ ነቢይ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ዐረገ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አርያም ፡ ፄዊወከ ፡ ፄዋ ። ወወሀብከ ፡ ጸጋከ ፡ ለእጓለ ፤ እመሕያው ። ወእሉሂ ፡ ነፍሳት ፡ እለ ፤ ወፅኡ ፡ እምደይን ፡ ወቦኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡፤ ገነተ ፡ ትፍሥሕት ፡ በክብር ፡ ወበስብሐት ፡ ወመልአ ፡ ትፍሥሕት ፡ በአርያም ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ መንክረ ፡ ወዕፁበ ፡ እምአመ ፡ ተፈጥረ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘተፈጥረ ፡ እስከ ፡ ምጽአተ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘከመዝ ፡ ግብር ። ሀበኒ ፤ እግዚኦ" ፡ ልበ ፡ አእምሮ ፡ ከመ ፡ እክል ፡ ጠይቆ ፡ ለገብረ ፡ ዚአከ ፡ ዘገበርከ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ገብረ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘኢገበርከ ፡ ለነቢያት ፡ ወለአ በው ፡ ቀደምት ፡ ወለካልኣን ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ዘደብር ፡ ወዘገዳም ፡ አኮ ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ ግብረ ፡፣ ሰብእ ፡ አላ ፡ ወሀብኮ ፡ ይግበር ፡ ዘትገብር ፡ አንተ ። ወምንተ ፡ እንከ ፡ ክህለ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ገብረ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ይስርር ፡ ውስተ ፡ አየር ፡ ወይዑድ ፡ ባሕረ ፡ እሳት ፡ አአላፍ ፡ ነፍሳት ። አኮ ፡ በጽኑዕ ፡ ጠባይዕ ፡ ማይ ፡ ወእሳት ፡ መሬት ፡ ወነፋስ ። ወአልቦ ፡ ካልአ ፡ ቱስሕተ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ አዳም ፡ ቀዳሜ ፡ ፍጥረት ። አዳምሰ ፡ ኢክህለ ፡ ይዑድ ፡ ወይጻዕ ፡ እምውስተ ፡ ሲኦል ፡ እስከ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ መድኅኒነ ። ኦገብረ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ምንተ ፡ የአክል ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ወሞገሥ ፡ ዕበይ ፡ ወክብር ፡ ወልዕልና ፡ ዘተጸጎከ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወሶበ ፡ ሀለይኩ ፡ እትናገር ፡ ገድለከ ፡ ይቀልል ፡ ሕሊና ፡ ከመ ፡ዘሰትየ ፡ ወይነ ፡ ከራሜ ፡ በትፍሥሕት ፡የዐይል ፡ ወይገብእ ፡ ወይብል ፡ እስከ ፡ አይ ፡ ወለማእዜ፡ መዋዕል ፡ እፌጽም ፡ ነገሮ ፡ እስመ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ነገሩ ፡ ዘኢይትኃለቀ፥ ።
that (this) land (is) far away." Our Lord said to him: "Go, I will make you arrive by my power. The angels will not distance themselves from you either, for you are honored like them. I also, according to what you will want, you will find me and, according to (what) you will ask me, I will act for you." After he had given (this) promise to his servant, our Lord disappeared from his (sight). The armies of the angels returned saying with cries of joy "glory!", with Däwit (= David) the prophet: You ascended on high having made captives and you gave your grace to men. The souls that had come out of damnation entered the Garden of joy with honor and with glory. Joy filled the heavens when one saw (this) admirable and stunning thing: since the world was created until the coming of Christ, such a thing had never been done. Give me, O Lord, the spirit of knowledge so that I may penetrate what you have done for your servant, for our father Gabra Manfas Qeddus, (and) that you have not done for the prophets nor for the patriarchs nor for the other saints of the mountain and the desert. He did not do human work, but you gave him to do what you, you do. How then could our father Gabra Manfas Qeddus fly in the air and travel through a sea of fire (so that) myriads of souls (might come out)? It is not with solid elements - water, fire, dust, and wind - and he did not have another complexion than 'Adäm, first man of the creation. Now 'Adäm could not circulate and come out of Sheol until our Savior came. O Gabra Manfas Qeddus, what is the extent of the grace, of the favor, of the greatness, of the honor, and of the elevation of which you have been gratified by God? When I think of telling your (spiritual) fight, (my) thought is light like one who has drunk old wine; with joy, it wanders and returns and says: "In how much time and what day will I finish his history, for numerous are his acts, innumerable?"
ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ገብረ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በምድረ ፡ ገቦታ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ወቦቱ ፡ ልማድ ፡ የአምር ፡ ዘኅቡእ ፡ ወዘገሀድ ። ወዓዲ ፡ የአምር ፣ ምሥጢረ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወዘበምድር ። ወዓዲ ፡ የአምር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ ንጹሓን ፡ ካህናት ፣፤ ወይሬኢ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይወርድ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወግብረ ፡ ኃጥኣንሂ ፡ ስጡሕ ፤ በቅድሜሁ ፡ ወከመ ፡ መጽሔት ፡ በአእይንቲሁ ። እስመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ የአምር ፡ ኩሎ ። ወርእየ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ገብረ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በምድረ ፡ ገቦታ ፡ ተኃቢኦሙ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ዮርባን ። ወየአክል ፤ ጐልቆሙ ፡ ፪፻ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተቀደሰ ፡ ዮርባን ፡ ወረደ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ምሥዋዕ ፣ ወተፈሥሐ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ጌር ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ርደተ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትሜጠዉ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘነሥአ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፤ ዘሠናይ ፡ ምግባሩ ፡ ወርቱዕ ፡ ልቡ ፡ እምሥጢረ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ዘጵርስፎራ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ለመልአክ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ምንት ፤ ኃጢአቶሙ ፡ ለሕዝብ ፡ ዘኢነሥኡ ፡ መን ፈሰ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ርኢ ፡ እስመ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ኃጢአቶሙ ፡
When our father Gabra Manfas Qeddus looked toward the land of Gabotä, he saw (it): he was accustomed to knowing what is hidden and what is manifest. He also knew the mysteries of heaven and those that (are) on earth. He also knew where the pure priests were and he saw the Holy Spirit descend. The acts of the sinners also were exposed before him, to his eyes like (in) a mirror, for whoever is of the Spirit knows everything. Our father Gabra Manfas Qeddus looked toward the land of Gabotä, (its Christian inhabitants?) having hidden themselves in the church at the moment of the eucharist; their number amounted to two hundred. When the eucharist was consecrated, the Holy Spirit descended on the altar, and our virtuous father rejoiced when he saw the descent of the Holy Spirit. He saw also (the faithful) communicating: no one, except the one whose way of acting (was) beautiful and the heart, straight, received the


[p. 454] {#po.46.p454}
ለሕዝብ ፤ ዘኢነሥኡ ፡ መንፈሰ ፡ በኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወተመይጠ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ገብረ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወርእዮሙ ፡ ለሰይጣናት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይነድእዎሙ ፡ ለሕዝብ ፡ በእሳተ ፡ አስዋጥ ፡ እምኩርጓኔ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ እስከ ፡ ይበጽሑ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቤቶሙ ። ወውእ ቱሰ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ይትፌሣሕ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘኮንዎ ፡ ቢጸ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወተኀጐሉ ፤ ኩሎሙ ፡ ወኢተርፉ ፡ ውስቴቶሙ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ዘአብደሩ ፡ ለንስሓ ፡ ወሰይጣንሰ ፡ ይትፌሣሕ ፡ በእ ንተ ፡ ዘረከበ ፡ ነዳያነ ፡ ግዱፋነ ። ወእሙንቱሰ ፡ ኃጥኣን ፡ እለ ፡ ኢገብሩ ፡ ንስሓ ፡ ለሕይወቶሙ ፡ በዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወኮንዎ ፡ ሐራ ፡ ለሰይጣን ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ መንክረ ፡ ወኀዘነ ፡ ወበከየ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ እግዚእየ ፡ ወአምላኪየ ፡ ፄወወ ፡ ወማህረከ ፡ ሐዝበከ ፡ ወኢያትረፈ ፡ ክልኤተ ፡ ምእተ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ አርባዕቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘበልዑ'" ፡ ኤፉደ ፡ ሥጋከ ፡ እስመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ መጦከ ፡ ሕዝበከ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ጫጥ ። ወዘ ንተ ፡ ብሂሎ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ገብረ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ወበከየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይግበር ፡ ምሕ ላለ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቀደሙ ፤! ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ ወበጺሖ ፡ህየ ፡ አውተረ ፡ ጸልዮ ፡ በደሴተ ፡ ማይ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ነበረ ፤ ቀዲሙ ።
spiritual mystery of the host. Our father said to an angel: "What is the sin of the people who did not receive the Spirit?" The angel said to our illustrious father: "Behold that numerous before God (are) the sins of the people who did not receive the Spirit." Our father Gabra Manfas Qeddus turned around and saw demons pushing the people with the fire of rods out of the courtyard of the church, until they had reached their house. As for Satan himself, he rejoiced that they had become his companions, were all lost, and (that) there remained among them only those who had preferred penance. Satan rejoiced to have found poor rejected ones. These (were) sinners who had not done penance during their life on earth and had become soldiers for Satan. When our father saw this prodigy, he was afflicted, wept, and said: "My Lord and my God, (Satan) has made captive and prisoner your people and has left only four men of the two hundred people who have eaten your protective body; indeed, it is said: You have delivered your people for free." Having said that, our father Gabra Manfas Qeddus went - and wept - to make supplications where the apostles (had (done it) before. Arrived there, he prayed with ardor in the island
ወተርእዮ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ለገብሩ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ አነሂ ፡ እም ሕር ፤ ለከ ፡ በበ ፡ ስሳ ፡ እልፍ ፡ በበ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ በዘተወለድኩ ፡ ባቲ ፡ በልደትየ ፡ ወበጥምቀትየሂ ፡ በበስሳ ፡ እልፍ ፡ እምሕር ፡ ለከ ። ወበትንሣኤየ ፡ በበስሳ ፡ እልፍ ፡፤ እምሕር ፡ ለከ ። ለእመ ፡ ወሰዶ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ አንተሂ ፡ ትነሥኦ ፡ እምውስተ ፡ ደይን ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘአትረፍኩ ፡ እምሰይጣን ፡ ወወሀብኩከ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ በከየ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ ብካየ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ነዳይ ፡ እምከመ ፡ ተፈጥረ ፡ ወተወልደ ፡ኮንኩ ፡እሱረ ። ኅበ ፡ አይቴ ፡ አሐውር ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ነዳይ ፡ ዘነሥአኒ ፡ ፄዋየ ፡ ማህረከ ፤ በጾም ፡ ወበጸሎት ፣ እምቅድመ ፡ ይትወለድ ፡ ዘነሣእኩ ፡ ወዘአስሐትኩ ፡ በኪንየ ፡ ወወሰድክዎሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ደይን ። ወከመዝ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ፈቀደ፣ ይሙት ። ወሰምዑ ፡ መንፈሳውያን ፡ ሰማያውያን ፡ ወምድራውያን ፡ ተፈሥሑ ፡ ወሰ ሐቁ ፡ ወአውፅኡ ፡ ነፍሳተ ፡ ዘሀለዉ ፡ በምድረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ በግዱድ ፡ ጻማ ፡ በጾም ፡ ወበጸሎት ። ወበአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ስግደታቲሁ ፡ ወትረ ፡ በበ ፡ አርብዓ ፡ እልፍ ። ወበበ፣ አርብዓ ፡ እልፍሂ ፡ ምእት ፡ ወኃምሳ ፡ መዝሙረ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ወይዜምር ፡ ዐሠርቱ ፡ ወኃምስቱ ፡ ነቢያት ፡ ወመኃልየ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ ወውዳሴ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ማርያም ። ወዘንተ ፤
(surrounded by water where he had remained previously. Our Lord - glory to him! - showed himself to his blessed servant and said to him: "I, I will make you a gift of 600,000 (souls) each of the days when I was born: (the day of) my birth and also (that of) my baptism, I will make you a gift of 600,000 (souls), and (the day of) my resurrection, I will make you a gift of 600,000 (souls). If Satan has taken him (= the sinner), you, you will take him back from damnation. I have left none (of them); out of (the grasp of) Satan, I have given them to you." When Satan heard that, after, he shed abundant tears saying: "Since this wretch was created and was born, I am bound. Where will I go far from (?) this wretch who, by fasting and prayer, has taken back from me my captives (and my) prisoners that, before he was born, I had taken and seduced by my industry and (that) I had dragged into great damnation?" And, speaking thus, Satan wanted to die. The spiritual heavenly and earthly beings heard him; they rejoiced, laughed, and brought out the souls that were in the land of Egypt by painful labors, by fasting, and by prayer. For a single day, his (= the saint's) prostrations (were) always each time forty thousand and forty thousand (times) also, he psalmodied the one hundred and fifty psalms of Däwit, (those) of the fifteen prophets, the canticles of Solomon, and the praises of Mary. And, having said that, he slapped his face three hundred times on the right (and) three hundred times on the left. He did all that in one


[p. 456] {#po.46.p456}
ብሂሎ ፤ ሠለስተ ፡ ምእተ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ይጸፍዕ ፡ ገጾ ፡ የማነ ፣ ሠለስተ ፡ ምእተ ፡ ፀጋመ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ይገብር ፡ በአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ወበአሐቲ ፡ ሌሊት ። ወበዘከመዝ ፡ ግብር ፡ መድኃኒተ ፡ ኮኖሙ ፡ ለነፍሳተ ፡፤ ኃጥኣን ፡ ያበክዮሙ ፡ ለሰይጣናት ፡፤ ወደክመ ፡ ኀይሉ ፡ ወለስሐ ፡፤ ሕምዙ ። ወፄወወ ፡ ወማኅረከ ፡ አቡነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ።
a single day and a single night. By such a manner of acting, he became the salvation of the souls of sinners and made Satan weep; the power of the latter weakened and his venom became ineffective; our father made him captive and prisoner through his prayer.
ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ ኢትዮጵያ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይተልውዎ ፡ አናብስት ፡ ወአናምርት ፤ ወጐልቆሙ ፡ ሠላሳ ፡ ይቅድምዎ ፡ ወሠላሳ ፡ ይትልውዎ ፡ አናምርትሂ ፤ ይተልውዎ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ እንዘ ፤ ይመርሖ ፡ መልአከ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ገብርኤል ፡ ተፅዒኖ ፡ በሰረገላ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ውእቶሙኒ ፡ አራዊት ፡ ተፅዒኖሙ ፡ ምስሌሁ ። ወአብጽሖ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ከብድ ፡ ወካዕበ ፡ ወሰዶ ፡ ዝቋላ ፡ መልዕልታይ ፡ ምድር ። ወቆመ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ኅበ፥፣ ጽንፈ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ወነጸረ ፡ ምሥራቀ ፡ ወምዕራበ ፡ ሰሜነ ፡ ወደቡበ ። ወርእየ ፡ ኃጢአ ቶሙ ፡ ለኢትዮጵያ ፡ ወኮነ ፡፤ ስጡሐ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ አዕይንቲሁ ፡ ወተተክለ ፡ በናላሁ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ አምሐልኩከ ፡ በሕያው ፡ ስምከ ፡ ኢይወፅእ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ወኢይቀውም ፡ በእገርየ ። ወነበረ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ እስከ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ አርብዓ ፡ መዐልተ ፡ ወአርብዓ ፡፤ ሌሊተ ፡ ወመጽአ ፡ ቃል ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ ዘይብል ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ ተዝካረከ ፡ ወዘጸውዓ ፡ ስመከ ፡ እምሕር ፡ ለከ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ገብረ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለመልአክ ፡ ዘኢተምህረ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ኢትዮጵያ ፡ ኢይወፅእ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ ባሕር ። ወሖረ ፡ መልአክ ፡ እምኅቤሁ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ እስከ ፡ ምእት ፡ ዓመት ። ወኀልቀ ፡ ኩሉ፣ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወተክዕወ ፡ ደሙ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ወማይኒ ፤ መሰለት ፡ ድመ ፡ ተርእየ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አዕፅምቲሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ በረድ ። ወይመጽኡ ፡ አጋንንት ፡ ወይነድፍዎ ፡ እምሥራቅ ፡ ወእምዕራብ ፡ እምሰሜን ፡ ወእምደቡብ ፡ በአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኃምስቱ ፡ምእት ፡ አጋንንት ፡ ይነድፍዎ ፡ በሐፆሙ ፡ ወትረ ፡ ለለዕለቱ ፡ እስከ ፡ ምእት ፡ ዓመት ፡ ወየሐርጽዎ ፡ አዕፅምቲሁ ፡ በአእባን ፡ በሊሓት ። ወአልቦ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እምቀዳሚ ፡ እስጢፋኖስ ፡ ሰማ ዕት ፡ እስከ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ኢይከውኑ ፡ ለአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡፤ መንክራቲሁ ፡
After that, (the saint) went to Ethiopia, lions and leopards following him—in their number, thirty to precede him and thirty to follow him; the leopards followed him likewise—and the angel of God by the name of Gabr'ël guiding him, mounted on a spiritual chariot, the animals mounted with him. (The angel) brought him to the land of Kabd, then led him to Zequälä, the high country. Our father stood at the edge of the lake and looked toward the east, the west, the south, and the north. He saw the sins of Ethiopia; they were spread out before his eyes and they planted themselves in his brain. He said to God: "I have adjured you by your living name: I will not leave this lake and I will not stand on my feet." He sat thus until forty days and forty nights had passed, and a voice came from heaven which said: "I will show mercy for your sake to him who shall make your commemoration and to him who shall invoke your name." Our father Gabra Manfas Qeddus said to the angel: "He who has not studied all of Ethiopia thoroughly shall not leave this lake." The angel left him and (he himself) remained sitting in this manner for one hundred years. His whole body was consumed, his blood flowed into the lake—the water resembled blood—and his bones appeared like crystal. The demons came and pierced him from the east and from the west, from the south and from the north: in a single day, one hundred and fifty demons pierced him with their arrows—continually, each day for one hundred years—and they broke his bones with sharp stones. From 'Estifänos (= Stephen) the protomartyr to Pétros (= Peter) the patriarch and all the martyrs who died for Christ, it did not return to any man to (accomplish) his wonders of a single day.
ወኩሎሙ ፡ ሰማዕታት ፡ እለ ፡ ሞቱ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ መጽአ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ወቆመ ፡ ጽንፈ ፡ ባሕር ፣ ወይቤሎ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ተንሥእ ፡ ወፃእ ፡ መሐርኩ ፡ ለከ ፡ ኢትዮጵያ ። ወተረክበ ፡ አዕፅምቲሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ስኩ ረተ ፡ መርፍእ ፡ ወገሠሦ” ፡ ወረሰዮ ፡ ከመ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ ወፈነዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ከብድ ። ወእ ምዝ ፡ አፅረጎ ፡ ሰማየ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ እስከ ፡ ሰብዓቱ ፡ ዓመት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ታሕቴሃ ፡ ለሰማይ ፤ ወበላዕሌሃ ፡ ለፀሐይ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ፈነወ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ከብድ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ቀዊሞ ፡ ትኩል ፤ ከመ ፡ ዓምድ ፡ እስከ ፡ ስድስቱ ፡፤ አውራኅ ፡ ነጺሮ ፡ ሰማየ ፣ ወኢከደነ ቀራንብቲሁ ፡ ወኢያትሐተ ፡ርእሶ ፡ ሰፊሖ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ተመሲሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ቋዕ ፡ ጸሊም ፡ ወረከበ ፡ አዕፅምቲሁ ፡ ስቁለ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ዘይትከደን ፡ ወነበረ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ርእሱ ። ወነሥአ ፡ ወሰቅሰቀ ፡ ክልኤተ ፡ አዕይንቲሁ ፡ ወቀረ ፡ አጥፊኦ ፡ አዕይንቲሁ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ነበረ ፡ እስከ ፡
After that, our Lord came, stood at the edge of the lake, and said to our father: "Rise and depart; I have shown mercy for your sake to Ethiopia." His (= the saint's) bones were found like the eye of a needle (?). (God) touched him, made him as he was before, and sent him toward Kabd. Then he made him ascend to heaven and (the saint) remained seven years in the lower (part) of heaven and above the sun. After (God) had sent him into the land of Kabd, he remained planted standing like a column for six months while looking at the sky; he did not close his eyelids nor did he lower his head, stretching his hands toward heaven. After that, Satan came to him, having taken the appearance of a black raven. He found his bones suspended in the sky by which they were covered and sat on his head; he took (it), gouged out his two eyes (and) dug, having extinguished his eyes. After that, (the saint) remained (blind) for ninety weeks; as always, he did not interrupt his prayer in this land. Then Mikä'ël and


[p. 458] {#po.46.p458}
ተስዓ ፡ ሰሙን ፡ ከመ ፡ ወትረ ፡ ጸሎቶ ፡ ኢያንተገ ፡ በውእቱ ፡ ምድር ። ወእምዝ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ወገብርኤል ፡ ወቆሙ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ገጹ ፡ ወነፍሑ ፡ኅቡረ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ። ወርእየ ፡ አፅይንቲሁ ፡ ወንጻሬሁ ፡ ያበርህ ፡ ምስብዒተ ፡ እምፀሓይ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ሖር ፡ ኀበ ፡ ዝቋላ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትትቃተሎሙ ፡ ለጸላእትከ ፡ ወይሁበከ ፡ ኅይለ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ ወምድር ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ዐርጉ ፡ ክልኤሆሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ።
Gabr'ël came, stood before his face, and blew together upon it: his eyes saw and his gaze shone seven times more than the sun. They said to him: "Go to Zequälä to fight your enemies, (God) will give you great power (coming) from heaven and (from) the earth." After that, both ascended to heaven.

He himself rose, went to Zequälä, and met on the way three weakened men from the monasteries, where they were sitting resting in the shade. Abbä Gabra Manfas Qeddus saw them and said in his spirit: "I am going to hide from them." But they hastened to cry out saying: "Do not abandon us (when you shall be?) in the dwelling of God! Carry us all three a little on your back, for a distance of a mile." (The saint) came and saw them, decrepit old men, entirely covered with white hair. He took one (of them) whom he carried on his back and brought him a mile (from there). The old man said to him: "Here, you are tired because you neither drink nor eat nor weep." Our father said to him: "How do you know me?" (The old man) said to him: "Go, bring my companions." (The saint) went, returned, left the old man there, and arrived where the two (others) were. He took one, brought him to the (first) old man, set him down, and returned. Then he took the other, brought him to the two (other) old men, and reunited him (with them). He said to them: "From where have you come? The sweetness of your odor rejoices the heart and transports the hearts!" The three men rose and stood, and the three seemed to him (= to the saint) as one. They said to him: "We are going to carry you, as you carried us a little, and we are going to bring you up to the seven heavens while carrying (you)." At that moment, the aspect of their face was changed; they put on the majesty of their divinity and became like flames of fire. Lightnings flashed and struck all around. The mountains and the hills collapsed and fell; all the stones were broken and became like ash.
ወይእተ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ነሥአ ፡ አብ ፡'አቡነ ፡ ገብረ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ?' ፡ ወጾሮ ፡ በዘባኑ፣ ወአብጽሖ ፡ እስከ ፡ ክልኤቱ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ወእስከ ፡ ማእከላይ ፡ ወተመጠዎ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ወነሥኦ ፡ ወጾሮ ፡ በዘባኑ ፡ ወአብጽሖ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ፪ሰማያት ፡ እስከ ፡ ማእከላይ ፡ ሣልሳይ ። ወተመጠዎቓ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወአብጽሖ ፡ ኅበ ፡ መንበሩ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወግሩም ፡ ወነበሩ ፡ ሾ ፡ ግጻዌ ፡ ወ፳ ፡ ሀላዌ ፡ ወ ፳ ፡ መንበር ፡ ወሐቀፎ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ወሰዓሞ ፡ አፉሁ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሐቀፍዎ ፡ ወሰዓምዎ ። በከመ ፡ ይስዕም ፡ አብ ፡ ወልዶ፣ ከማሁ ፡ ሰዓምዎ ፡ ወሐቀፍዎ ፡ ወአርአይዎ ፡ ፪ ፡ መናብርተ” ፡ እምነቢያት ፡
At that moment, the Father took our father Gabra Manfas Qeddus, lifted him onto his back, and brought him up to the second heaven, up to the (second?) mediator; the Son received him, took him, lifted him onto his back, and brought him into two (other) heavens up to the third mediator; the Holy Spirit received him and brought him to the holy and terrible throne. The three persons—one single nature and one single throne—sat down and our Lord took him in his arms and kissed his mouth. The Father, the Son, and the Holy Spirit took him in their arms and embraced him: just as a father embraces his son, so they embraced him and took him in their arms. [They showed him four dwellings; in addition to the prophets and the apostles—one dwelling—and the righteous and the martyrs, there remained three twice (as large). Our Lord said to him: "Heaven and earth, the sun, the moon, and the stars are not enough: they do not make the price

ወሐዋርያት ፡ መንበር ፡ ወእምነ ፡ ጸድቃን ፤ ወሰማዕት ፡፤ ወይተርፍ ፡ ይመክዕቢተ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየአክል ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወምድር ፡ ፀሓይ ፡ ወወርኅ ፡ ወከዋክብት ፡ ሜጠ ፤ አሐቲ ፡ ጸጐርከ” ፡ ኢይከውን ። ሖር ፡ ረድ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ዝቋላ ፡ ወታስጥሞሙ ፡ ለአጋንንት ፡ ዘነደፉከ ፡ አዕፅምቲከ ፡ ወሀለዉ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይዜሃሩ ፡ ኢየአምሩ ፡ ዘአንሣእ ኩከ ፡ አነ ፤ ሀሎኩ ፡፤ በውስተ ፡ ባሕር ፤ ወእተሉከ፤‹ ፡ ወሊቃነ ፡ መላእክት ፥፤ ይተልዉከ ፡ ወመባርቅተ ፡ እሳት ፡ ይቀድሙከ ። ወሰረረ ፡ አቡነ ፡ በዘባነ ፡ መብረቅ ፡ ወፈነውዎ ፡ ጀግጻዌ ፤ ወተመይጡ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መንበሮሙ ። ወወረደ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ወወድቀ ፡ ኀበ ፤ አርእስቲሆሙ ፡ በአስይፍተ ፡ እሳት ፡ ወአንደድዎሙ ፡ መባርቅት ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ ሐመደ ፡ ወሐመዶሙ ፡ ወሰዱ ፡ ነፋሳት ። ወጐልቆሙ ፡ ለእሙንቱ ፡ አጋንንት ፡ ፎ፻ ፤ እልፍ ፡ ወ፪፻ ፤ ወኩሎሙ ፡ ኀልቁ ፡ በአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ወኢተረፈ፤ ፡ አሐዱ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ዐርጉ ፤ መባርቅት ፡ ወሊቃናት ፡ ወአቡነሂ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ከብድ ፤
of a single one of your hairs. Go, descend to Zequälä and drown the demons who pierced your bones: they are boasting about it (and) do not know that I have resurrected you. I, I am in the lake and I will follow you; the seven archangels will follow you and lightnings of fire will precede you." Our father flew off on the back of a lightning bolt; the three persons sent him and returned to their dwelling. Our father descended and fell upon their (= the demons') heads with swords of fire. The lightnings burned them, they became ash, and the winds carried away their ash. The number of these demons (was) seven thousand two hundred; all perished in a single day and not a single one remained. After that, the lightnings and the arch(angels) ascended (to heaven) and our father went to the land of Kabd, where there were lions, leopards, bears, and large serpents.
ኀበ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ አናብስት ፡፤ ወአናምርት ፤ ወድብሂ ፤ ወተመናት ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ አሐተ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ወሰሐቦሙ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ፳ ፤ አባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ዘዋልድባ ። ወካልኡ ፡ አባ ፡ አንሰሳ ፡ ዘምድረ ፡ ሐዘሎ ፡ ወሣል ሱሂ ፡፤ አባ ፣ ብንያም ፤ ዘታሕታይ ፡፤ ምድር ፡ ዘምስለ ፤ አናብስቲሆሙ ፡ ወበጽሑ ፡፤ ምድረ ፡ከብድ ፡ ወተሰወረ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ወነበሩ ፡ በምሕላ ፡ ፪ ፅለተ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይረክብዎ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ወፅኡ ፡ አናብስት ፡ ኅቡኣን ፡ ወበጽሑ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ ቦዞቅዱ ሳን ፡ ወአኀዙ ፤ አናብስቲሆሙ ፡ ወበልዑ ፤ ሥጋሆሙ ፡ ወወሐጡ ፡ ወለሐሱ ፡፤ ደሞሙ፤ ፡ በቅጽበት ። ደንገፁ ፡ እሉ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ወተሰወሩ ፡ እሉ ፡ አናብስት ፡ ወኀ ዘኑ ፡ እሉ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ ኀዘነ ። ወተከሥተ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ኀዘኖሙ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ገብረ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወተንሥአ ፡ ወመጽአ ፡ ኀቤሆሙ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ኅይል ፤ ዘተው ሀበ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ ዘምስለ ፡ ስሳ ፡ አናብስቲሁ ፡ ወምስለ ፡ ስሳ ፡ አናምርቲሁ ፡ ወመላእክት ፡ ጸዊሮሙ ፡ ሐይመተ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ወጸጐረ ፡ ርእሱ ፡ ለብሰ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ እንተ ፡ዳስ ፤ ወይሰሐብ ፡ እስከ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወጸጐረ ፤ ጽሕሙ ፡ ወክሳዱ ፡ ይሰ ጣሕ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወይለብስ ፡ ተዓፎ ። ወመጽአ ፡ ኀቤሆሙ ፡ በዐቢይ ፡ ኀይል ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ሰላም ፡ ለክሙ ፡ ቅዱሳኒሁ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወደንገፁ ፡ እሉ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ ግርማሁ ፡ መደንግፅ ፡ ወእሉ ፡ አናብስት ፡ ወአናምርት ፡ ያንገረግሩ ፡ በገጹ ፡ ይነብቡ ፡፤ ወይጥኅሩ ፡ በምድር ፡ ከመ ፡ ፈረስ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ለእሙንቱ ፣ ቅዱሳን ፡ በምንት ፡ ግብር ፤ መጻእክሙ ፡ ኀቤየ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀሎኩ ፡ ባሕቲትየ ፡ በዝመካን ፡ ዘገዳም ። ወይቤልዎ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ መጻእነ ፡ ሶበ ፡ አእመርነ ፡ ጸሎተከ ፡ ቅድ ስት ፤ ርእሰ ፡ ባሕታዊያን ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ዓለም ። ወንሕነሰ ፡ መጻእነ ፡ ኀቤከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትንግ ረነ ፤ ግብረ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፤ ወኢረከብናከ ፡ መጺአነ ፡ ኀዘነ ፡ ወበከይነ ፡ እስከ ፡ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ወእንዘ ፡ ንገብር ፡ ምሕላለ ፡ በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ መጽኡ ፡ አናብስቲከ ፡ ወአኀዙ ፡ አናብስቲነ ፤ ወበልዕፅዎሙ ፡ ወወሐጡ ፡ ወለሐሱ ፡ ደሞሙ ፡ በቅጽበት ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡
After that, one day, saints came who (were) with their lions, the Holy Spirit had led them; one (was) abbä Sämu'ël (= Samuel) of Waäldebbä, the second, abbä 'Ansasä, from the land of Hazalo, and the third, abbä Benyäm (= Benjamin) from the low country. They arrived at the land of Kabd and our father hid himself. They remained seven days in supplications until they had found him. At the end of seven days, the hidden lions (of the saint) came out and arrived where the three saints were. They seized the lions of the latter, devoured and swallowed their bodies, and licked their blood in the blink of an eye: the saints trembled. The lions disappeared. The saints experienced great affliction. Their affliction was revealed to our father Gabra Manfas Qeddus; he rose and came to them with great power that had been given to him by heaven, he who (was) with his sixty lions and with his sixty leopards, the angels carrying a tent of light. His hair clothed his whole body like a hut and dragged to the ground. The hairs of his beard and of his nape spread on the ground; he was clothed, covered (thus). He came (therefore) to them with great power and said to them: "Peace to you, saints of God!" The saints trembled when they saw his terrifying majesty; the lions and the leopards rolled before him, cried out, and roared on the ground like horses. Our father said to the saints: "For what reason have you come to me, where I am alone in this place of the desert?" The saints said to him: "We came when we had knowledge of your holy prayer, chief of the solitaries in the entire world. We, we came so that you might tell us the work of God. Having come, we did not find you; we were afflicted and wept for seven days and, while we were making supplications on this day, your lions came, seized our lions, ate and swallowed them, and licked their blood in the blink of an eye." Our father said to his lions: "Why

አቡነ ፡ ለአናብስቲሁ ፡ ለምንት ፡ በላዕክሙ ፡ ዘኢአዘዘክሙ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በሊዐ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ዘኬድኩ ፡ ጸበለ ፡ እገርየ ፡ እስከ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ሞትክሙ ፡ ትፍኡ ፡ ዘበላእክሙ ። ወአብቀዉ ፡ ወተፍኡ ፡ ዘበልዑ ፡፣ ኩሎ ፣፤ ሥጋሆሙ ፡ ወአዕፅምቲሆሙ ፡ ዘምስለ ፡ ደሞሙ ፡ ወኢእምአዕፅፅምቲሆሙ ፡ ወኢእምሥጋሆሙ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ከርሦሙ ፡ ወቄዑ ፡ በይእቲ ፡ጊዜ ፡ ከመ ፡ ቅጽበት ። ወተመይጠ ፡ አቡነ ፡ መንገለ ፡ ምሥራቅ ፡ ወአእኩተ ኀበ ፡ አምላኩ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ዘአንሣእኮ ፡ ለአልዓዛር ፤ ከማሁ ፤ አንሥኦሙ ፤ ወኢትንሥ ኦሙ ፡ ዘወሀብኮሙ ፡ ለአግብርቲከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትልውዎሙ ። ወይእተ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ባረከ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ወላዕለ ፣ ሥጋሆሙ ፡ በድኖሙ ፡ ለአናብስት ፣ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ተንሥኡ ፡ በኀይለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወተንሥኡ ፡ አናብስት ፡ በቅጽበት ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ ከመ ፡ ቀዳሚ። ወከልሑ ፡ ወአንገርገሩ ፡ አስመኩ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ወሰገዱ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እገሪሁ ፡ ወተናገሩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እሉ ፡ አናብስት ፣ ወይቤሉ ፡ እምይእዜሰ ፡ ንተሉ ፡ ኪያከ ። ወእሉሰ፥ ፡ ኢክህሉ ፡ ኪያነ ፡ ወአንተሰ ፡ አንሣእከነ ፡ ወአውፃእከነ ፡፤ እምከርሠ ፡ አናብስት ፡፣ ወፈነዎሙ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፤ አጋእዝቲሆሙ ። ወአስተዓፀቡ ፡ እሉ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ግብሮ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወይቤ ፡ አባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ገብረ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እግዚአብሔርነ ፡ አንተ ፡ ወንሕነሰ ፡ ሰብአ ፣ መሰልከነ ፣ ዘከማነ ፡ ዘሞተ ፡ ታነሥእ ፡ እምከርሠ ፡ አናብስት ፡ ታወፅእ ፡ ዘተበልዑ ፡ አናብስቲነ ፡ ርኢነ ፡ መንክረ ፡ በዛቲ ፡ ፅለት ።
have you eaten, you to whom God did not command to eat—if not the dust that I shall have trodden with my feet—until the day of your death? ... spit out what you have eaten." (The lions) opened (their mouths) and spat out what they had eaten, all their (= the other lions') flesh and their bones with their blood; (there remained) nothing of their bones nor of their flesh in their belly. They vomited at that moment in the blink of an eye. Our father turned toward the east, praised God, and said: "You who resurrected 'Al'azar (= Lazarus), resurrect them likewise and do not take them, they whom you gave to your servants so that they might follow them." At the same moment, he blessed them as well as their bodies—the carcasses of the lions—and said to them: "Rise by the power of God." The lions rose in the blink of an eye and were as before. They cried out, rolled (on the ground), lay down beside our father, and prostrated themselves at his feet. These lions spoke like men and said: "Henceforth, we will follow you. They could not (resurrect) us, but you, you resurrected us and made us come out of the belly of the lions." Our father sent them to their first masters. These saints were astonished at the work of God. Abbä Sämu'ël said to our father Gabra Manfas Qeddus: "Are you God? We, we believe you to be a man like us; you resurrect him who is dead; from the belly of the lions, you make our lions come out that were eaten! We have seen a wonder this day!"
ወእምዝ ፡ አመ ፡ ሠሉሱ ፡ ለመጋቢት ፡ ሐመ ፡ አባ ፡ ገብረ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በዕ ለተ ፡ ዐርብ ፡ አመ ፡ ቀርበ ፡ ሞቱ ። ወአኀዞ ፡ ደዌ ፡ ወሕማም ፡እስከ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ለሞት ፣ ሶበ ፡ ቀርበ ፡ ሰዐተ ፣ ሞቱ ። ወመጽኡ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ባሕታውያን ፡ ኅቡኣን ፡ እለ ፡ ስሞሙ ፡ ፍሬ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወዘርአ ፡ ቡሩክ ። ወያዕቆብ ። ወብንያም ። ወዮሴፍ ። ወነገሮሙ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ይመውት ፡ ወዘከመ ፡ ወሀቦ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኪዳነ ። ወሰሚዖሙ ፡ በከዩ ፡ ወኀዘኑ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሞቱ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ርእሰ ፡ ባሕታዊያን ። ወበቀዳሚት ፡ ሰንበት ፡ ወወዐለ ፡ በጸማ ፡ ወበድካም ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይልሕብ ፡ ወየሐፉ ። ወኢየዓርፍ ፡ ስኢለ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አምላኩ ፡ በከመ ፡ ልማዱ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ነፍስ ። ወሶበ ፡ መስየ ፡ ደክመ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወስእነ ፡ ተናግሮ ፡ ወእሙንቱኒ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያምጽእዎ ፡ ለገብረ ፡ እንድርያስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይርአይ ፡ ሞቶ ፡ ወከመ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ስምዓ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ኅቡኣን ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ወነገርዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሙት ፡ ብፁዕ ፡ ገብረ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ በከየ ፡ ወኀዘነ ፡ መሪረ ፡ ወተንሥአ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ሰርከ ፡ ሰንበት ፡ ለጸቢሐ ፡ እሑድ ፡ እስመ ፡ ወድአ ፡ ወመስየ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ አሐተ ፡ ሰዓተ ፤ ሌሊት ። ወተንሥኡጾ ፡ እስመ ፡ ርቅ ፡ ፍኖቱ ፡ እማኅደሩ ፡ ለቀሲስ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ክቡር ። ወ፪ሰዓተ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ ረከብዎ ፡ ሰኪቦ ፡ ከመ ፡ አንበሳ ፡ ግሩም ፡ በማእከለ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ኅበ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘይኬልሎ ፡ ወይጹልሎ። አልቦ ፡ እለ ፡ ረከብዎ ፡ በጽጐ ፡ ዘቅሩብ ፡ እምፍኖቱ ። ወውእቱሰ ፡ ሰኪቦ ፡ ሰፍሐ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ በከመ ፡ ሰፍሐ፣
Then, on the third day of maggäbit, abbä Gabra Manfas Qeddus fell ill, on Friday when his death was near. The illness and the suffering seized him until he reached death when the hour of his death was near. Many hidden solitaries came to him whose names (are:) Ferë geddus, Zar'a buruk, Yä'qob (= Jacob), Benyam (= Benjamin), and Yosëf (= Joseph). Our father told them that he was going to die and that God had given him a covenant. Upon hearing (this), they wept and were afflicted because of the death of the saint, for this one (was) the chief of the solitaries. On Saturday, he spent the day in suffering and weakness, burning (with fever) and sweating. He did not cease to supplicate his God, as was his custom, for all souls. When evening came, his body weakened and he could not speak. Those (who were) with him (went) to find Gabra ‘Endreyäs so that he might see his death and be a witness (to it). When they arrived at him, the hidden (solitaries) told him that the blessed Gabra Manfas Qeddus was going to die. When he heard (this), he wept and was afflicted bitterly. He rose and went to him, on the evening of Saturday when Sunday was beginning to dawn, for the evening was already advanced and it was the first hour of the night. They rose, for the road (was) long from the dwelling of the priest (to) where the illustrious (hermit) was. At the fourth

hour of the night, they found him lying like a majestic lion in the middle of the desert, where no one surrounded him nor shaded him; they did not find him in the place that (was) near his path. Being lying down, he stretched his arms toward heaven as our Lord Jesus Christ had stretched (his) on the cross at the moment of his passion until the moment of his death. When (the priest saw him,) he saw the light that (was) upon him, the grace of God that (was) upon his face, his resplendent beauty, and his long beard, white as snow. When it was the seventh hour of the night, his soul separated from his body. At that moment, one heard a noise (coming) from heaven like the noise of thunder, and lamps like hail and like slingstones—which thundered and turned—descended from heaven to earth, each (having) its color and each, its form, (lamps) which shone like the sun, the moon, and the stars. Those who were there trembled and were afraid because of this very terrifying (phenomenon) that had occurred, for it had descended upon them. They could absolutely not touch his (= the saint's) perfect body. After he was dead, they withdrew and fled, for they were distraught because of the fear and the trembling that had descended upon them: the earth trembled, the mountains wavered, and there was a great cry at the moment of the departure of the soul of the saint. As for the saints, they stood at a distance to see and hear the mystery of heaven and earth that was occurring. God strengthened the spirit of the saints so that they might understand

what was happening in honor of our father Gabra Manfas Qeddus (and) so that they might be witnesses of what they had seen God descend with his Trinity to our father before his soul (went away?). Jesus said to him: "I have come to give you an offering for your commemoration: that he who shall write, and he who shall have written, the book of your life may go with you publicly and that his stay may be in the houses of light; I will write his name in my formidable dwelling. That he who shall make your commemoration believing without failing may pass the sea of fire and stand before you without shame. If he gives bread to the hungry, I, I will give him the bread of heaven and he shall never be hungry. If he gives drink to the thirsty, I, I will give him drink forever of milk with the little children. If he gives incense, even if he is impure, may he be with you. If he gives fine flour, may he approach with you to the heavenly Jerusalem. If he gives oil on the day of your commemoration, may he be with 'Abrehäm, Yeshäq, and Yä'qob-'Esrä'ël. If he offers a lamp, may he cross the sea of fire and the darkness with twelve luminous lamps that shine seven times more than the sun. Those who shall gather on the day of your commemoration, I, I will gather them on Mount Seyon (= Zion) with all the saints." Our father supplicated God and said to him: "I have lived 362 years, I have not drunk water and I have not thought of eating with my body neither (of the fruit) of the trees nor of the

ገዳም ፡ እመሂ ፡ ዐራዘ ፡ ለሥጋየ ፡ ወነበርኩ ፡ ዕራቅየ ፡፤ ወለእመ ፡ ኀደርኩ ፡ በባሕር ፤ ዘምስለ ፤ ዓሣ ፤ ወጉማሬ ፡ ወነበርኩ ፡ በፆም ፡ ዘምስለ ፡ አዕዋፍ ፡ ወነበርኩ ፡ እንዘ ፡ አዓይል ፡ በአድባር ፡ ምስለ ፡ አንበሳ ፡ ወድብ ፡ ወነምር ፡ ወተመን ። ዘንተ ፡ ዓሥራተ ፡ ትሁቦሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳን ። ወአንሰ ፡ እስእለከ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ ኦሆ ፡ በለኒ ። ወአውሥአ ፡ እግዚእነ ፣ ወይቤሎ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኃጢአተ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ንሥሐ ፡ እምሕር ፡ ለከ ፡ አኮ ፡ እምክርስቲያን ፡ ወውሉዶ ፡ አረሚኒ ፡ ዘይገብር ፡ ተዝካረከ ፡ ይድኅን ፡ ወይኩን ፡ ምስሌከ ። አመ ፡ ተፈልጠት ፡ ነፍስከ ፡ አመ ፡ ሐሙሱ ፡ ለመጋቢት ፡ ይግበሩኬ ፤ ፲ወ፪ ፤ ከመ ፡ ለማርያም ፡ እምየ ፡ ይግበሩ ፡ ማኅበረ ፡ ወአነ ፡ እባርክ ፡ ማኅበ ሮሙ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ልሂቅ ፡እሁቦ ፡መንበረ ፡ ሠናየ ። እምይእዜሰ ፡ ፅርግ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አብያተ ፡ ብርሃን ። ወንሣእ ፤ አክሊላተ ፡ ፲ወ፪ ፡ ወ፲ ፤ እልፍ ፡ ማኅቶት ፡ በየማንከ ። ወ፲ ፡ እልፍ ፡ በፀጋምከ ። ፲ ፡ እልፍ ፡ በቅድሜከ ። ፲ እልፍ ፡ በድኅሬከ ። ወአዕናቄ ፡ ብርሃ ናት ፡ ፲ወ፪ ። ወንሣእ ፡ አፍራሰ ፡ እሳት ፡ ለቀበላከ ። ወአውሥአ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ገብረ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት፣፣ ለእለ ፡ ይገብሩ ፡፤ ተዝካርየ ፡ በጥቡዕ ፡ ልብ ፡ እስከ ፡ እስፍንቱ ፡ ትውልድ ፡ ትምሕር ፤ ሊተ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እምሕር ፡ ለከ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይከውን ፡፲ ወ፳ ፡ ትውልድ ።
fruits of the desert. (I have not thought) either of a garment for my body and I have remained naked; if I have stayed in the lake with the fish and the hippopotamuses, I have dwelt in the woods with the birds and I have remained wandering in the mountains with the lion, the bear, the leopard, and the serpent. This offering, you give (it) to the saints. I, I supplicate you, O Lord, hear my (request)." Our Lord answered and said to him: "Even if he has committed a sin without (doing) penance, he who—not (only) among the Christians but also (among) the pagans—shall make your commemoration, I will show (him) mercy for your sake. May he be saved and be with you. When your soul shall have separated (from your body), on the fifth of maggäbit, let twelve do as (the twelve apostles) for Mary my mother, let them make an assembly, and I, I will bless their assembly; if there (is a man) old, I will give him a beautiful dwelling. Now, ascend into the houses of light, receive twelve crowns, one hundred thousand lamps in your right hand, one hundred thousand in your left hand, one hundred thousand before you and one hundred thousand behind you, as well as twelve stones of light. Receive horses of fire to welcome you." Our father abbä Gabra Manfas Qeddus took the word again and said to our Lord Jesus Christ—glory to him!—: "Those who shall make my commemoration with a firm heart, until what generation will you show (them) mercy for me?" God said to him: "I will show (them) mercy for your sake until there are fifteen
ወካዕበ ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ ጽራሐ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ ዘይብል ፡ ንሥኡ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለገብረ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፤ ወውስድዎ ፡ ወይኩን ፡፤ መቃብሪሁ ፡ በኢየሩሳሌም ፡ በየማነ ፤ ምሥዋዕ ። ሚካኤል ፤ ወገብርኤል ፤ ወኩሎሙ ፣ መላእክት ፡ ገብሩ ፡ ይባቤ ፣ በቅድመ ፡ ነፍሱ ፡ ንጽሕት ፡ በስብሐት" ፡ ዘዘ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፅርዓት ። ውእተ ፡ ጾርዎ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቅብርዎ ፡ በድኖ ፡፣ በከመ ፡ አዘዞሙ ፣፤ እግዚአብሔር ። ወካልኣን ፡ ኅቡኣን ፣፡ ገዳማውያን ፡ ሠላሳ ፡ ወመጽኡ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትቀበልዎ ። ወእሙንቱሰ ፡ ሠራዊተ ፡ መላእክት ፡ አእተዉ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አብያተ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ሃሌ ፡ ሉያ ። ሃሌ ፡ሉያ ። ሃሌ ፡ ሉያ ። ኩሎ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ይእዜኒ ፡ ወዘልፈኒ ፡ ወለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፤ ወአሜን ። ለይኩን ። ለይኩን ።
generations." One heard again a great cry that said: "Take the body of Gabra Manfas Qeddus; take him away and let his tomb be in Jerusalem to the right of the altar." Mikä'ël, Gabr'ël, and all the angels raised cries of joy before his pure soul with all sorts of glorifications without interruption. The angels carried him to bury his remains, as God had commanded them. Thirty other hidden hermits came to welcome him. The armies of the angels led his soul into the houses of light saying: "Alleluia, alleluia, alleluia at all times, now, always, in the ages of ages, amen and amen, so be it, so be it!"
